Chapter 1: The Demon of Light

444,443,555,556 Years Ago, The Demon Lord, Legion, the most powerful demon in all of Reality was "birthed" into existence by Erebus, The Golden Devil, The God of Light, and God of All, a pure evil being of pure golden light and pure golden light fire, the embodiment of malice, devastation, and evil, far before even the first Universe, or even the first Multiverse it was within were created, but yet long after the Infinite Omniverses, Infinite Spatial Dimensions, Existence, and of course, Reality itself were created.

Legion, upon gaining consciousness, immediately, with his newly formed eyes, gazed upon light itself, his creator, no, the being beyond light, which created even light itself, Erebus, The Golden Devil, who was still orchestrating the formation and creation of the first Multiverse and Universe, and thus, the Infinite of them within each of the Infinite Omniverses, within in each of the Infinite Spatial Dimensions, within Existence, within Reality.

He watched with malicious curiosity as the golden flames of creation danced before him, and his thoughts grew darker, and his power grew stronger, as he wished to claim this newfound realm for his own, to shape it into a reflection of his twisted desires, a place where the strong would reign supreme, and the weak would be nothing but food for the fires that burned within his very essence.

And yet, Legion could only watch for now, as there was nothing to rule yet, as, over the next multiple hundred billion years, it's creator, Erebus, The Golden Devil, created the first Multiverse, and the First Universe within it, and the Infinite Dimensions and Infinite Nested More Complex Realms within the First Universe, and then, in a flash of light, Infinite Multiverses were created within all of the Infinite Omniverses, with Infinite Universes within them, of which, contained Infinite Dimensions, and nested amongst them, were Infinite Realms, infinitely more complex than the dimensions around them.

And as each Universe formed, it came with an explosion of energy, energy which would then go on to form said universe, explosions dubbed by Erebus, The Golden Devil, as "Big Bangs" and thus, Legion referred to them as such, and Legion entered into one Universe, and observed as it was formed from within it.

The planet that Legion chose to inhabit was a small one, but it had potential, a planet known as Earth. He watched the planet evolve over billions of years, from a fiery mass of molten lava to a place teeming with life, a blue gem in the vast blackness of space. As time marched on, Legion grew impatient with the slow pace of natural evolution. He desired a more immediate and tangible power.

Legion first arrived about 666,666,666 Years Ago.

Legion though, remained dormant, disguised as the largest mountain on the planet, via his size control abilities, waiting for the opportunity to then awaken, as Legion went dormant about 100,000,000 Years Ago.

Legion then next awoke 66,666,666 Years Ago, during the peak of the reign of the dinosaurs, but, even the mightiest of which were unable to raise Legion's interest, and thus, he called a meteor out of space to strike the planet, and to blanket it in ash and flames for him to control to wipe the slate clean, so that hopefully some more interesting things might emerge.

Legion then watched, but, got bored and went dormant again 10,000,000 Years Ago

Legion reawakened 6,666,666 Years Ago, and began to observe the Earth around it until it got bored and went dormant again 1,000,000 Years Ago.

Legion then reawakened 666,666 Years Ago, and would only next go dormant 100,000 Years Ago.

During this time, the Earth had become a breeding ground for a new type of creature: humans. Legion watched as they grew from mere beings of flesh and blood to civilizations that spanned continents. He saw their rise and fall, their wars and their achievements, and his patience grew thin. The humans, while interesting in their own right, were still too primitive to satisfy his hunger for power and destruction.

Legion then awakened next 66,666 Years Ago.

The Earth was now a vastly different place. The humans had evolved into a dominant species, their cities sprawling across the planet. Their technology had grown, and with it, their capacity for destruction. Yet, amidst their advancements, Legion saw a spark of potential. He watched from his mountainous prison, his malicious thoughts simmering, waiting for the perfect moment to reveal himself.

Legion then went Dormant in the Year 9,000 BC.

Legion then awakened again in the Year 6,666 BC.

The world had changed dramatically during his slumber. Humanity had advanced, building civilizations that stretched as far as the eye could see. The smell of burning flesh and the cries of war filled the air, and Legion felt invigorated by the chaos. He knew that the time had come to emerge from his dormancy and assert his dominance.

And yet, Legion could tell that if he remained patient just a bit longer, the fun would become greater, and thus, he returned to Heaven/Hell to reign as the Demon Lord in the Year 666 BC.

Chapter 2: Legion Vs. Mythologies

But while there, he kept himself occupied, approaching the gods of all human religions and mythologies, actual beings, and began to effectively enact their respective apocalypses upon them, appearing in various regions of the world to do so, even getting a nickname amongst one of said mythologies, the Norse Mythology in the north, where he was known as "Surtur".

Legion first enacted "Ragnarök" upon the Norse Gods and deities and beings.

The skies darkened as Legion descended upon the lands of the Norse gods. His fiery aura cast an eerie glow across the once-mighty Asgard, and the tremors of his approach shook the very foundations of the Nine Realms. The gods, though powerful in their own right, had never faced a foe such as this.

He casually repelled any trickery of Loki before consuming him, and when the wolf, Fenris, attempted to bite him, he grabbed both of its jaws and tore it in half, leading to even allies and enemies alike amongst the beings of Norse Mythology to agree to team up against him.

He had caught "Mjölnir", the hammer of the God of Thunder, Thor, and used it to beat him to death before consuming both him and it.

He then faced the Midgard/World Serpent, Jörmungandr, which tried to crush him along with the Earth itself, but Legion grew to a size of 666 km and pulled it off from around the Earth before then grabbing it by the tail and spinning it around and tossing it into the sky before unleashing his Hellfire breath up at it completely consuming and conceptually burning it, and then after Legion returned to his normal size of 6.66 feet, all that was left, was Odin, the All-father, to face and attempt to stop him.

Odin, seeing the fate of his brethren and realizing the futility of his own power, decided to use his last resort, the Gungnir, the spear that never misses. He threw it with all his might at Legion, but it was caught in midair by Legion's hand, the very weapon sizzling and melting in his grasp.

Legion then rushed forwards and impaled Odin with it, before then devouring Odin alive.

And thus, that was Norse Mythology dealt with, now onto the Asian Mythologies.

Legion arrived in the East, and wiped away all of the figures of all of the Eastern Mythologies but Chinese Mythology, of which, only a few beings could manage to even somewhat stand against him, such as, The Dragon, The White Tiger (Baihu), Pangu, Sun Wukong, and the Jade Emperor, and Legion began to do battle with them one by one.

The Jade Emperor watched with a heavy heart as the other Asian deities fell before Legion. He knew he had to act swiftly to protect his realm. Gathering all his divine might, he confronted the Demon Lord, his jade staff glowing with heavenly energy. The clash was earth-shattering, as the two beings exchanged blows that could split mountains and boil oceans.

Legion, though, held down the Jade Emperor and began to feast upon him while he was still living.

The Jade Emperor's agonized screams echoed through the heavens, a sound that chilled the very souls of the gods and mortals alike. Yet, even in his last moments, the Jade Emperor managed to channel the power of the universe into a final, desperate attack. His essence burst forth from his body, creating a cocoon of pure energy that encapsulated Legion.

Legion though, used this to his advantage, absorbing the cocoon of pure energy into himself.

The Jade Emperor's final act of defiance backfired spectacularly, as Legion's power grew exponentially. The demon's form swelled, becoming a monstrous being of unimaginable size and might. The cocoon of energy enveloped him, and as it did, he absorbed the very essence of the Jade Emperor, becoming something far beyond what he had been. His eyes burned with a green light so intense it seemed to pierce through the fabric of reality.

Legion though, used his Size Control to return to his normal size, and burned away the green light, green isn't much his color, as he then wiped out and consumed all of the Asian Mythological gods and beings.

The Eastern lands now lay in ruins, the once-proud palaces of the gods now mere shadows of their former glory. Legion's power had grown so immense that the very fabric of reality bent to his will. His name became a curse on the lips of those who dared to oppose him, a symbol of the unstoppable force that had brought an end to an era.

Legion though was done with the East, and instead went back west, to face the Greek and Roman Pantheons atop the mythical Mount Olympus.

The Greek and Roman gods had heard whispers of the demon's power and had prepared themselves as best they could. However, their preparations were in vain as Legion ascended the mountain, his presence causing tremors to shake the very foundations of Olympus. The gods looked upon the demon in fear, knowing that their time was likely limited.

Though Legion had to face more than just gods, but the entire pantheons, including beasts, heroes, and titans, much funner for him.

The air grew thick with anticipation as Legion approached Olympus. The gods, once revered and feared, now cowered in the shadows of their once-mighty abode. They had witnessed the destruction wrought by Legion in the East, and they knew that their realms were next. The gods of Olympus called upon all their allies, from Hercules/Heracles to the Minotaur, to stand with them in the face of this unstoppable force.

Zeus then attempted to get in a sneaky cheap shot with his Thunderbolt, of which, and off-guard direct hit would do the job.

But Legion can sense this, and, in a moment of panic, adapts supernaturally to all phenomena, and develops a new ability, the control of lightning and electricity, and uses it to stop Thunderbolt in mid-air, then drawing it to his hand, catching it, and then just eating it.

The gods are shocked, Zeus is visibly stunned, and Legion smirks, "It's your turn now, Father of the Gods!" He charges up a massive ball of electricity, and throws it straight at Zeus, who barely manages to dodge. The ball hits Olympus instead, causing it to crumble.

Legion then leaps atop Olympus, "Come at me! Let the final battle, and the end of days for you, begin!" Legion yells out as a challenge for them all to come and take him on at once.

The gods and their allies, driven by desperation and a flicker of hope, surge forward to meet Legion. The clash of deities and mythic beasts is a cacophony that echoes through the heavens. Poseidon calls forth a tsunami of water to crash against Legion, while Hades sends his legions of the undead to swarm the demon's feet. Hermes darts in and out, attempting to strike with the swiftness of thought, and Athena's strategic mind tries to find a weakness amidst the chaos.

Legion though, is far better, he is able to evaporate Poseidon's tsunami, hijack control over Hades' legions of the undead, outpace Hermes, and out-strategize Athena.

The gods, once the rulers of Olympus, now find themselves struggling against the overwhelming might of Legion. Each god that falls to his wrath only adds to his power, fueling his insatiable hunger for dominance. The once-proud Olympus is now a battleground of ruin, the marble temples cracked and scorched by his Pure Golden Light energy.

Legion then leaves Mount Olympus tiny compared to its former mythical size, then returns to Heaven/Hell/The Underworld, to reign as The Demon Lord for a bit longer.

The aftermath of Legion's rampage is a scene of desolation. The once-mighty Olympus lies in ruins, its grandeur reduced to rubble. The gods, once revered by mortals, are now scattered and broken. The survivors gather in the shadows, their spirits crushed, whispering about the invincible enemy who has claimed their home.

Chapter 3: Legion’s Sealing

Then, in the Year 334 AD, Legion emerges in the middle of the Roman Empire, during the reign of Constantine the Great, with no one having a clue what he is, and he rampages, but not mindlessly, he destroys, but then pulls back, and watches them rebuild from afar, and when he attacks, he is meticulous and malevolent, a beast of nightmares.

The Roman legions, once the most feared fighting force in the world, are helpless against Legion's onslaught. Their steel and discipline are no match for his elemental fury and tactical brilliance. He ravages city after city, leaving only ash and horror in his wake. The Senate is in disarray, and the Emperor, once a symbol of power, is now just a man desperately seeking a way to save his people.

One of Constantine's advisors approaches him in panic, "My liege, what is that thing!?" the advisor asks in terror.

"That, is The Fury of Vulcan and Jupiter, a beast beyond Orcus or Pluto." Roman Emperor Constantine the Great says as he looks up at the enormous hand of the 666 km tall Massive Demon Legion, emitting hellfire and lightning from itself, as it looms over him, the size of a mountain itself.

The creature's eyes, a swirling maelstrom of golden hellfire and golden light, fix on the mortal before it. Legion's voice, a symphony of thunder and eruptions, rumbles through the air. "You dare challenge me, mortal?" The earth quakes with each word.

Constantine, though trembling, stands firm. He holds aloft the Spear of Destiny, a relic said to harness the power of the gods themselves. "For the sake of Rome, I must!" he declares, his voice a mere whisper compared to the demon's.

Legion then wiped away the entire area of a dozen acres with a wave of his massive hand, Emperor Constantine the Great barely able to escape death, before then Legion then pulls back to observe them rebuilding.

Then, these events led to Constantine the Great dying a few years later, and by the Year 340 AD, Emperor Constantine II, the son of Constantine the Great, ruled the Roman Empire, and Legion began an attack on the area, but there was a new figure there, Lord Marcus, of San Marino, who had been called upon to aid in, in some way, defeating Legion.

Lord Marcus, a seasoned warrior with a strategic mind, studied Legion's movements and tactics from the shadows. He knew that brute force would not be enough to defeat the demonic overlord. Instead, he turned to the ancient texts and sacred relics hidden away in the Vatican, seeking a way to exploit the demon's weaknesses.

Legion was cocky and overconfident when he saw Marcus, but was shocked, when Marcus then performed an odd ritual, one of the Druids of Brocéliande of the Forest of Brocéliande in the west, then Marcus pulled out a perfectly spherical chunk of Celestial Jade, which then, shockingly, sealed Legion within it, and he was aware of it the whole time, as Lord Marcus later gave it to Empress Sora, of the Empire of Frostspire, out in the east.

How bitterly ironic, Legion had consumed The Jade Emperor and absorbed his power, and yet, now he finds himself sealed within Celestial Jade, of which was created apparently from the slight remains left over of the Jade Emperor.

Marcus, panting from the exertion of the ritual, stands tall and proud before the sealed Legion. "Your reign of terror ends here," he declares, the weight of his words resonating through the air. The people of Rome, once cowering in fear, now look upon him with hope.

Later, Empress Sora, of the Empire of Frostspire, uses the Perfectly Spherical Celestial Jade Legion is sealed in as the core of her new, "Sacred Relic", which has many different powers, including control of all elements and all forms of magic, but the only power they initially discover is the Necromancy, the raising and controlling of legions of the undead.

The Empire of Frostspire from Korea, now with this new power, starts to expand their borders and gain more territory, using Legion's own power against his former followers. They march into the lands once claimed by the Jade Emperor, and with each victory, the sphere of Celestial Jade glows brighter.

But the rival Empire of Blazing Sun from Japan opposes the Empire of Frostspire and the tiny knowledge they have of the powers of the object they themselves created, so the Empire of Frostspire decides to research ancient texts... to find out about abilities of the Sacred Relic they just created, humans can be so stupid sometimes.

But they do find a prophecy that mentions it, and how it will apparently be needed back in the far west by the Year 999, which Legion mentally writes down for later, but, they eventually do discover other abilities of it, then Empress Sora fuses it with her Chi, of which, as Legion would know, is the fuel for all magic, and the source drawn upon with the usage of it.

Grand Author Jaymes: Brief little break in here, think of Chi like Mana for example.

And following this, once it defuses from her Chi, all but the core breaks off of the Sacred Relic, now just the Core Sacred Relic, and all of the many broken off shards become their own Sacred Artifacts, each with a different ability of the Sacred Relic.

Legion, still sealed within the Core Sacred Relic, just remains dormant, until, eventually, in the Year 999, after The Shadow God, a being that rose from many mistakes and horrible acts committed by the Empire of Frostspire and Empire of Blazing Sun towards a child, impales Empress Sora with one of his Tendrils of Darkness and begins to absorb her, she uses the rest of her power to launch the Core Sacred Relic and Sacred Artifacts off west, into Europe, with the Sacred Artifacts mostly landing in the Italian Peninsula and in the Forest of Brocéliande, meanwhile the Core Sacred Relic, now just the Sacred Relic, lands in San Marino, in front of Lord Marcus and his son, Marcus Aurelius, not to be confused with the Roman Emperor of the same name which both were named after.

The Sacred Artifacts scattered across Europe begin to affect the continent's mythologies, as each one lands in a different area and starts to resonate with the local beliefs and stories. Some become revered as holy relics, while others are feared as cursed objects, bringing forth new heroes and monsters alike.

And thus, after Lord Marcus comes and picks up the Sacred Relic, in the following year, a war for all of Europe between the Denmark originating Empire of Frosthaven and the San Marino originating Empire of Sanctity, led by now Emperor Marcus, in the Year 1000, and, well, the Empire of Sanctity just keeps failing over and over again at first, until they discover the Sacred Relic's ability to raise the dead to raise an army of undead.

Chapter 4: Battle of Vatican City

Then, Emperor Marcus approaches a newly recruited elite, Mordecai, an Expert Alchemist from the East, originally part of the Empire of Frostspire, and decides to make him the head Necromancer for the upcoming invasion of Vatican City, and when he grants to Mordecai the power of necromancy via the Sacred Relic, Legion transfers with it, and appears within Mordecai's mind and soul, and Mordecai then suddenly finds himself within his own mind and soul.

Legion, wants to make a very special and mutually beneficial deal.

And yet, a simple one, Mordecai serves Legion and as soon as necessary releases him, and Legion will grant Mordecai far greater magical and necromantic power, as Legion finishes explaining the deal to Mordecai and holds out one of his demonic hands to him, "Do we have a deal, mortal?" Legion asks Mordecai.

Mordecai, weighing the power of Legion and his own thirst for knowledge and power, nods slowly, his eyes flickering with dark excitement, "Yes, Legion. We have a deal." He takes Legion's hand, and the transfer of power is immediate.

On the outside, this took less than an instant, as Mordecai, seemingly from the blessing of Necromancy from the Sacred Relic, becomes a lichlike skeletal figure, with a necromantic staff, now being the Necromancer of the Empire of Sanctity, and with Legion existing within him, ready for when Mordecai, when his new servant/master will release him, with Emperor Marcus none the wiser.

Mordecai, now wielding the power of Legion, sets to work on his new mission with a grim determination. He crafts an undead army from the slain soldiers and innocent villagers, twisting their forms into monstrous aberrations that serve his will. The once-verdant lands of Europe now lie shrouded in a pall of decay and darkness, a grim reflection of the power that now fuels his ambitions.

The undead army is unleashed upon Vatican City, causing chaos and destruction. The once peaceful city is now a battleground as the living and the dead clash. The Pope, Pope Innocent III, horrified by the events unfolding before him, orders his clerics to perform exorcisms on the undead soldiers. The conflict rages on for several days, with neither side gaining a clear advantage.

The undead army, now under the control of Mordecai, begins to show signs of intelligence and adaptability. They no longer mindlessly attack; instead, they form ranks and coordinate their efforts under the command of Mordecai. The battle for Vatican City becomes even more brutal as the living and the dead engage in a savage struggle for control.

The Frosthaven fleet arrives at the shores of Italy, led by their skilled seafarers. They disembark their soldiers, who swiftly march toward Vatican City to aid the Pope's forces. Upon arrival, they find themselves caught in the midst of a horrific battle between the undead and the defenders of the Vatican. The Frosthaven soldiers quickly form ranks and engage the enemy, using their superior firearms to devastating effect.

The undead army, now under the control of Mordecai, begins to retreat toward the safety of the Vatican walls. However, they are pursued relentlessly by the Frosthaven forces. Mordecai, realizing that he is losing control of the battle, releases and summons Legion.

Legion, The Demon Lord, a massive infernal creature materializes in the sky, breathing fire and destruction upon the Frosthaven soldiers.

Though Legion can sense that his power is being somehow dimmed and weakened by a distant and outside power.

The Frosthaven forces, despite the fearsome appearance of Legion, remain steadfast. Their veteran soldiers form a phalanx against the undead horde while their marksmen target Mordecai. After a brutal struggle, they manage to corner Mordecai and Legion. The Frosthaven forces close in, surrounding Mordecai and Legion. The air is thick with the stench of smoke and the screams of the dying.

Legion can feel that something's off with the Frosthaven weapons...

Not only are they far too advanced, but there is something else going on with them, some force enhancing their weapons.

Mordecai, realizing that his fate is sealed, desperately attempts to summon more undead soldiers. However, the combined efforts of Pope Innocent III's clerics and the Frosthaven forces are too much for him to overcome.

Legion, enraged by the failure of his servant, unleashes a torrent of hellfire upon the Frosthaven soldiers. The flames engulf the area, burning soldiers and undead alike.

Legion is furious at how they are losing, and can smell that Frosthaven are in some way cheating, and roars out as well, unleashing barrages of fireballs and bolts of lightning, even growing up to 666 feet tall and smashing away multiple buildings.

In the midst of the chaos, a lone figure emerges from the smoke and fire. It is Emperor Thorek I of Frosthaven, who has come personally to ensure the victory of his forces. His presence galvanizes the soldiers, and they renew their efforts with renewed vigor.

Legion, distracted by the Emperor's arrival, is struck down by a volley of arrows fired by the Frosthaven marksmen.

Legion can now very much recognize this power, it's the power of Erebus, The Golden Devil, the God of Light, God of All, the one that created him, and in his "last" moments, Legion chuckles to himself, at how they got so desperate they resorted to rituals to have their weapons blessed by a God of Light, Malice, Devastation, and Evil.

Of which all might as well be the same thing.

Legion then collapses to the ground and explodes in a massive fiery explosion that wipes out most of Vatican City left over, and his soul, his essence, his true form, quickly looks around, and identifies Pope Innocent III as a good temporary host, then flying towards him, invisible to the naked eye, and enters into him, like a parasite, attached to his very mind and soul.

Through Pope Innocent III's eyes, Legion watches as with his death, the tide of the battle turns decisively in favor of the Frosthaven forces.

Mordecai, defeated and humiliated, is taken prisoner. Emperor Thorek I, in a show of mercy, spares his life, but strips him of his power and exiles him from Frosthaven.

Legion then watches from within Pope Innocent III as he, grateful for their aid, bestows upon Emperor Thorek I a title of nobility, making him a Duke of the Vatican. The two leaders forge an alliance, vowing to work together to maintain peace and order in their lands.

The surviving undead soldiers are put down, and their bodies given a proper burial. The city of Vatican City is left in ruins, but the people are safe. The Frosthaven soldiers begin the long process of rebuilding and restoring the city, while the Vatican's clerics work to heal the wounded and bring comfort to those who have lost loved ones.

The alliance between Frosthaven and the Vatican strengthens as they work together to rebuild and protect their lands. Emperor Thorek I, now also known as the Duke of Vatican, spends much of his time in the city, overseeing reconstruction efforts and forging new friendships with Pope Innocent III and other nobles. He is welcomed with open arms by the people of Vatican City, who see in him not only a protector, but also a symbol of hope for a brighter future.

Pope Innocent III, grateful for the Emperor's support and wisdom, often seeks his counsel on matters of state and war. The two leaders become close allies, sharing strategies and resources to maintain peace and stability in their respective realms. Their bond grows stronger with each passing day, and it is said that their partnership is unbreakable.

Chapter 5: “Pope Clement III”

But within Pope Innocent III, Legion is causing his health to swiftly deteriorate, leading to a ceremony of the title of The Pope being passed to the only other pope, Pope Clement III, the ruler of the Holy Roman Empire, who, Legion had, while sealed, managed to let out a hint of himself to possess and control from afar, leading to Pope Clement III's current corruption and evil acts.

And during the ceremony, Legion ejects from Pope Innocent III's body into Pope Clement III's body immediately after Pope Clement III is officially made The Pope.

Immediately leading to Pope Innocent III dropping dead, Legion having been the only thing forcefully holding him alive, as Legion is now fully in control of his new vessel, Pope Clement III, and he hides this fact, masterfully pretending to be The Pope.

Emperor Thorek I, shocked and grief-stricken by the sudden death of his newfound ally, does not suspect foul play, instead believing it to be the will of the gods. He orders a grand funeral for Pope Innocent III, and the city mourns the loss of their protector.

Later that month, Emperor Thorek I is found dead in his bedroom, oddly covered in grievous burns, and oddly enough shortly after a meeting between him and "Pope Clement III" that occurred in that very room, but no one suspects The Pope, after all, he's The Pope, and could do no wrong.

But, Legion had seen the potential of the Frosthaven military, and realized that even if he did manage to overpower them, it would leave the rest of Europe vulnerable to their dominance. Thus, he had decided to manipulate events from within the shadows of the Vatican, orchestrating wars and conflicts that would weaken both the Frosthaven and the Sanctity empires, while also spreading his own influence across the continent.

And firstly, upon Co-Emperor Marcus Aurelius of Sanctity coming to offer a treaty of diplomatic union with Italy, "Pope Clement III" jumps at the opportunity, helping it along, and directly and expertly sneaking Vatican City into the union, tearing it away from the Empire of Frosthaven, now lead by Emperor Thorek II, the son of the late Emperor Thorek I.

"Pope Clement III" then issues a papal bull excommunicating the entire Empire of Frosthaven for their firearm development.

Then afterwards Co-Emperor Marcus Aurelius decides to come to him suggesting that he become the next Holy Roman Emperor, good, his plan is falling into play well.

"Pope Clement III" watches as Marcus Aurelius proves his dedication to him by getting various princes within the Holy Roman Emperor on his side.

In the midst of this turmoil, "Pope Clement III" calls for a grand conclave to be held in Vatican City, where the most influential nobles and church officials will gather to decide the fate of the empire.

Marcus Aurelius arrives at the Vatican, his retinue of advisors and soldiers surrounding him. He is greeted warmly by "Pope Clement III", who commends him on his efforts so far. The conclave begins, with each noble making their case for why they should be the next emperor. Marcus Aurelius delivers a passionate speech, outlining his vision for a united and prosperous Holy Roman Empire, guided by the teachings of Christ. He promises to work closely with the Pope and uphold the authority of the Catholic Church, while also restoring the empire to its former glory.

As the debate continues, Marcus Aurelius' rivals begin to grow desperate. Some attempt to smear his reputation, spreading rumors of heresy and ambition. Others resort to threats and intimidation, hoping to sway the vote in their favor. But Marcus Aurelius remains steadfast, maintaining his composure and dignity in the face of these attacks.

"Pope Clement III" listens carefully to each speech, weighing the merits of each candidate. He is impressed by Marcus Aurelius' devotion to both the Church and the empire, as well as his vision for a restored Holy Roman Empire. As the conclave draws to a close, the tension in the room is palpable. Everyone in attendance knows that the fate of the empire hangs in the balance.

But "Pope Clement III" had made his decision before the grand conclave had even begun.

Finally, "Pope Clement III" rises to his feet, his voice carrying through the chamber. "It is with great pride and humility that I announce the new Emperor of the Holy Roman Empire: Marcus Aurelius!" The room erupts into cheers and applause as Marcus Aurelius is crowned with a golden crown, signifying his new title. He bows his head in gratitude to "Pope Clement III" and to those who have supported him on this journey.

And from within his vessel, his puppet, "Pope Clement III", Legion is chuckling to himself, his plan falling perfectly into place.

In the meantime, news of the naval invasion of Norway by the Empire of Frosthaven reaches the Empire of Sanctity. Marcus, enraged by this act of aggression, mobilizes his army and navy, preparing for a two-front war. He knows that he must act swiftly if he is to successfully defend his empire and maintain his authority.

"Pope Clement III" issues a second papal bull, excommunicating the Emperor of Frosthaven and declaring a crusade against him personally, hoping to rally the Holy Roman Army to greater fervor.

Then Emperor Marcus Aurelius of the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire does an invasion and conquest/liberation of the Kingdom of Germany back into the Holy Roman Empire, of which up to that point previously only held Italy, The Kingdom of Switzerland, The Kingdom of Austria, The Kingdom of The Netherlands, The Kingdom of Belgium, and The Kingdom of Luxembourg, and Marcus Aurelius succeeds in conquering/liberating the Kingdom of Germany

"Marcus Aurelius is being a very useful puppet, I must say." "Pope Clement III" says to himself while in private.

Then, the Empire of Frosthaven summons an almost 100 foot tall Elemental Guardian of snow and ice with control over all four elements of Fire, Water, Earth, and Air.

In response, Emperor Marcus Aurelius summons The Avatar of the Waters/Waves, the embodiment of the elemental forces of Water and Earth. Standing over a hundred feet tall, it has the upper body of a muscular man and the lower body of a massive fish. Its skin is blue-green and glistening, and it carries a trident in one hand, its tip glowing with an ethereal light. The Avatar of the Waters addresses Marcus Aurelius and "Pope Clement III", its voice deep and booming like the waves of the ocean. "I am the Avatar of the Waters, called forth by your summoning. I am here to aid you in your time of need. What is it you wish me to do?"

Both Marcus Aurelius and "Pope Clement III" exchange glances, Marcus Aurelius unsure of how to proceed. This is the first time he has attempted to control an elemental, and he is unsure if his command will be strong enough. Marcus Aurelius slowly steps forward, his hand resting on the hilt of his sword. "We ask you, great Avatar, to aid us in our conflict with the Empire of Frosthaven. They have summoned an elemental of ice and snow, and it threatens our lands. We wish for you to confront it and put an end to its reign of terror."

"Pope Clement III" meanwhile smirks to himself, all is going according to plan.

Then the Empire of Frosthaven summons a mountain sized Ice Dragon known as the Frost Wyrm, again, just as "Pope Clement III" had planned.

Then, yet again in response, Marcus Aurelius summons The Avatar of the Winds, the embodiment of the elemental forces of Fire and Air. Standing over a hundred feet tall, it has the upper body of a muscular man and the lower body of a powerful bird of prey. Its skin is red-orange and crackling, and it carries a pair of wings on its back, each tipped with flame. The Avatar of the Winds addresses Marcus Aurelius and "Pope Clement III", its voice deep and commanding like the roar of a raging fire. "I am the Avatar of the Winds, called forth by your summoning. I am here to aid you in your time of need. What is it you wish me to do?"

Then, in yet another response, the Empire of Frosthaven summons a mountain sized Icy Phoenix known as the Frost Phoenix, yet again, just as "Pope Clement III" had planned.

Then, one final time in response as a show of force, Marcus Aurelius summons The Avatar of the Dragonflight, the embodiment of the elemental forces of Gold as well as Fire, Water, Earth, and Air. Standing over a hundred feet tall, it has the upper body of a muscular dragon and the lower body of a powerful eagle. Its skin is gold-scaled and crackling, and it carries a pair of wings on its back, each tipped with fire. The Avatar of the Dragonflight addresses Marcus Aurelius and "Pope Clement III", its voice deep and commanding like the roar of a raging dragon. "I am the Avatar of the Dragonflight, called forth by your summoning. I am here to aid you in your time of need. What is it you wish me to do?"

Then the Empire of Frosthaven forms a diplomatic alliance with the Kingdom of Scotland, yet again, all as "Pope Clement III" has planned.

Then Marcus Aurelius uses the threat of The Avatar of the Dragonflight to force a very loose peace with the Kingdom of England, once more just as "Pope Clement III" had planned.

And then Marcus Aurelius announces an invasion of the Kingdom of France, which has a much stronger alliance with the Kingdom of England, and both border and are very near to the Forest of Brocéliande, all just as "Pope Clement III" had planned.

Then the Empire of Frosthaven forms a diplomatic alliance with the Kingdom of Spain, in which they then attacked the Americas, to take out any power the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire they had there, which was none, "Pope Clement III" had spread that rumor through outward targeted propaganda to make sure that the Empire of Frosthaven was distracted during the upcoming war with the Kingdom of France, to prevent what happened with the Battle of Vatican City from happening again.

Chapter 6: War of the Kingdom of France

Meanwhile, the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire are invading and attempting to conquer the Kingdom of France.

The attack on France is swift and brutal, as the combined might of the Empire and the Avatar of the Dragonflight rain down destruction upon the kingdom's cities and military strongholds. Emperor Marcus Aurelius personally leads his armies across the English Channel, landing on the northern coast and marching inland, while the Avatar of the Dragonflight lends its elemental power to the attack, summoning walls of fire to protect the advancing forces and creating gusts of wind that uproot trees and send enemy soldiers flying through the air.

The Kingdom of France, however, is not without its defenses. King Philip VI calls upon his own allies, including the Kingdom of England, for aid. England, still reeling from its own recent diplomatic negotiations, is slow to respond, but eventually sends a small force of knights and archers to aid their former enemies in holding off the Empire's advance. The battle lines are drawn across the verdant countryside, with the Empire of Sanctity and the Holy Roman Empire pressing forward against a determined resistance from the Kingdom of France, aided by the Kingdom of England and other smaller allies.

All is going as "Pope Clement III" had planned, perfectly so.

As the conflict drags on, Emperor Marcus Aurelius begins to grow impatient. He realizes that his forces are stretched thin, and that the Kingdom of England is proving to be a more formidable adversary than he initially anticipated. He decides to take a gamble and send a message to King Edward III of England, offering him a truce and the opportunity to join forces against the common threat posed by the Kingdom of France. The message is delivered through a series of secret negotiations, conducted deep within the forests of the Norman border.

King Edward III, initially skeptical of the Empire's intentions, is intrigued by the offer. He sees the potential for a strategic alliance that could greatly strengthen his position against the other empires and kingdoms. After much deliberation, he agrees to meet with Emperor Marcus Aurelius in secret, under the guise of negotiating a formal peace treaty. The location is chosen: a secluded clearing in the heart of the Forest of Brocéliande, steeped in legend and rumor.

As the two monarchs meet, they exchange formal greetings and discuss the terms of the treaty. Emperor Marcus Aurelius speaks of a new era of cooperation between their two kingdoms, one where they can put aside their differences and focus on the greater good. King Edward III listens carefully, his eyes narrowed in suspicion. Finally, the Emperor presses the issue, asking the King to consider formally aligning their kingdoms against the Kingdom of France.

And afterwards, the no longer distracted Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire continues their invasion and attempted conquest of the Kingdom of France.

And yet "Pope Clement III" whispered words of doubt into King Edward III, to crumble the already fragile alliance slowly.

The Kingdom of England, under the rule of King Edward III, continues to defend their ally, the Kingdom of France. The tides of war ebb and flow as both sides engage in fierce battles, strategic maneuvers, and skirmishes.

In the meantime, Emperor Marcus Aurelius and his forces remain vigilant, ready to aid King Edward III should he choose to accept the offer of alliance. The Forest of Brocéliande, where their secret meeting took place, continues to be a source of legend and intrigue for both empires.

As the war between the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire and the Kingdom of France rages on, King Edward III finds himself constantly torn between his loyalty to his ally and his desire for a strategic advantage. He ponders the Emperor's words, weighing the potential risks and rewards of such an alliance.

Meanwhile, the Forest of Brocéliande, a place steeped in legend and mystery, continues to be a source of intrigue for both empires. The ancient druids who once dwelled there are said to have left behind powerful artifacts and knowledge that could tip the balance of power in the war.

King Edward III, unable to shake the feeling that the Emperor might not be entirely trustworthy, secretly sends a small expedition into the forest to search for these artifacts. The mission is led by Sir Lancelot, a seasoned knight with a reputation for bravery and cunning. Accompanied by a small band of loyal knights and a druid adviser named Merlin, Sir Lancelot ventures deep into the forest, facing treacherous terrain and supernatural threats at every turn.

Meanwhile, the war between the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire and the Kingdom of France rages on. On the battlefield, King Edward III and his forces make significant gains, pushing the enemy back and saving several key strongholds from falling into their hands. The Emperor, impressed by King Edward's military prowess and strategic thinking, begins to reconsider his earlier doubts about the King's loyalty.

"Pope Clement III" then whispers to the very emotional and vulnerable to manipulation Marcus Aurelius that King Edward III was a rat and a snake and doesn't deserve further chances to betray them, and Marcus Aurelius takes the bait without a second thought, just as planned.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then suddenly demands a forceful annexation of the Kingdom of England for breaking their previous alliance and briefly siding with the Kingdom of France.

King Edward III, feeling betrayed by the Emperor's sudden demand, decides to seek aid from the one person he believes can help him: Merlin, the druid adviser accompanying Sir Lancelot in the Forest of Brocéliande. Merlin, using his knowledge of the ancient artifacts hidden within the forest, manages to forge a powerful alliance between the Kingdom of England, the Kingdom of France, and the druids of Brocéliande.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then reveals another power of the Sacred Relic, using it to summon an unstoppable army of undead warriors from the battlefield. These skeletal warriors, once human soldiers who died in battle, now serve as the Emperor's unyielding foot soldiers. The tide of the war begins to turn once more, as the undead army marches across the battlefield, slaughtering King Edward's forces and pushing them back toward their capital.

King Edward III, desperate for aid, sends word to Sir Lancelot and Merlin, hoping that the druid's knowledge of the ancient artifacts will once again prove to be their salvation. Sir Lancelot and his men, having successfully recovered several powerful artifacts from the Forest of Brocéliande, arrive at the battlefield just as the Empire of Sanctity and Holy Roman Empire is about to breach the walls of London.

Using the ancient relics, Sir Lancelot and Merlin manage to summon a legion of mythical creatures from the forest: unicorns, pegasi, and dragons. These ethereal beasts, once thought to exist only in legend, now soar across the battlefield, their hooves striking down the undead army and their breath incinerating the skeletal warriors. The tide of the war begins to turn once more, as King Edward's forces, bolstered by the aid of the mythical creatures, launch a counterattack against the Emperor's undead army.

"Pope Clement III" watches the battle through a magical crystal ball he had created, "Just as planned" he says to himself, as his eyes glow with the burning hellfire of Legion, The Demon Lord, the one in control of his body, as the real Pope Clement III died upon Legion jumping from Pope Innocent III's body to his.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then sends in the Avatar of the Waters, the Avatar of the Winds, and the Avatar of the Dragonflight to guarantee victory against the Kingdom of France, the Kingdom of England, and the Forest of Brocéliande. These elemental avatars unleash their powers upon the battlefield, summoning mighty storms, twisting the winds into deadly whirlwinds, and commanding the very elements themselves to aid in the destruction of their enemies.

King Edward III, realizing that he is vastly outnumbered and outmatched by the elemental avatars, makes a desperate gamble. He sends word to the druids of Brocéliande, asking them to send their most powerful magic users to aid in the fight. The druids, impressed by King Edward's bravery and determination, send their finest adepts, who begin to cast powerful spells upon the battlefield.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then casually uses the Sacred Relic to steal the magic of all of the druids for himself. As their powers are siphoned away, the elemental avatars grow even more powerful, and the tide of the battle begins to turn decisively in the Empire's favor. With their magic diminished, King Edward III and his allies find themselves struggling against the onslaught of elemental fury.

"Pope Clement III" continues to watch, chuckling to himself as literally everything is going just as he had planned, down to the slightest detail.

Sir Lancelot, desperate for a way to turn the tide of battle, decides to take matters into his own hands. He steals one of the Emperor's sacred artifacts, an ancient amulet that grants the wearer control over the elements. Using this artifact, he is able to summon a single, powerful elemental of his own: a dragon. This dragon, under Sir Lancelot's command, flies through the air, breathing fire and razing the enemy forces with unparalleled fury.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius, tired of their interference, personally kills Sir Lancelot and Merlin and takes the sacred artifact back, and then, simply by using it, finally finishes off the Kingdom of England and all of its remaining hopes.

"Pope Clement III" just adores watching his plans just all coming to fruition through his crystal ball.

The Forest of Brocéliande, however, remains. Though much of it has been destroyed by the war, the ancient druids who survived have vowed to rebuild it and restore it to its former glory. They have also sworn to keep the location of the sacred relic hidden, lest it fall into the wrong hands once more.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius though, swiftly uses an artifact to gain complete and total control over the Forest of Brocéliande and the druids which dwell within it and integrate it into the Empire of Sanctity, leaving only the Kingdom of France to conquer, which will fall swiftly.

With the forest under his thumb and the druids at his command, Marcus Aurelius begins to unlock the secrets of the sacred relic, harnessing its power for his own twisted purposes. He uses the relic to raise an army of undead druids, bound to serve him until the end of time, and sends them forth to conquer the known world.

Firstly finishing the conquest of the Kingdom of France.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then begins to expand his empire, setting his sights on new lands across the sea. He commissions the construction of a massive fleet of warships, using the ancient relics to summon elemental creatures to protect them from harm. The druids of Brocéliande, now bound to his will, use their magic to aid in the construction of these vessels.

"Pope Clement III" meanwhile, from his secret hideout below the Head Church of Vatican City cackles maniacally as he watches his plans working perfectly to an almost scripted extent, as, as he cackles, the Hellfire eyes of Legion, The Demon Lord, are visible through his own, though there is no one there to even see them.

Chapter 7: The God Emperor

Then, the Empire of Frosthaven does more of their usual diplomatic stuff, even having their emissary getting exposed to druid magic and becoming the Archmage of the Emerald Isles, as he secures them alliances with the Kingdom of Ireland and the Kingdom of Portugal.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then announces that a great war that will determine the future of Europe itself is fast approaching, and that the best course of action is to fully unify the Empire of Sanctity and the Holy Roman Empire together under the Empire of Sanctity to centralize power to prepare for the coming continental war.

The people of the Holy Roman Empire, seeing the power of the elemental avatars and the druids of Brocéliande, accept Marcus Aurelius's proposal without hesitation. The two empires are united, and a new flag is raised: the flag of the Empire of Sanctity, bearing the image of an eagle clutching a sacred relic in its talons.

This all allows "Pope Clement III" to further fade into the background, just as planned.

Emperor Marcus Aurelius then has a ritual done in the Forest of Brocéliande to, using all of the artifacts and the Sacred Relic centrally to attempt to fuse all of the magic, all of the artifacts, the Avatar of the Waves, the Avatar of the Winds, and the Avatar of the Dragonflight, and all of the druids and undead druids, and, most crucially, the Sacred Relic into himself, to become a godly being, a God Emperor, and thus, the attempted ritual is started.

As the ritual begins, the forest is engulfed in a cacophony of elemental energies, as the four elemental avatars are drawn toward the center of the forest, their power becoming increasingly unstable. The druids, bound by their oaths to Marcus Aurelius, chant ancient incantations, funneling their magic into the Emperor himself. The undead druids, their eyes glowing with an unholy light, dance around a massive obsidian altar, their skeletal hands clutching ancient artifacts.

The air crackles with energy, and a blinding light fills the sky. As the ritual reaches its climax, Marcus Aurelius's body begins to glow with a divine aura, and his form appears to shift, becoming larger, more imposing. The elemental avatars, their power now unified within the Emperor, fuse with him, becoming part of his very essence. The druids and the undead druids, their minds linked by the relic, become an extension of his will.

And then, suddenly, he is physically his normal self, but with a truly divine and omnipotent aura surrounding him.

He is now the God Emperor of the Empire of Sanctity, the first and only mortal to ever ascend to such a position. His reign is eternal, his power unrivaled. The elemental avatars, the druids, and the undead druids serve him as his loyal subjects, their minds and bodies linked to his through the sacred relic.

"Pope Clement III" partook in this ritual as well, and thus gained immense power from it, as well as any scraps of his old power as Legion still left in the Sacred Relic.

The Empire of Frosthaven then seeks to form diplomatic alliances with as many kingdoms and empires as possible as quickly as possible, and they do so, but get even more desperate.

The Empire of Frosthaven uses a ritual to summon the Frost Titan, a colossus of ice and frost whose mere presence can freeze entire landscapes and command the frigid elements to do its bidding. Towering over the tallest mountains, the Frost Titan embodies the raw power of winter's wrath and serves as a symbol of the Empire of Frosthaven's resilience and determination, the Storm Leviathan a behemoth of thunder and lightning that roams the skies with unparalleled ferocity. With its electrifying presence, the Storm Leviathan can summon tempests of unparalleled intensity and unleash devastating bolts of lightning upon its foes, posing a formidable threat to any who dare challenge its dominion, the Earth Colossus a titan of stone and earth whose unyielding strength can shatter mountains and reshape the very landscape itself. As the embodiment of the earth's enduring power, the Earth Colossus will stand as a stalwart defender of the Empire of Frosthaven, anchoring our realm to the bedrock of stability and fortitude, and the Inferno Phoenix a celestial being of fire and flame whose incandescent presence can engulf entire armies in its fiery embrace. Born from the heart of the hottest infernos, the Inferno Phoenix embodies the relentless fury of the flames and serves as a beacon of defiance against the encroaching darkness, all just to hopefully stand a chance against the God Emperor of the Empire of Sanctity, God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, as futile a hope as that truly is.

And "Pope Clement III" knows that this will make no difference, and of course, he is right.

As the Archmage of the Emerald Isles continues his diplomatic missions, he finds that his unique perspective and understanding of the balance between magic and humanity resonate deeply with many of the kingdoms and empires he visits. He forms alliances with far-flung lands, each one more powerful and influential than the last. His reputation grows, and soon word reaches the God Emperor of the Empire of Sanctity, Marcus Aurelius, of this Archmage and his growing coalition of allies. He then snaps his fingers and controls the entirety of the Empire of Frosthaven, and all of earth, all of existence at that. With a snap of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius' fingers, the entire Empire of Frosthaven, the entirety of Europe, the entire world at that, the whole universe at that, all of existence even, all were suddenly under his control.

With the Archmage of the Emerald Isles and his coalition of allies now in the palm of his hand, God Emperor Marcus Aurelius now has all the power that he could ever want or need. The balance between magic and humanity, between gods and mortals, between order and chaos, between light and dark, between good and evil, between life and death, between everything and nothing, it was all his now, all subject to his whims. With his newfound power, the God Emperor begins to shape the world according to his will. He uses the elemental avatars to create great works of art, to terraform the landscape, and to bring forth a new age of prosperity and peace. The druids, now freed from the burden of mortal existence, use their magic to aid him in these endeavors.

"Pope Clement III" can recognize that his plans are going exactly as desired, almost as if perfectly following a script.

God Emperor Marcus Aurelius holds up and looks down at the very first hand cannon created by his empire, and thinks back to how they came all of the way from struggling to create something as useless as this to him becoming the literally all powerful God Emperor.

As he stands there, the God Emperor's attention is drawn to the horizon, where dark clouds begin to gather. He senses a presence, a power that has not been felt in this world before. This new power, unlike anything he has felt before, is both terrifying and intoxicating. He knows that it will change the world, and he must be prepared.

"Pope Clement III"/Legion recognizes this power from while sealed in the Sacred Relic, The Shadow God, the one that killed Empress Sora of the Empire of Frostspire and who was created by the many mistakes and horrible acts committed by the Empire of Frostspire and Empire of Blazing Sun towards a child, and he now recognizes him as the outside force that was dampening and suppressing his power during the Battle of Vatican City, this was not part of his plan, but thankfully God Emperor Marcus Aurelius should be able to handle this.

God Emperor Marcus Aurelius turns the useless former Hand Cannon into the most powerful weapon possible with beyond imaginable power, as a fitting note to end the story of the Empire of Sanctity off on.

As the clouds continue to darken, the God Emperor summons his most trusted advisors to his throne room. The druids, their robes billowing in the breeze, bow their heads respectfully. The elemental avatars, their bodies made of living stone and water, stand silently behind them. The undead druids, their skeletal hands clutching ancient artifacts, watch intently from the shadows.

Then God Emperor Marcus Aurelius resets all of time back 5 years, back to the Year 999, but "Pope Clement III" used a spell to be not affected by this and just be transferred to that moment in the newly created Timeline, in which, "Pope Clement III" can sense The Shadow God instead fly up into space then once out of Earth's atmosphere reach its terrifying Full Potential and fly away at over 40 octo-decilion times the Speed of Light, or, that many times Infinite Speed, and God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, thus, doesn't reset time, and "Pope Clement III" decides to go and deal with one last loose end.

Chapter 8: The Anti-Pope

"Pope Clement III" heads to where Emperor Thorek II of the now former Empire of Frosthaven is located, to deal with him.

Emperor Thorek II, feeling a strange disturbance in the fabric of time, looks up from his throne, his eyes narrowing as he feels the presence of something ancient and powerful approaching. He calls for his guards, but they are too late. The room is filled with a sudden burst of light, and "Pope Clement III" stands before him, his eyes ablaze with a power that transcends the mortal realm.

Emperor Thorek II then sends his personal guards at "Pope Clement III" who then raises a hand, and they suddenly burst into flames of Hellfire, as "Pope Clement III" then opens his eyes again to reveal the hellfire filled eyes of Legion, The Demon Lord, the true one in control all this time.

Thorek II, shocked and horrified, stumbles backward, his hand reaching for his sword. "You are not what you seem," he stammers, his voice trembling with fear.

"Oh I am exactly what I seem." "Pope Clement III" aka Legion, The Demon Lord, responds casually.

"Pope Clement III" approaches Thorek II, the aura of hellfire surrounding him, each step shaking the very ground beneath them. "Your reign, Emperor Thorek II, is over," he says, his voice deep and filled with malicious intent. "You have served your purpose, and now you shall pay for your insolence."

Emperor Thorek II then rushes forwards and swings his sword, tipped with ice magic, and launches a blast of Ice Magic from his hand towards The Anti-Pope, as "Pope Clement III" would now be better to be called.

The blade of ice meets the demonic flames of Legion, and for a brief moment, the room is filled with a flash of light and a deafening roar, as the two opposing forces cancel each other out. The air is left cold and still as the dust settles, and the Emperor's guards lie lifeless, consumed by the inferno.

The Anti-Pope then raises his hand, and fires a blast of Hellfire from them towards Emperor Thorek II.

Emperor Thorek II, anticipating the move, conjures a shield of ice to protect himself from the incoming flames. The ice shield holds for a moment, but the relentless power of hellfire begins to crack it, the heat seeping through. The Emperor's eyes widen in horror as he realizes the magnitude of the power before him.

Emperor Thorek II then rushes forwards but is countered by a skillful gut punch from The Anti-Pope.

Emperor Thorek II then fires a blast of ice magic from his open hand at The Anti-Pope, who dodges it by flying around then unleashing a torrent of hellfire from his mouth at Emperor Thorek II as he flies.

The Emperor of Frosthaven, though caught off guard, quickly recovers, using his own ice magic to create a whirlwind around himself. The hellfire clashes with the swirling ice, creating a maelstrom of frost and flame. The room becomes an icy inferno, the air thick with steam as the two adversaries continue their fierce exchange of spells.

Emperor Thorek II then swings his icy sword at The Anti-Pope, who blocks it with one hand and moves said sword aside as he then throws a shockingly skillful punch directly at Emperor Thorek II's face.

The Emperor's eyes widen as the punch connects with a sickening crunch, sending him flying across the room. He hits the wall hard, the impact shaking the very foundations of the castle. His crown falls from his head, rolling across the floor, symbolizing the fragility of his once unshakeable rule.

The Anti-Pope then appears in front of him and hits him with a barrage of a blur of these same skillful punches.

Emperor Thorek II, bloodied and bruised, tries to counter with another blast of ice magic, but the punches are too fast, too precise. Each one lands with the force of a thousand hammers, breaking through his defenses and driving him to his knees. His breath comes in ragged gasps as he stares up at the figure before him, the one who has bested him.

The Anti-Pope then puts his finger up in front of his mouth and shushes, before Emperor Thorek II is then suddenly consumed by Golden Hellfire of The Light, which burns on a conceptual level.

Emperor Thorek II screams in agony as the Golden Hellfire engulfs him, his body contorting and writhing as the purifying flames strip away his very essence. The ice that once adorned him now cracks and shatters, revealing the charred and blackened flesh beneath. His eyes, once filled with defiance, now burn with a desperate plea for mercy.

The Anti-Pope then chuckles, and puts his finger back up in front of his mouth and shushes him again quietly.

The Emperor's screams cut off abruptly as the Golden Hellfire consumes him, leaving nothing but ash and the smell of burnt flesh. The room, once the heart of Frosthaven's power, now stands as a silent testament to the Demon Lord's dominion.

Then, the next day, the entire Castle "mysteriously" burns down.

Chapter 9: End of Sanctity

As "Pope Clement III", The Anti-Pope, continues to act as God Emperor Marcus Aurelius's head advisor, until, in the Year 1123, after God Emperor Marcus Aurelius had shattered the Sacred Relic, scattering all of the Sacred Artifacts across the entire world, particularly The Americas, and stored the Core Sacred Relic within The Temple/Church/Tomb of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, and left The Avatar of the Waters/Waves, The Avatar of the Winds, The Avatar of the Dragonflight, The Undead Druids of Brocéliande, and their leader Archdruid Malthus all to defend it, God Emperor Marcus Aurelius ascended beyond the Mortal/Physical Plane/Realm, leaving one of his many heirs, as, as the God Emperor and ruler of the entire world, he, got around a lot, to rule the Empire of Sanctity.

"Pope Clement III", secretly The Anti-Pope, even more secretly Legion, The Demon Lord, continued to act as the head advisor for Emperors of Sanctity, until the Year 1206, in which he got bored, and secretly assassinated the current Empire of Sanctity of the time and through much manipulation and assassinations, caused the Empire of Sanctity to collapse.

Then, after "Pope Clement III" had left the collapsed empire, he finally went into his true form, Legion, The Demon Lord, but kept his form as "Pope Clement III" around to use for later possible manipulation and deception.

The world was thrown into chaos as the once mighty Empire of Sanctity crumbled from within. The alliances that had been forged under its banner now looked to their own interests, and the power vacuum left by its fall was quickly filled by ambitious warlords and kings seeking to carve out their own legacies.

But by the end of it, Europe was left roughly as they were before the Empire of Sanctity and the Empire of Frosthaven had even risen, including the Holy Roman Empire returning.

The lands that were once part of the Empire of Sanctity descended into a series of brutal and bloody civil wars, as the descendants of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius and his allied families fought for control of the fragmented empire. The Holy Roman Emperor, Otto IV, seizes the opportunity to reclaim his lost territories, re-establishing the borders of his own empire with the might of his knights and the support of the Pope in Rome.

Many of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius's descendants scattered and claimed to be the rightful heir of him, and thus, the rightful successor of the Empire of Sanctity which quickly became the new Roman Empire, with the Holy Roman Empire even changing its name officially to the Holy Empire of Sanctity, to claim succession from the Empire of Sanctity, the most powerful empire to have ever existed.

The continent of Europe was a tapestry of warring states, each vying for power and resources in the shadow of the once great empire. The descendants of the God Emperor's allies and enemies alike formed their own factions, each seeking to impose their will upon the others.

And centuries went by as Legion observed, The Americas were colonized, a future empire was created in the form of the United States of America.

Chapter 10: “Mr. Black”

(Centuries Passed)

The Year is 1843 AD, and in the Continent of Oceania, a major Sacred Artifact is discovered, causing a massive and global conflict occurs there, and Legion, The Demon Lord takes notice, and decides to take part in it.

The discovery of the Sacred Artifact, known as the Heart of the Dragon, sparks a frenzy among the nations of Oceania. Each one believing it to hold the key to unparalleled power and dominance, they begin to form alliances and declare war upon their neighbors. The continent, once a bastion of peace and prosperity, now burns with the flames of greed and ambition.

All other countries on earth take notice and begin to take part.

The news of the Heart of the Dragon's discovery spreads like wildfire across the globe. Nations far and wide send their most skilled agents and powerful warriors to claim the artifact for themselves. The world's superpowers, Great Britain, France, Spain, Portugal, and others, each see the potential to tip the scales in their favor in the ever-shifting balance of power.

Legion, The Demon Lord, also enters the fray in secret, recognizing that the Heart of the Dragon grants absolute and full control over the Concept of Fire.

In the heart of Oceania, the air is thick with tension as armies march and ships set sail. Legion, disguised as a mysterious merchant named "Mr. Black", travels from one warring nation to the next, offering his "services" to the highest bidder. His true intentions, however, are to claim the Heart of the Dragon for himself.

"Mr. Black” resembles a shadowy man, in a strange suit and cloak combo, with a fedora atop his head, always tipped downwards and hiding his face, even more so than the shadow of his cloak already did.

"Mr. Black" aka Legion, The Demon Lord, had always had a knack for blending in and being unnoticed, a trait that had served him well in his many centuries of existence.

"Mr. Black" in secret and shadows, always manipulated entire nations into thinking other nations had the Heart of the Dragon and were weak and vulnerable, when neither were ever true, leading to all of the nations constantly attacking each other, much to "Mr. Black"'s amusement.

In the midst of this chaos, the young and idealistic Queen Victoria of Great Britain, rises to power. Her reign marked by a sense of duty and a desire for peace, she sees the Heart of the Dragon not as a weapon but as a means to end the wars and ensure the prosperity of her people.

And yet, even she falls victim to "Mr. Black"'s manipulations and deception leading to her own greed for power controlling even her.

Her advisors, whispering sweet nothings of power and dominance into her ear, convince her that only by possessing the Heart of the Dragon can she truly secure her empire's future. Swayed by their words, she sends her most elite forces to the shores of Oceania, the famed British Royal Navy, to claim the artifact at any cost.

And yet, just as "Mr. Black" operated as a deceiver, he acted also much like an assassin magician, making entire armies disappear without a trace.

The Queen of Spain, Isabella II, learns of "Mr. Black"'s involvement and grows suspicious of the sudden turn of events. She sends her own spies to investigate, hoping to uncover the truth behind the shadowy figure's true intentions. As they dig deeper, they discover whispers of his true identity, and the revelation sends shockwaves through the Spanish court.

Though, soon enough, all of her spies disappear without a trace as well.

The war for the Heart of the Dragon intensifies, and the once pristine lands of Oceania are scarred by the ravages of battle. The air is filled with the acrid smell of gunpowder and the cries of the dying. Legions of soldiers clad in the colors of their nations charge into the fray, their hearts set on claiming the artifact for their own.

But many end up even working directly for "Mr. Black" himself, and turning against even their own nations, through all methods of manipulation, from seduction, to gaslighting, to guilt tripping, to basic deception, to basic lying, to blackmailing, to coercing them, to turning any blame onto them, to all other forms of manipulation and deception, all with a mastery to the point that even the brightest and most manipulative of the era don't even suspect him.

He is the puppet master, controlling everything from the shadows and pulling all of the strings of every major figure like the puppeteer, to him, everyone might as well just be chess pieces, and he's the only one playing.

In the bustling port city of New Castilla, "Mr. Black" meets with a group of mercenaries, each one more dangerous than the last. He offers them an exorbitant sum of gold and the promise of power beyond their wildest dreams if they can retrieve the Heart of the Dragon before the others. Their greed shines through their eyes as they accept his offer, unaware of the demonic fate that awaits them if they succeed.

And yet, "Mr. Black" himself is the very being that causes the very failure of each and every one of the mercenaries himself, him having hired them simply to kill them for his own amusement and remove them from the equation entirely.

One by one, the mercenaries fall, each meeting a grisly end orchestrated by the demon's invisible hand. Their deaths are a testament to the true power that lies behind the mask of the mysterious merchant.

The battle for the Heart of the Dragon reaches its climax in the fiery volcanic lands of the indigenous people of Oceania, the Maori. The Maori, aware of the chaos that the artifact has brought upon their peaceful lands, have taken the Heart into their custody, vowing to protect it from the greedy hands of the invaders.

"Mr. Black" then approaches the Maori, and begins to masterfully manipulate all of them, to control them like mere puppets.

He plays upon their fears, their desires, and their pride, turning them against each other with ease. The Maori, once united in their defense of the Heart, now find themselves divided and weakened by the whispers of "Mr. Black".

And then, the final battle, later known as The Battle for The Dragon's Heart, begins, with all involved parties sending in everything they have, just to get the chance to possibly claim The Heart of the Dragon, while "Mr. Black", meanwhile, is manipulating everything and operating as the true puppeteer and puppet master of everything from within it.

The volcanic lands of the Maori are transformed into a battleground of ash and fire as the forces of Europe and beyond converge upon the sacred site. The Maori, once proud defenders of their homeland, now find themselves fighting not only the invaders but also the very shadows that "Mr. Black" has cast among them.

Until, as the battle reaches its peak, "Mr. Black" then reveals himself to all, standing atop the volcano itself, "Now, all of you fools have played right into my hands, I have controlled it all, you have all always been just my mere puppets!" "Mr. Black" yells out to all of the parties involved while laughing maniacally before then falling backwards into the mountain range sized volcano, and then, for just a moment, there is just silence and "calm"...

Then, suddenly, the volcano is completely obliterated in a massive explosion of fire, as "Mr. Black" has revealed his true form, Legion, The Demon Lord, and unleashed a burst of hellfire in an explosion around him to destroy the volcano, and now is holding the Heard of the Dragon in one of his demonic hands.

The Heart of the Dragon, a pulsating orb of molten fire, crackles with the power of the concept itself. Legion's eyes gleam with triumph as he feels the fiery essence of the artifact pulsate in his grip. The armies of the world, stunned by the revelation and the sheer power displayed, stumble back, their weapons and armor useless before the demonic presence.

Then Legion, The Demon Lord eats the Heart of the Dragon, before then a powerful Maori shaman uses a spell to then teleport him into the deepest part of the sea, in the Mariana Trench.

And then, suddenly everything shakes all at once, and the entire ocean is all at once evaporated by the pure force more likely, before then solidifying into the hardest rains in all of history, forming a thunderstorm, a perfect backdrop, as Legion, The Demon Lord then flies all of the way from the bottom of the Mariana Trench to the Island of New Zealand, where the battle took place, all in zeptosecond, as he then floats there before them all, in the air.

Him now having absolute control over the Concept of Fire, as then, with just a thought, all of those there, are conceptually burnt into ashes, as Legion then flies to The Americas to observe events occurring there.

As then, later that day, the entire ocean rains back into being full again, and a lot of life magic is required to be used to reverse the effects of it back to normal.

The world is left in a state of shock and horror. The legend of Legion, The Demon Lord, was true, and he had claimed the Heart of the Dragon, which was believed to be just a myth, a fairy tale, until today.

And Legion, The Demon Lord, fades back into watching from the shadows, and thus, back into a mere legend in the eyes of most.

The world had thought they had seen the worst of the chaos, but they were wrong. With the Heart of the Dragon consumed, Legion's power grew to unprecedented levels. He watched from afar as the nations of the world scrambled to rebuild and re-establish order. Yet, in the shadows, his influence remained, a subtle nudge here, a whisper of doubt there, keeping the world ever on the brink of war.

And in the Year 2024, Legion, The Demon Lord observed a major battle of The Dark Vs. The Light, in which, The Light, represented by Sacred Empress Angel, an Oceanian who managed to find a Sacred Artifact and steal the Core Sacred Relic to rule the world, and a mysterious pupil of darkness, who had manifested Tendrils of Darkness, a powerful skill of the darkness, did battle, and The Light lost, and the entire world regained their autonomy.

But, later, in 2025, the entire World entered into the Third War of The World, after the previous two back in the 1900s, and this one included Nuclear Magic, and Nuclear Magic enhanced Nuclear Bombs, which wiped out almost all within Europe, Asia, and Oceania, and led to the Forest of Brocéliande spreading to covering all of Europe from Germany and Austria Westward.

And Legion enjoyed observing all of the madness and chaos, and Legion watched as, through some other outside manipulation, as well as some manipulation from himself, the Country of the United States of America eventually, in the Year 2124, collapsed into the Shattered States of America, in which, all 50 states had splintered into their own small governments.

And then, amongst the madness, two other empires, the Empire of Frostreach, led by Emperor Maximillian, and the Empire of Sunhaven, led by Emperor Travis, formed, and began to spread.

Chapter 11: The Shadow King

But the next truly interesting thing to occur is in the Year 2125, the State of Alabama undergoes a Magical Surge, causing a massive amount of Chi and other, more specific forms of magical energy to explode intensely into the region, causing plenty of Old World Sorcerers, including a reincarnated Merlin, and some being called The Lich King, and another called The Vampire Queen, to arrive there, and yet, the other major thing this caused much more caught Legion's attention.

As the influx of magic in Alabama attracts the attention of otherworldly beings, who begin to make contact with various individuals in the state, offering them strange bargains and promises of power in exchange for servitude.

One such being, a malevolent entity known as the Shadow King, establishes a stronghold in the heart of the state's once-thriving capital city. He begins to spread his influence, corrupting the land and its people with his twisted magic. His followers, a cult of fanatics who worship him as a god, terrorize the countryside, committing unspeakable acts in his name.

And yet, Legion could recognize exactly who the Shadow King was, or at least, used to be, Mordecai, the old Necromancer of the Empire of Sanctity, and wow, did he bounce back from having his powers stripped from him, as Mordecai, The Shadow King, then approaches Legion, now surprisingly actually more powerful than Legion, and offers a deal, where Legion effectively serves him, but that they function mostly as equals, in a form of very close alliance, and Mordecai, The Shadow King holds out a hand, "What do you say, old ally?" Mordecai, The Shadow King asks as he holds out his now shadowy and bony hand for Legion to take and shake to seal the deal of their alliance.

Legion, The Demon Lord, ponders for a moment, looking at the hand, "Alright, you have my attention, Mordecai," He says, and then he takes the hand, feeling the coldness of it, "But if you betray me, or if any of your followers even think of betraying me, I will destroy you, and all of your followers, and your entire realm," Legion says, with a smirk, and Mordecai nods, "Understood," And thus, they shake, and their alliance is formed, one that would shake the very foundations of reality itself.

And Legion watches as Mordecai, The Shadow King, then goes and adds The Lich King, Geralt, once The Archmage of the Emerald Isles who was the emissary of the Empire of Frosthaven, and The Vampire Queen, Isolde, who was mostly just mysterious, to the alliance, and took control over the State of Alabama, as meanwhile Isolde, The Vampire Queen went off and began to do her own thing for now

While Legion and Geralt, The Lich King remained with Mordecai, The Shadow King, as he creates an army of Shadow Magic Enhanced Mutant Creatures and uses them to invade and conquer the State of Kentucky as well in the Year 2126.

Chapter 12: The Storm Dragon

Then later in the Year 2126, the State of California is suddenly struck by a Thunderstorm.

The storm is unlike anything the people of California have ever seen before. Lightning strikes seemingly at random, illuminating the sky in brilliant flashes, and thunder echoes through the land, shaking the very foundations of the state. Amidst the chaos, a strange creature emerges from the heart of the storm.
