It is a towering, winged beast, part eagle and part dragon, its scales shimmering like polished steel in the lightning's glare. The people of California, having never before encountered such a creature, name it the Storm Dragon.

As the storm continues to rage, the Storm Dragon circles high above the state, its massive wings creating powerful gusts of wind that uproot trees and topple buildings. The people of California are terrified, not knowing what to make of this new threat. Some believe it to be a harbinger of doom, while others see it as a divine messenger, sent to punish them for their sins.

The people of the State of Kentucky, now thoroughly subjugated, are forced to build massive temples and shrines in honor of Mordecai, The Shadow King, spreading his twisted doctrine of darkness and despair.

Modrecai, The Shadow King, pleased with the progress of his invasion, begins to focus his attention on the southern states. He sends out a proclamation, promising freedom and prosperity to those who will willingly submit to his rule, while those who resist will face utter destruction. Many are drawn in by the false promises, while others see the writing on the wall and choose to flee westward, hoping to find sanctuary in the untouched territories of Arizona or New Mexico.

Then the Empire of Sunhaven combines their forces with the Californian Adventurers as well as the Empire of Sunhaven's own Elite Shadow Mages, and Mordecai, The Shadow King, sensing the threat posed by the combined forces, sends his most powerful servants and minions to aid the Storm Dragon in its last stand, and decides to head there himself, along with Legion, and he also called in Geralt, The Lich King and Isolde, The Vampire Queen to aid them.

The final battle is epic in scale, as the forces of good and evil clash high atop the mountain. The air is thick with the cacophony of battle cries, spells, and the beating of wings. The Storm Dragon, its strength sapped by the elite shadow mages' necromantic magic, struggles valiantly against the onslaught, but it is clear that the tide has turned.

But then Mordecai's servants and allies arrive to aid the Storm Dragon, apparently named Donderdraak.

The battle becomes even more desperate, as the forces of evil begin to gain the upper hand. The elite shadow mages, exhausted from their previous efforts, are forced to fall back, and the reinforcements from the Empire of Sunhaven find themselves caught in the crossfire. Donderdraak, emboldened by his newfound allies, launches a fierce counterattack, driving the adventurers and the California army back.

The emperor, realizing the dire situation, orders his forces to fall in around the adventurers and the California army, forming a protective barrier. The soldiers and mages of the empire fight valiantly, holding the line against Donderdraak and his allies. Mordecai, The Shadow King, sensing the opportunity, prepares to make a decisive strike, but before he can act, a lone figure appears from the ranks of the adventurers.

It is a legendary figure, clad in ancient armor and wielding a sword forged in the fires of legend. The figure, known only as the Last Champion, begins to fight its way through the ranks of Donderdraak's forces. Mordecai, The Shadow King, recognizing the threat posed by this newcomer, sends his strongest minions to stop him.

The Last Champion battles valiantly, single-handedly turning the tide of the battle. Donderdraak, realizing that he is losing ground, decides to make a desperate gamble. He flies high into the air, leaving the battlefield momentarily, and begins to gather energy from the ley lines that course through the mountain.

Mordecai, The Shadow King, seeing this, smirks. He knows that if Donderdraak can harness enough power, he will be able to unleash a devastating spell that could obliterate everything within miles. Mordecai orders his minions to focus their efforts on stopping the Last Champion, while he himself begins to prepare for the impending attack.

The Last Champion, unaware of Donderdraak's plan, continues to fight his way through the ranks of Mordecai's minions. His sword flashes like lightning, his armor shining like polished steel in the dim light of the battle. As he nears Donderdraak, he can feel his presence, a malevolent energy that chills him to the bone.

And upon now fully feeling The Last Champion's presence, Legion briefly freezes up, as memories of his sealing, an event that recently the memory of has begun to haunt him, flashes through his mind, as he recognizes exactly who The Last Champion is, or, at least, once was, Lord Marcus of San Marino/Emperor Marcus of the Empire of Sanctity, the man that originally sealed Legion,  and Legion has a silent panic attack.

Mordecai, The Shadow King, seeing the Last Champion's progress, becomes increasingly desperate. He sends his most powerful allies, including Geralt, The Lich King, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Legion, The Demon Lord, to stop the Champion.

This brings Legion back to his senses and makes him then analyze the situation and LOCK IN.

But the Last Champion is like a whirlwind, cutting through their ranks with ease. Geralt, The Lich King, unable to match the Champion's speed, unleashes a wave of undeath, summoning a horde of skeletons and zombies to attack.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen, her fangs bared, swoops in, attempting to drain the Champion's strength with a bite, moving at blinding speeds.

The Demon Lord, his demonic aura choking the air, launches a barrage of fireballs and lightning bolts at the Champion, who somehow dodges them perfectly, impossible, lightning moves at 70% of the Speed of Light, aka, 70% of countably infinite speed!

Meanwhile, Donderdraak has gathered enough energy and begins to focus it into a massive ball of energy. Mordecai, The Shadow King, sensing the impending attack, orders his minions to fall back, forming a defensive line around the Emperor and his forces. The Last Champion, still unaware of Donderdraak's plan, continues his relentless advance. As he reaches Donderdraak, Mordecai, The Shadow King smirks, confident that Donderdraak's spell will obliterate everything in its path.

Legion very briefly wonders if Mordecai is actually insane for not calling for a retreat, considering that blast will completely obliterate everything for a hundred miles in all directions on a molecular level, and could even wipe out the entire EARTH on a molecular level as well.

Donderdraak lets out a deafening roar and unleashes the energy ball towards the center of the battlefield. The Last Champion, realizing the danger too late, tries to deflect the attack with his sword. The ball of energy collides with his sword, sending a blinding flash of light and heat through the air. When the light fades, the Last Champion lies motionless on the ground, his armor dented and his sword shattered.

Legion pats over his whole body, relieved he is still in one piece and not having his physical form completely obliterated, The Last Champion's sword and body must have absorbed most of the blasts energy, causing only the top of the mountain to get completely obliterated on an atomic level.

Mordecai, The Shadow King, seeing his chance, sends out a wave of darkness that engulfs the fallen champion. The darkness begins to flow into the champion's body, healing his wounds and restoring his strength. As the champion rises to his feet, he feels a newfound connection to the darkness, his body now tingling with power. He turns to face the Shadow King, his eyes glowing with a sinister light.

Geralt, The Lich King, seeing the champion's transformation, realizes that he has been outmatched. And in an admittedly very wise move, he flees the battlefield, seeking refuge in his tomb deep within the mountains. Isolde, The Vampire Queen, meanwhile, much less wise, enraged by the loss of her servant, swoops down, fangs bared, ready to claim the champion as her own. But the champion, now under the influence of Mordecai, The Shadow King, repels her, driving her away with a wave of darkness.

The wave of darkness even completely and atomically obliterates her body, and yet, she almost instantly regenerates back from literally nothing.

Legion, seeing the power the champion now wields, is both intrigued and afraid. He decides to wait and observe, biding his time until the champion's allegiance is clear, and also begins to, through various puppets he had set up in the case of an emergency, begins to observe the goings on around the SSA, particularly involving the two empires for an opportunity to retreat. Mordecai, The Shadow King, pleased with the champion, whose name is apparently Lothar, and his newfound loyalty, grants him the title of Dark Lord, entrusting him with command over the remaining minions.

Donderdraak, exhausted from his exertion, hovers above the battlefield, watching the events unfold. He senses the shift in power and influence, and decides to remain neutral for now, content to bide his time until the balance of power once again tips in his favor.

The newly crowned Dark Lord, the Champion now known as The Dark Lord, Lothar, surveys his domain with a satisfied smile. He can feel the weight of Mordecai, The Shadow King's trust upon his shoulders, and he intends to use that power to shape the world according to his will. His first order of business is to consolidate his rule, eliminating any potential threats and rivals.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen, enraged by her failure to control the Champion, retreats to her domain, plotting her revenge. Legion, cautious but intrigued by the Champion's newfound abilities, remains on the sidelines for now, biding his time until the perfect opportunity arises, and begins heavily checking his puppets for possibly opportunities in relation to the two empires, considering that the alliance with Mordecai, The Shadow King is quickly crumbling, and he is feeling an extent of genuine fear right now. Donderdraak, ever watchful, continues to monitor the shifting alliances and power struggles, preparing for the day when he will once again play a more active role in the fate of the world.

As The Dark Lord, Lothar begins his reign, he immediately sets about strengthening his position. He forms new alliances with other powerful entities, such as the Lich Queen and the Necromancer King, who see in him a potential rival to The Shadow King, Mordecai's former dominance. He also seeks out ancient artifacts and lost knowledge, hoping to further increase his already formidable power.

Chapter 13: The Dark Emperor

The Empire of Sunhaven, following that incident in relation to the Shadow King, decides to attempt to further research Arcane Magic in an attempt to gain more answers about the past, and about the Empire of Frosthaven and the Empire of Sanctity.

Legion, seeing this as an opportunity, decides to aid the Empire of Sunhaven in their research, in exchange for information about the other empires, and possibly a more advantageous alliance as well as beginning to manipulate them from within. He sends one of his most cunning and powerful demons, known as the Grand Inquisitor, to act as an advisor to the Emperor and his court.

Meanwhile, The Shadow King, Mordecai continues to consolidate his power, forming an uneasy alliance with Donderdraak, who sees the potential for chaos and opportunity in this new balance of power. The two beings strike a deal: Donderdraak will aid Mordecai in his endeavors, in exchange for the freedom to wreak havoc upon the world as he sees fit.

Mordecai, growing weary of Donderdraak's unpredictability, forges a new alliance with The Shadow Queen, Lilith, who holds the key to unlocking ancient necromantic rituals from the Shadow Realm that could potentially grant immortality to Mordecai and solidify his reign forever.

The Empire of Sunhaven, aided by Legion's Grand Inquisitor, successfully uncovers ancient knowledge about the Shadow King's true origins, revealing him to be a fallen hero from a long-forgotten age, cursed to walk the earth as a shadow of his former self, they even discover him to have formerly been the Necromancer of the Empire of Sanctity who had his powers stripped from him by Emperor Thorek I of Frosthaven after the Great Battle of Vatican City.

The "New Power", first sensed by the God Emperor Marcus Aurelius in the Year 1000, suddenly begins to appear in the State of Alabama, currently controlled by Mordecai, The Shadow King.

Legion, intrigued by the Empire of Sunhaven's discovery, decides to reveal himself to the Emperor, Emperor Travis, offering an alliance against The Shadow King, Mordecai in exchange for information about The Shadow Queen, Lilith and her domain. Emperor Travis, cautious but aware of The Shadow King's growing power, agrees to the alliance.

Meanwhile, the "New Power", first sensed by the God Emperor Marcus Aurelius in the Year 1000, and once known as The Shadow God, suddenly begins to appear in the State of Alabama, which is currently controlled by The Shadow King, Mordecai.

The people of Alabama, already exhausted by the eternal struggle against The Shadow King, are at first confused and disoriented by the presence of this new force. Some believe it to be a sign of divine intervention, sent by the gods to liberate them from their dark oppressor. Others, more skeptical, view it as another trick by The Shadow King, a ruse to lure them into a false sense of security.

But suddenly, the "New Power" begins to form into a figure, one of darkness, currently hidden by shadows slowly fading to reveal it, a being beyond even The Shadow King, one comparable in power to even the God Emperor Marcus Aurelius himself, who had ascended from this realm over a thousand years ago.

The figure steps forward, its form obscured by the dark tendrils that seem to writhe around it, almost as if it were a living manifestation of the shadows themselves. It speaks, its voice cold and commanding, and its words send shivers down the spines of all who hear it.

"People of Alabama," it says, "I am he whom you have been awaiting. I am he who will lead you to victory against the Shadow King. But know this: I do not come to play the role of a savior. I am not here to liberate you. I am here because it suits my purposes. I am the Dark Emperor, and I claim this realm as my own."

The Dark Emperor's words echo through the land, stirring both fear and hope within the hearts of the people. Some bow before him, pledging their allegiance, while others flee in terror. Donderdraak watches from afar, his massive form shifting in the sky, sensing the shift in power and the potential for chaos.

Legion also observes from afar, feeling true, unadulterated terror for the first time in all of his multi-hundred billion year existence.

The Shadow King, Mordecai, growing ever more desperate to maintain control, begins to make a series of rash decisions. He strikes a deal with The Shadow Queen, Lilith, offering her unimaginable power in exchange for her aid in defeating The Dark Emperor. The Shadow Queen, Lilith, ever cunning, agrees, but secretly plans to betray both The Shadow King, Mordecai and The Dark Emperor, using their conflict to further her own nefarious goals.

But, before they can even realize it, they are suddenly little more than mere puppets to The Dark Emperor.

The Dark Emperor, having already claimed the land, begins to shape it to his liking. He raises dark towers that reach up to the very heavens, their spires dripping with malevolent energy. He creates an army of shadows, twisted beings that bend to his will, and unleashes them upon the world. The people of Alabama, once free from The Shadow King's rule, find themselves trapped in an even more insidious web of darkness and despair.

Chapter 14: The Shadow Queen

The Empire of Sunhaven decides to research ancient artifacts in an attempt to be able to possibly counter the seemingly omnipotent power of the Dark Emperor.

Legion, still curious about The Shadow Queen, Lilith, decides to send one of its most cunning and powerful servants, a Balor known as the Eye of Flame, to infiltrate The Shadow Queen Lilith's domain and gather information on her.

And then, suddenly he can no longer feel a connection to the Eye of Flame, and thus, decides to go with the Empire of Sunhaven's idea of researching ancient artifacts, giving critical information to them, considering he is quite sure that the Eye of Flame is either dead, being tortured, has been made a puppet, or some mixture of the three.

The Empire of Sunhaven, having received the critical information from Legion, begins its research into ancient artifacts, focusing on those that are rumored to have the power to banish or bind The Dark Emperor. They study scrolls, decipher ancient texts, and consult with scholars and sages from across the world. They also begin to search for these artifacts, sending expeditions into the most remote corners of the realm, and even beyond, into the Ethereal Plane.

Legion reveals to them that the Sacred Relic that they have is in truth only the core shard of it, and that, the Sacred Relic shard they found being guarded by The Storm Dragon, Donderdraak was actually an Sacred Artifact, a piece of the actual Sacred Relic, which, when fully completed, is the only thing possibly capable of countering the Dark Emperor, considering his power had even God Emperor Marcus Aurelius concerned, and that they haven't the slightest clue how major that truly is, so, they must collect the rest of the Sacred Artifacts and do a ritual involving them, the main core of the Sacred Relic and multiple beings and objects of sufficient and significant magical and arcane power.

The Empire of Sunhaven, now more determined than ever to bring peace and freedom back to Alabama, begins an extensive search for the remaining Sacred Artifacts. They send emissaries to foreign lands, offering alliances and resources in exchange for aid in their quest. They also commission the greatest mages and sorcerers in their realm to scour ancient texts and lore for any information that might lead them to the location of the missing artifacts.

Legion then reveals that the thing they thought was the Sacred Relic was merely the most major Sacred Artifact, and that the actual Core Sacred Relic is in the Temple/Church/Tomb of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius in San Marino, the original core of the Empire of Sanctity.

The Empire of Sunhaven, emboldened by this newfound knowledge and their alliances with other kingdoms and organizations, launches a massive operation to locate and retrieve the remaining Sacred Artifacts. Their agents scour the world, searching in every nook and cranny, digging through ancient ruins and exploring forgotten caverns. They cross paths with other factions also seeking the artifacts, leading to tense standoffs and daring heists.

Meanwhile, Legion continues to secretly aid the Empire, providing information and resources when needed. The Eye of Flame, the Balor sent to infiltrate The Shadow Queen, Lilith's domain, manages to gather valuable intelligence about her plans and weaknesses. This information is relayed back to the Empire, allowing them to adjust their strategy and prepare for any potential confrontations with The Shadow Queen, Lilith.

But Legion does warn to not trust this intelligence blindly, for it could be fake and mere deception.

But they are all shocked to learn that The Shadow Queen, Lilith is now, just like The Shadow King, Mordecai, merely a puppet to The Dark Emperor.

The Empire of Sunhaven, now more determined than ever, continues their quest to retrieve the remaining Sacred Artifacts. They send their most trusted agents to San Marino to retrieve the core Sacred Relic from the Temple/Church/Tomb of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius. The operation is difficult and dangerous, as they must navigate the treacherous politics of the Empire of Sanctity and face the guardians of the temple, who will not give up their sacred charge without a fight.

Meanwhile, Legion, through the Eye of Flame, discovers that The Shadow Queen, Lilith has been amassing an army of her own, preparing for a final confrontation with The Dark Emperor. The Eye of Flame manages to steal a map from her lair, revealing her plan to invade the Shadow Realm and claim its power for herself. The Empire of Sunhaven uses this information to their advantage, striking a deal with The Shadow Queen, Lilith to share the power of the Shadow Realm in exchange for their aid in banishing The Dark Emperor.

But Legion doesn't believe a thing of this, especially after learning that The Shadow Queen, Lilith is a mere puppet of The Dark Emperor and it's debatable whether she even has free will, after all, only a manipulator can recognize another manipulator

The Shadow Queen, Lilith asks them why they would want to banish The Dark Emperor, then leaves them shocked by revealing that The Dark Emperor is the origin of why the Empire of Frosthaven came to be, which is in of itself, the previous version of the Empire of Sunhaven, thus making The Dark Emperor responsible for the existence of the Empire of Sunhaven.

Legion barely believes this though, considering this could very easily just be The Dark Emperor speaking to them through The Shadow Queen, Lilith

The Empire of Sunhaven, still determined to banish The Dark Emperor, agrees to The Shadow Queen, Lilith's terms. They begin to prepare for the ritual, gathering the remaining Sacred Artifacts and readying themselves for the confrontation with The Dark Emperor. Legion, acting as a double agent, provides them with information about The Shadow Queen, Lilith's true intentions, warning them that they cannot trust her.

Legion even internally plans to and begins to subtly set the seeds for him to become the Emperor of the Empire of Sunhaven, considering they are currently a bunch of easily manipulated idiots.

He also reveals that the ritual won't work, because there literally is no method with any hope of banishing The Dark Emperor without the full True Sacred Relic.

The Empire of Sunhaven, disheartened but not defeated, continues their quest for the remaining Sacred Artifacts. They dispatch their most skilled agents to locate and retrieve them from their hiding places across the world. Meanwhile, Legion, still serving as a double agent, continues to provide them with valuable intelligence about The Shadow Queen, Lilith's intentions. The Eye of Flame, embedded deeper within her ranks, discovers that she has been secretly amassing an army of undead soldiers, preparing for a final battle against The Dark Emperor.

The Empire of Sunhaven then learns from Legion that attempting to infiltrate San Marino to get the Core Sacred Relic would be an awful idea due to how immensely dangerous Europe has become over the centuries.

The Empire of Sunhaven, still determined to retrieve the Core Sacred Relic, decides to instead focus on locating the rest of the missing Sacred Artifacts. They send out teams of their most skilled agents to search for them, combing through ancient texts and legends in hopes of finding clues to their whereabouts.

Chapter 15: Emperor of Sunhaven

Legion also begins to put into play his masterful plan of manipulation and deception to become the leader of the Empire of Sunhaven, considering their current leadership is a bunch of morons.

He starts by planting the seeds of doubt within Emperor Travis's mind, subtly suggesting that the pursuit of the Core Sacred Relic is futile without understanding its true nature and power. He hints at the possibility that the real key to defeating The Dark Emperor lies not in brute force, but in the shadows themselves.

Emperor Travis, desperate for a new approach, begins to listen more closely to Legion's counsel. He sees the wisdom in Legion's words and decides to shift the empire's focus from direct confrontation to one of strategy and guile. He calls for a meeting with his most trusted advisors to discuss Legion's proposal.

Legion firstly, manipulates Emperor Travis into trusting him to the point of appointing him as his second-in-command, and thus, the one that would become emperor if he were ever to die, and to announce it on live television in an announcement to the entire Empire of Sunhaven, and this is utter child's play for Legion.

The Empire of Sunhaven, now with Legion as its second-in-command, starts to rebrand their quest as a search for the "Sacred Knowledge" rather than just artifacts, which is more palatable to the public and less alarming. This shift in narrative allows them to operate more freely and gather allies under the guise of scholarly pursuit.

Then Legion places a tiny bit of himself into Emperor Travis and, during a more minor meeting, uses this to then cause Emperor Travis's heart to just suddenly stop.

Panic ensues as the Emperor of Sunhaven, Emperor Travis, collapses to the ground, his heart having suddenly ceased to beat. The room is filled with gasps and cries of despair, as the once vibrant ruler of the empire lies lifeless before his stunned council. Legion, feigning shock and grief, steps forward and takes command, declaring a state of emergency and announcing his succession to the throne.

And it goes off without a hitch, and then, just like that, Legion, The Demon Lord, is the Emperor of the Empire of Sunhaven, and immediately begins his work, firing all of the other leadership, literally, in some cases, and hiring much more competent people and demons and such in their places, making an overall infinitely more competent leadership.

The new Emperor Legion, now in power, begins his reign with a clear vision: to harness the power of the Sacred Artifacts and use them to not only defeat The Dark Emperor but to also reshape the world in his image. He calls for the best and brightest from across the realms to come to Sunhaven and aid in his quest for power. His words resonate with those who seek change and a world free from the tyranny of darkness.

Which Legion finds ironic, considering The Light is the true tyrannical power in control, but, he doesn't bring it up, and that's intentional, considering that he, as a demon, is a being of The Light.

The Shadow, one of the three Break-off states from the Empire of Sunhaven, is struck by a disastrous Hurricane.

The hurricane, named Hurricane Aldric, strikes the Shadow with unprecedented force. Winds gust up to 250 kilometers per hour, causing widespread damage to the already fragile infrastructure. The storm surge floods low-lying areas, forcing millions to evacuate their homes and seek higher ground. Power lines are toppled, roads are washed away, and buildings are reduced to rubble.

The Shadow government, already weakened by internal strife and external threats, is overwhelmed by the disaster. Its emergency response systems are inadequate, and many of its leaders are killed or injured in the storm. The survivors are left to fend for themselves, scrambling to find food, shelter, and medical care.

As the world begins to learn of the devastation wrought by Hurricane Aldric, aid begins to pour in from allied nations and international relief organizations. The Empire of Sunhaven, still reeling from the loss of the Shadow, must now balance its own needs with the urgent task of providing aid to its former sister state.

Legion, now the leader of the Empire, mobilizes his forces to help with the recovery effort. He dispatches his most trusted agents to San Marino to investigate the possibility of safely retrieving the Core Sacred Relic. After weeks of careful planning and reconnaissance, they report back to the Demon Lord with a daring plan to infiltrate the Vatican City and steal the relic while the world's attention is focused on the Shadow's recovery, but then report that shockingly the Empire of Frostreach, the rival empire to the Empire of Sunhaven, has already gotten the Core Sacred Relic. And further reports how they have already returned to their empire, against which war isn't possible currently without crossing the lands of neutral states.

Legion, knowing that the Empire of Sunhaven would not be pleased with this news, decides to keep it a secret for the time being, after all, chaos within the empire would do no good, despite how amusing it would be to him, as a demon. He orders his agents to continue their search for the remaining Sacred Artifacts while also aiding the Shadow in its recovery. As months pass, the Shadow begins to rebuild under the watchful eye of Legion. The infrastructure is restored, homes are rebuilt, and the survivors find hope once again.

Meanwhile, the Empire of Sunhaven continues to gather information about the whereabouts of the remaining Sacred Artifacts. They dispatch their most trusted agents to investigate leads in South America, where it is rumored that the Heart Sacred Relic may be hidden. After weeks of searching, they finally locate it deep within the heart of the Amazon rainforest, guarded by a tribe of warriors who have sworn to protect it until the end of time.

The news of the disaster does not go unnoticed by the other nations. Emperor Aldric of Frostreach, now strengthened by the Core Sacred Relic and the loyalty of his subjects, organizes a massive relief effort. Aid pours in from across the world, with food, water, medicine, and building materials being flown in and trucked in to assist the struggling survivors.

Donderdraak, now a symbol of hope and renewal in the wake of destruction, accompanies the relief efforts, using his immense power to clear debris and create makeshift landing zones for aircraft. The Undead Druids of Brocéliande, led by Archdruid Malthus, set up camps to provide medical care and shelter for the injured, while the Avatars of the Four Elements work tirelessly to rebuild the damaged infrastructure.

The people of the Shadow, once proud and independent, are now forced to rely on the aid of others. They are humbled by the experience, and many begin to question their government's ability to protect them. The Emperor of Frostreach, seeing an opportunity to bring the Shadow back into the fold, offers his assistance in rebuilding and restoring order, as well as his protection against any further threats.

The Shadow's government, recognizing the wisdom and strength that Emperor Aldric possesses, accepts his offer of aid and protection. They pledge their loyalty to the Frostreach Empire, acknowledging that only by uniting can they hope to rebuild their shattered homeland.

Emperor Aldric, moved by the people's humility and resilience, agrees to accept the Shadow into his fold. He appoints a new governor, a trusted advisor who has proven himself loyal and capable during the relief efforts. The governor, grateful for the opportunity to serve, vows to rebuild the Shadow stronger and more resilient than ever before.

The Empire of Sunhaven decides to attempt to research ancient artifacts hidden beneath ruins they attempt to find in a lost city they attempt to find.

They send a team of archaeologists and historians to South America, where they believe the Heart Sacred Relic may be hidden. Months of arduous exploration and excavation lead them to a long-lost city, hidden deep within the jungle. The city, once thriving and bustling with life, is now overgrown with vines and reclaimed by the wilderness. The team of researchers clear a path through the dense foliage and begin their search for any signs of the Heart Sacred Relic.

As they delve deeper into the city, they come across ancient ruins and hidden chambers, filled with artifacts and relics that shed light on the history and culture of the people who once lived there. They find intricately carved statues depicting the Heart Sacred Relic, hidden away in secret vaults and protected by ancient traps and guardians. The archaeologists work tirelessly, piecing together the clues and solving the puzzles that lead them closer to their ultimate goal.

Days turn into weeks, and weeks into months as the expedition continues. The team begins to grow weary, but their determination remains strong. They are driven by the knowledge that they are on the brink of discovering something truly significant; a relic that could change the course of history. One fateful day, after hours of painstaking excavation, they uncover a hidden chamber deep within the heart of the city.

The chamber is illuminated by a faint, otherworldly light, emanating from a pedestal in the center of the room. Upon the pedestal rests the Heart Sacred Relic, its glow pulsing with an ethereal energy. The archaeologists approach with caution, their hearts racing with anticipation and fear. As they draw nearer, they can feel the relic's power radiating off of it, as if it is alive. The air grows thick with a sense of reverence and awe.

One of the team members, an elderly historian named Dr. Weston, steps forward. His hands tremble as he reaches out to touch the Heart Sacred Relic. As his fingers graze its smooth surface, he feels a surge of power course through him, like a bolt of lightning. The relic begins to hum, its light growing brighter, and its energy pulsing with an almost audible rhythm.

Chapter 16: Empire of Frostreach

Then the Empire of Frostreach apparently attacks and conquers and annexes the State of Iowa, which smells fishy to Legion, considering that literally only provides a direct border with the Empire of Sunhaven which he rules, and, well, he can see it in the cards, but keeps his realization to himself.

The Empire of Frostreach then through stealth and espionage, and also because Legion might have "accidentally" left a few key doors open, manages to steal the Sacred Artifacts gathered by the Empire of Sunhaven, after all, he doesn't need them himself, and considering the Empire of Frostreach is led by a direct descendant and the true heir of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, it's only fitting.

The Empire of Sunhaven, after having had their Sacred Artifacts, Sacred Relic, and Heart Sacred Relic stolen from them by the Empire of Frostreach in North Dakota, decides to attempt to negotiate the ceasefire with them.

The diplomats of Sunhaven send an envoy to the capital city of the Empire of Frostreach, hoping to engage in peace talks and retrieve what was taken from them. The envoy is met with a mixture of suspicion and curiosity by the leaders of Frostreach. They understand the importance of the artifacts and relics and are reluctant to simply hand them over without some sort of concession.

The envoy meets the Emperor of Frosthaven, Emperor Aldric, the descendant and heir of the God Emperor of Sanctity and the Holy Roman Empire Marcus Aurelius himself, who is currently holding the Core Sacred Relic.

The room is adorned with intricate tapestries depicting scenes of imperial triumph, the walls lined with statues of former emperors and great generals. The throne upon which Emperor Aldric sits is crafted from solid gold and adorned with countless precious gems. His presence commands respect and awe.

The envoy of Sunhaven presents their case, explaining the historical significance of the stolen artifacts and relics and stressing the importance of their return. Emperor Aldric listens intently, his expression unreadable. After a moment of contemplation, he leans forward in his throne, resting his chin on his hands.

He then proceeds to explain that they aren't getting their Sacred Relics and Sacred Artifacts back, because, by blood, they belong to him and his empire.

Emperor Aldric continues, "My ancestor, the God Emperor of Sanctity and the Holy Roman Empire Marcus Aurelius himself, who I am currently holding the Core Sacred Relic for, entrusted me with the caretaking of these artifacts and relics. They were meant to be a symbol of unity, not a tool of division. By stealing them, you have brought nothing but strife and turmoil upon yourselves."

He then continues talking, beginning to berate and belittle the Empire of Sunhaven.

"Also, your empire carries the blood of the empire my ancestor used the True Sacred Relic to defeat, you aren't worthy to even lay your eyes upon them, let alone touch them, let alone possess them."

Emperor Aldric's voice booms through the room, echoing off the walls. The envoy of Sunhaven winces at the words, feeling a mix of anger and frustration rising within them. They attempt to maintain their composure, but it's becoming increasingly difficult.

"Your words, Emperor Aldric, only serve to further illustrate your unfitness to hold such sacred artifacts and relics," the envoy says, their voice steady despite the turmoil within. "You have twisted their true meaning and intent. They were meant to unite all people, not to be used as tools of division and oppression."

Emperor Aldric chuckles before speaking.

"Yes, under my ancestor's empire, and mine, the successor of it, not the empire it was used to defeat, nor yours, the descendant of that wretched empire."

Emperor Aldric pauses for a moment, leaning back in his throne, studying the envoy of Sunhaven intently. "You speak of unity, of bringing people together, of peace. Yet, your actions speak otherwise. You have attacked our lands, stolen what does not belong to you, and sought to start a war. Tell me, what kind of peace do you truly seek?"

Emperor Aldric pauses for a moment, before beginning to yell out his next words.

"I stole what is rightfully mine from your Empire! The ones who truly stole them are your empire! And, don't even think of declaring war, or I will repeat what my ancestor did, and use them to dissolve your empire and bring it all rightfully under my control!"

Emperor Aldric's voice booms out in rage.

The envoy of Sunhaven, taken aback by the Emperor's anger, struggles to maintain their composure. They take a deep breath, trying to calm themselves before responding. "Your words, Emperor Aldric, only serve to further illustrate your twisted understanding of history and the true intent of these artifacts. They were meant to unite all people, regardless of empire or bloodline. It is you who seek to divide us, to keep us apart."

Emperor Aldric bursts out laughing.

"Only because by the end of it there was only one empire left to unite under, my Ancestor's!"

Emperor Aldric is bursting out laughing like a madman, yet his eyes show that this entire time he couldn't be more calm.

"Oh, please, spare me the sob stories and the victim complex! It's not like your empire was the one that got to keep the relics after your ancestor's empire was disbanded!" Aldric says between bouts of laughter. "And don't you dare say that I'm trying to divide you! It's your own damn fault that you attacked us and started this whole mess! If you had just left things as they were, none of this would have happened!"

Emperor Aldric finally composes himself, his face turning serious. "Now, I suggest you take this message back to your emperor. Tell him that I will not give up what is rightfully mine, and that if he continues down this path of war, he will only bring ruin upon his people."

The envoy of Sunhaven listens, feeling a mixture of anger, frustration, and fear. They nod solemnly, understanding the gravity of the situation. "I will convey your message to my emperor," they say, rising from their seat. "However, know that this does not mean peace is impossible. There may still be room for negotiation."

Emperor Aldric then begins speaking again.

"I indeed do seek peace, but my peace doesn't include an Empire of Sunhaven, or said Empire of Sunhaven holding my Sacred Relics and Sacred Artifacts."

Emperor Aldric, despite the angry words he is using, couldn't be more calm.

The envoy of Sunhaven listens carefully to Aldric's final statement, pondering its implications. They nod, understanding that peace will not come easily in this situation. As they turn to leave the throne room, their heart heavy with the weight of the message they carry, they can't help but wonder if there truly is any hope for reconciliation between their empires. The halls of the palace seem to echo with the tension and uncertainty of the moment, as if the very stones themselves are bracing for the storm that is to come.

Legion though, overall, doesn't care very much.

Then, some California scientists made Mutant Creatures that wreaked havoc there, and Legion can't help but feel kinda bad for the State of California, and he's a demon, considering they first got attacked by Donderdraak, and now Mutant Creatures created from within, and apparently the Empire of Frostreach attempted to take down the Mutant Creatures, and failed, and apparently the scientists fled to the Arctic Circle and made new Mutants called "Neohumans", and the Empire of Frostreach decided to let them exist, and had them help destroy the older and savage Mutant Creatures, and then they spread about the globe, and many of them even joined the Empire of Sunhaven, how handy.

Chapter 18: The Alien Invasion

The State of Alabama, under the control of the Dark Emperor, is suddenly struck by an Alien Invasion.

The Neohumans of Frostreach and California, alarmed by the news, spring into action. They realize that the Empire of Sunhaven, distracted by its recent advancements in energy weapons technology, may not be able to offer much assistance in repelling the invaders. They decide to mount a two-pronged attack on the alien forces, hoping to drive them back before they can establish a foothold in the United States.

Meanwhile, the Empire of Sunhaven, despite its preoccupations, sends a small contingent of its most advanced soldiers to aid in the effort. Equipped with the life-saving nanotech armor and energy weapons, they are a formidable fighting force. As they arrive at the scene of the battle, they are awestruck by the scale of the conflict. Alien ships fill the skies, pouring countless numbers of soldiers onto the ground below. The air is thick with the sounds of explosions and the screams of the wounded.

The Neohumans of Frostreach and California quickly coordinate their efforts, using their advanced energy weapons to take down the alien ships from afar while their soldiers engage the enemy on the ground. The aliens, however, prove to be a formidable foe, displaying advanced technology and strange abilities that the human forces have never encountered before. The battle rages on for days, with neither side gaining a clear advantage.

Meanwhile, the small contingent of soldiers from the Empire of Sunhaven undergo an intense training program, learning to harness the full potential of their energy weapons and nanotech armor. They are eager to join the fray, but their superiors insist that they must wait for the right opportunity to make a difference. Finally, they are given the signal to engage, and they charge into the heart of the battle, using their advanced technology to turn the tide of the conflict.

The Neohumans of Frostreach and California, who have been fighting valiantly but are beginning to suffer heavy casualties, are overjoyed when they see the reinforcements arrive. The soldiers from Sunhaven quickly form a cohesive unit, working seamlessly with their allies to push back the alien forces. Their energy weapons prove to be particularly effective against the aliens' armored exoskeletons, while their self-healing nanotech armor allows them to absorb the aliens' powerful energy attacks with ease.

As the battle reaches its climax, the combined forces of the Neohumans and the Empire of Sunhaven manage to gain the upper hand. The aliens, realizing that they cannot win, begin to retreat, launching a desperate last-ditch effort to destroy as much as possible before they are forced to abandon their invasion. The skies fill with explosions as the aliens destroy their remaining ships, while their ground forces launch a wave of suicidal attacks against the human defenders.

The soldiers of Sunhaven find themselves at the center of the chaos, their advanced energy weapons and nanotech armor allowing them to hold the line against the tide of alien soldiers. They form a tightly knit group, their discipline and training shining through in the midst of the confusion. The Neohumans, impressed by their bravery and skill, begin to see them less as outsiders and more as allies in this common struggle.

The Empire of Sunhaven decides to attempt to contact and establish diplomatic relations with the alien invaders to learn more about their technology.

The Neohumans of Frostreach and California, impressed by the soldiers from Sunhaven, begin to collaborate with them more closely on other projects, including energy weapons research and nanotechnology development.

Chapter 20: The Uno Battle 0

(85 Years Later)

The Year is 2220 and Legion, Isolde, and Donderdraak are playing a heated game of uno together...

Isolde seems to get to Uno too fast, leaving both Donderdraak and Legion suspicious of her of cheating.

The Vampire Queen's laughter fills the underground chamber, echoing off the cold stone walls. She holds up her hand, displaying her cards with a flourish. "But I shall be watching you closely from now on."

The room goes silent, and the air grows tense. Isolde's smile falters, but only for a moment. Then she laughs, tossing her cards onto the table. "Ah, you caught me," she says, her eyes sparkling with amusement. "But in all seriousness, we mustn't let our guard down. Even in games of leisure, we must be vigilant.", Isolde says somewhat teasingly

Isolde's fangs grew long, and her eyes turned into pools of darkness, ready to strike back at the dragon. Donderdraak's scales began to crackle with lightning, the very essence of the storm he once was, as he glared at his former allies.

His massive form is a blur of lightning and shadow as he slams into the demon lord, sending a shockwave through the room. The clang of steel and the crackle of electricity fill the air as the two ancient beings clash. Legion, his larger form a terrifying spectacle of brimstone and claws, meets Donderdraak's fury with a ferocity that would make lesser demons cower.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen then flies in faster than both of them and uses her immense strength for her size and blood manipulation to then throw both of them around.

Isolde then also controls the blood within them to further throw them around.

Isolde, unperturbed by the accusations, had been quietly preparing for the moment she could unleash her own power. She had felt the shift in the air, the tension between her two companions, and she knew that the game was no longer just a game. With a grace that belied her deadly nature, she darts forward, her hands a blur as she grabs fistfuls of their essences, dragging them apart and into the air.

Donderdraak then returns back to fighting position the fastest of the three, and launched barrages of lightning and small Charged Attacks at both of them.

Legion, his further demonic form a towering specter of rage, dodges a bolt of lightning and swipes at Donderdraak with his fiery claws, leaving a trail of scorched air in their path. Isolde, now a tornado of darkness, due to her basic understanding of dark magic, weaves around them, her crimson eyes never leaving her targets as she prepares to pounce.

Isolde entered into a state known as Queen's Bloodlust, a lesser version of Queen's Bloodfury but one that doesn't cause nearly guaranteed death, but still massively increases her speed and strength and such.

Legion though, was prepared to at a moments notice enter his own higher state, known as Berserker, where he, well, goes berserk, but all of his physical attributes increase immensely.

But Donderdraak, not one to be underestimated, is too quick for them. With a twist of his massive body, he throws them off, sending them crashing into the far corners of the chamber.

Then Legion fires massive beams of hellfire and electricity while Isolde joins him with beams of blood magic and energy as their beams combine and Donderdraak counters with beams of lightning and electricity from his hands, leaving them evenly matched.

The room is now a spectacle of color and chaos, as the three beings of immense power battle it out. The air crackles with energy, the walls of the chamber etched with the marks of their clashing attacks. Each beam of light and dark magic colliding with a deafening clap, sending waves of force that ripple through the very fabric of the room. Chapter 21: The Demon Kingdom

"Anyways, you two, what game do you want to play next? Another game of Uno" Legion then asks them casually, now out of his Berserker state.

Isolde is seemingly chuckling at his reaction to winning while he was in his Berserker state, while Donderdraak is clearly very jealous at losing to Legion, both in the battle, and in Uno.

The Vampire Queen wipes the blood off her fangs with the tip of her tongue, her eyes still glowing faintly with the remnants of her power. She looks over at the smoking dragon, who is now visibly smaller in defeat. "Perhaps we should play something less... explosive next time," she suggests, her voice still echoing with the power of the Queen's Bloodlust.

"Of course, of course." Legion responds, "Can you two think of anything we need to do relating to the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings?" Legion asks, refusing to refer to it by its acronym of USSB.

Isolde, her eyes glowing a bit less, looks over to Legion, "Well, we do need to check on the new members and make sure that the recent influx of vampires from the Dracula Kingdom are fitting in well. And don't forget the Ice Elemental and Archdruid Malthus. They're still getting used to the new environment."

"Oh yes, the Ice Elemental..." Legion responds, the jealousy extremely clear in his voice, after all, the Ice Elemental has both a better fight record than Legion and is taller than him by 34 meters at their peak heights.

Isolde can't hold herself back from chuckling at Legion's jealousy of the Ice Elemental, of which he does a terrible job at hiding, and Donderdraak can't help but join in with the chuckling.

"Don't worry, Legion," she says soothingly, "you're still the biggest baddie around here. Besides, size isn't everything, right?" She winks playfully, her mood shifting from battle-ready to casual in a heartbeat.

"Shut up! Anyways, my anger reminded me, I need to make a trip back to Heaven, or Hell, or the Underworld, whichever name you wanna refer to it by, to transfer my castle here, along with my entire kingdom there now that I think about it." Legion says in response.

The two look at him bewildered, "Wait, you can just move your entire kingdom?!" Donderdraak exclaims.

"Of course, I mean, Isolde already did it with her's, the, Dracula Kingdom, if I am remembering correctly, right Isolde?" Legion asks, pointing out how this wouldn't be the first time they did something like this.

Isolde nods, a small smirk playing on her lips, "Indeed, I did. And it's been quite an adjustment for everyone. But if it means keeping our people safe and our enemies at bay, then it's a price worth paying."

Legion then rips a hole in reality into Heaven/Hell/the Underworld, which is, of course all of those at once, much to the shock of Isolde and Donderdraak, who thought maybe it was just a Realm that contained all of them within it, not that they were all literally one and the same.
, "Alright you two, wanna just let me do this solo, or accompany me there?" Legion then asks them.

The two exchange a look, then nod in agreement. They had seen their friend do some pretty wild things before, but opening a portal to another plane of existence was a new one.

Legion then hops through the hole in the fabric of reality into Heaven/Hell/the Underworld, "I honestly just prefer to refer to this place as the Infernal Realm, for ease of conversation, after all, either way, is where most people go where they die anyways." Legion comments.

Isolde and Donderdraak follow close behind, their eyes wide with wonder and a hint of fear. The sight before them is unlike anything they've seen before. The skies are a swirling mix of fiery reds and icy blues, and the landscape stretches on forever, a tapestry of darkness and light.

But it looks like the icy blues and darkness are both being cruelly choked by the fiery reds and light respectively.

Legion nods solemnly, "Yes, it seems the Infernal Realm is in a bit of a turmoil lately. The balance between the Fiery and the Frozen is out of whack. But that's another problem for another day. For now, let's get this castle moving!"

Legion then leads them along through a village of demons, who all swiftly bow to him upon sight, and to a massive and fiery and truly infernal castle.

The castle, a towering monolith of brimstone and steel, loomed over the landscape, its spires piercing the tumultuous sky. The air around it was thick with the scent of sulfur and the cries of the damned, a stark contrast to the serene calmness of the USSB Realm. As they approached, the demons and other creatures of the night grew more and more restless, sensing the power of their lord and his allies.

Legion then opens the doors, and it goes straight into the throne room, where on the throne was the acting ruler in Legion's place set up by Legion himself, the Demon King, "Hey Army, I'm back, we're moving the kingdom to a new Realm I co-rule." Legion says to the demon on the throne, who then promptly bows deeply, "O-of course my lord." Army, The Demon King responds as he bows.

"Great, I've got the paperwork already set up, just need your signature here, here, and here." Legion says as he presents a parchment to Army, who takes it, looks it over, and nods before signing it with a flourish.

Legion then makes it disappear into fire, like a devil's contract, and then, he looks to Donderdraak, who shrinks down the Infernal Kingdom into a small orb, as then Legion opens another hole in the fabric of reality back into the USSB Realm, and the trio then hops through it.

They emerge in the grand hall of the USSB headquarters, and the orb in Legion's hand grows into the massive castle again. The castle unfurls from the orb, its fiery stones and steel beams stretching out and taking form, the ground shaking with its weight. The demonic inhabitants of the castle stream out, bewildered but also excited by the new surroundings.

The many demonic villages then appear throughout the USSB Realm as well, along with their demon inhabitants.

"Wow Donderdraak, guess we're lucky you learned that trick from Geralt while you both worked for that Empire of Frostreach, aren't we?" Legion comments, due to Donderdraak learning that trick of shrinking down massive things to transport them from Geralt, The Lich King, while both were part of the Empire of Frostreach.

Donderdraak nods, "Indeed, that was quite the... educational experience." He says with a hint of bitterness in his voice, remembering his past servitude.

"Too bad we couldn't convince Geralt himself to join us, he blew me off, stating he had "more important things to do" than join the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings, sucks for him more than us." Legion says, "Also, didn't you once mention how you didn't really even serve the Empire of Frostreach, just worked as part of them as part of a deal you made a "long" time ago, when in my eyes at least it wasn't that long ago, only 1,216 Years ago now." Legion further comments.

Isolde, watching the demons integrate with the USSB society, muses aloud, "Perhaps we should consider a council for the various supernatural factions. It could help us avoid misunderstandings and potential conflicts in the future."

"Are you saying that us demons inherently cause chaos and conflicts? Stereotyping much..." Legion responds sounding offended, but in a mostly joking and humorous way.

"Well, I wouldn't say that, but we do have a reputation to maintain," Isolde says with a wink, her fangs gleaming in the flickering light of the now fully formed castle.

"With who? Nobody outside of the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings even knows it exists outside of Geralt, and very few people even know that he even exists, and pretty much none would want to seek him out." Legion responds.

"I know, but we could still have a council for everyone within the society, it'd help with organizing events and keeping everyone on the same page, not just preventing conflicts." Isolde says, "Plus it'd be a good way to get everyone to know each other better."

Chapter 22: The Manipulator

"Sure, you can work on that, I'm more a manipulator than a diplomat, unlike you Isolde." Legion says, and Isolde and Donderdraak are admittedly shocked at hearing Legion refer to himself as a "manipulator", and just have to further ask.

"How do you manipulate people, exactly?" Donderdraak asks curiously, his voice a low rumble.

"Oh, in a lot of ways, and under various disguises and identities." Legion responds, and this just makes Isolde and Donderdraak even more curious.

"You mean, like you're not just Legion all the time?" Isolde asks with a raised eyebrow, "How many identities do you have?"

"Oh, I had two major fake ones, but, in total, I've lost count." Legion responds, and this just makes Isolde and Donderdraak even more curious and intrigued.

The thought of Legion, the feared demon lord, having other identities that they were unaware of, piqued their interest. "Who were they?" Isolde asks, her voice a mix of wonder and suspicion.

"Well we of course have one that was less a "fake" identity, and more a real identity I stole, Pope Clement III." Legion says as he shape-shifts back into the body of Pope Clement III, who Isolde has no clue who he is, but Donderdraak does.

"Wait, THAT Pope Clement III? The Pope from during the time of the Empire of Sanctity and former Emperor of the Holy Roman Empire? And how long were you him for?" Donderdraak asks in shock.

"Oh, from late Year 1000 until the Year 1206, not that long." Legion responds, as if he hadn't just said a time frame covering over 200 years.

The revelation leaves Isolde and Donderdraak stunned, "That's over 200 years! What could you have possibly achieved as the Pope that you couldn't as Legion?" Isolde asks, her eyes wide with astonishment.

"Well, I didn't have my body for about 6 years of that, and, manipulation of Marcus Aurelius and the events around that time as a whole, just pulling strings, and all that happened went exactly according to my plans from back then, ah, good times." Legion responds casually while reminiscing.

Donderdraak and Isolde exchange glances, still trying to process the sheer scope of Legion's influence. They had known he was powerful, but this was on an entirely different level.

"And what about that other identity?" Isolde asks.

Legion then shape shifts into "Mr. Black", "Good old "Mr. Black"" Legion says as "Mr. Black", and this time neither Isolde nor Donderdraak have a clue who that is.

"Who?" Donderdraak asks.

"Well, back in the Year 1843 through to the Year about 1845, I as Mr. Black manipulated every major country and nation in the world, using them all like puppets and chess pieces, as they all competed for a Sacred Artifact, the Heart of the Dragon, and I manipulated them all and pulled the strings on everything, leading to a global conflict over the artifact between all nations fighting each other, it was glorious! And in the end I myself ended up claiming and consuming and absorbing the Heart of the Dragon, which granted control over the Concept of Fire, that was a fun time." Legion as "Mr. Black" explains, and Donderdraak seems to have an epiphany.

"Oh, THAT was the major event in the Year 1843, I, as the Storm Dragon, could sense something going on back then, even from my lair in what would lated become the State of California, but never knew what it was." Donderdraak comments.

Isolde, still trying to wrap her head around the idea of Legion being the Pope for over two centuries, finally speaks up, "But why the Pope? And what is this Heart of the Dragon artifact?"

"Well, when I, um... died... back in the Battle of Vatican City, I quickly latched my essence onto the soul of Pope Innocent III, causing him to slowly die, leading to Pope Clement III, who I had while sealed away back then let out a tiny bit of myself to eventually attach onto his soul, manipulating him into doing truly devious acts, but then, at the ceremony of Pope Clement III becoming THE Pope, I hopped from Pope Innocent III's pretty much already dead body to that of Pope Clement III, mostly due to the power I had politically and in regards to large scale manipulation as him, and as for the Heart of the Dragon, it was a Sacred Artifact, one that split off from the Sacred Relic when God Emperor Marcus Aurelius did so to split it apart and prevent others from abusing its power, which he clearly kinda failed at in total, yet also succeeded, and the Heart of the Dragon granted absolute control over the Concept of Fire itself." Legion explains, with both Isolde and Donderdraak for the most part understanding, but Isolde has no clue who God Emperor Marcus Aurelius is or what the Sacred Relic is.

Isolde nods slowly, trying to digest the information. "And what happened to this 'Mr. Black' identity?" she asks.

"Essentially, at the end of the last war, I revealed myself as him, then fell backwards into the volcano where the Maori people defending the Heart of the Dragon tried to hide it, and transformed into my actual self, and completely obliterated the volcano from within with a burst of infernal energy and magic, while grabbing the Heart of the Dragon for myself, then I ate and absorbed it, until a Maori shaman tried to stop me by teleporting me to the deepest part of the ocean, the Mariana Trench, but I then, with a burst of infernal energy and magic, instantly evaporated the entire ocean before arriving back in a True Instant, also known as a Planck Instant, and burnt away all of those opposing me, including the leaders of many powerful nations, and then, the intense rain to end all rains afterwards refilled back up the ocean, as lots of life magic was used to restore all of the life in the ocean, so pretty much, I just haven't used him in a while, because he would be too obvious." Legion further explains.

Donderdraak nods thoughtfully, "Ah, I see, quite the... dramatic exit you had there. But what's our next move now?"

"I dunno, was gonna do a scan of the entire Earth using magic to search for something interesting to do, considering I'm bored, you two do something else to keep yourselves occupied." Legion says, before then using a scan spell on the entire Earth to search for something to keep himself occupied with.

Isolde and Donderdraak look at each other, then back at Legion. "Well, if you're looking for something to do, we do have that council idea for the USSB to work on." Isolde suggests, trying to lighten the mood.

"I meant something actually interesting to do, you two deal with that, especially you Isolde, considering your the diplomat amongst us three." Legion responds, getting back to his scan spell on the entire Earth of the Mortal Realm.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, decide to use this time to explore the newly-expanded USSB Realm, now housing the Infernal Kingdom and its demonic inhabitants. They are met with a mix of curiosity and caution from the demons, who recognize them as companions of Legion but are unsure of their intentions.

Chapter 23: The Kingdom of Elysium

Legion meanwhile tears a hole in the fabric of reality in the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings) Realm and hops through it into the Mortal Realm, then closing it behind him, to instead do his scan spell in the Mortal Realm itself to increase its speed and efficiency.

The demons, now living in a realm where they are not feared and hunted, begin to assimilate into the USSB society. Some find work as guards, using their natural abilities to protect the supernatural citizens from any threats that may arise. Others open up shops and stalls, selling their wares and offering their unique skills to those who seek them.

Meanwhile, back in the Mortal Realm, Legion continues using his scan spell on the Earth of the current Year of 2220, during the era of forced peace caused by the True Sacred Relic, trying to find something interesting to amuse himself with and to dim his boredom.

The scan reveals to him the existence of the Kingdom of Elysium, a hidden realm nestled in the Himalayas, which has somehow remained untouched by modern technology and warfare. Intrigued, Legion decides to pay them a visit, curious about their secrets and potential for alliances or conflict.

He is mostly hoping for conflict, he's itching for a fight with something that isn't his fellow co-leaders of the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings.

The Kingdom of Elysium is indeed a place of wonder and mystery. High in the Himalayas, it remained untouched by the chaos and destruction of the modern world. It was a bastion of ancient magic and knowledge, governed by a council of immortal beings known as the Elders. The moment Legion's demonic energy was detected, the council knew they had an uninvited guest.

The Elders, wary of the demon lord's infamous tactics, dispatched their most skilled warriors and mages to intercept him. The air crackled with anticipation as the two sides met in the heart of the hidden valley. Legion, ever the strategist, had anticipated a challenge and had brought a few tricks of his own. He summoned a swarm of lesser demons to serve as a distraction, while he approached the Kingdom's borders under the guise of a mortal diplomat seeking an audience with the council.

As the battle raged outside, Legion presented himself as a peaceful envoy, his true form hidden beneath a glamour. The guards, trained to detect deceit, were fooled by his charismatic words and the forged scrolls that bore the seal of the USSB. The gates to Elysium slowly opened, revealing the gleaming city of gold and jewels nestled within the mountains. Legion stepped inside, a sly smile playing on his lips.

Legion then slipped into the meeting office of the Elders, sitting in the one vacant chair, still under his human disguise.

The Elders, ancient and wise, looked upon him with a mix of curiosity and caution. "Who are you, mortal, to seek an audience with the immortals of Elysium?" spoke the eldest, his eyes piercing through the guise.

Legion then shed his human disguise with fire, "I come offering a very simple deal." Legion said with a calmness that almost seemed to contradict his demonic and infernal appearance.

The Elders, unphased by his true form, had seen many strange things in their long lives. The eldest spoke again, "A deal, you say? We are not a people to be swayed by material riches or power, demon. What could you possibly offer us?"

"Well, the deal is simple, you gather all of your forces, from the beginners to the most elite, along with yourselves, and you fight me, and, well, here comes the actual deal..." Legion begins, giving the Elder's time to process his words and possibly respond.

The Elder's eyes narrow, "If we win, what do we gain?"

"An alliance with the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings, a powerful association of supernatural beings with its own separate realm, an alliance you would be foolish for denying..." Legion responds, giving the Elders time to process it and ask what happens if they lose.

The room is silent for a moment, the air thick with tension. The Elder's eyes never leave Legion's, as if searching for any hint of deceit. Finally, the youngest of the council speaks up, "And what would be the cost of losing?"

"Well, quite simply, you lose, in all ways and aspects." Legion says while quietly chuckling, the implication is clear, if they lose, they lose their lives and their entire Kingdom is destroyed, "After all, it is an all out battle." Legion further explains.

The youngest Elder seems to be the most skeptical, but the eldest nods slowly, his gaze unwavering. "We will consider your proposal," he says, his voice echoing with ancient wisdom.

"Oh, so you want to just be destroyed or just slay me and gain nothing for it?" Legion asks, much to their confusion, "Because the thing up for discussion is the rewards, not the battle itself, that WILL happen." Legion further explains.

The Elder's expression hardens, "You wish to test our mettle, then so be it. But if we win, we wish for your protection from the outside world. A promise that no harm will befall our lands from your kind."

"A deal has been agreed upon, now, gather all of your forces and prepare yourselves for combat, all of you and your forces against just me alone." Legion explains, and walks out, and in front of the entrance of the Kingdom of Elysium, waiting for them to prep themselves for the upcoming battle against him and him alone.

The Elders look at each other, knowing full well what this demon is capable of, but also knowing that a promise from a being of his power could mean the survival of their Kingdom. They nod in unison and begin to make the necessary preparations, summoning their most powerful beings and readying their ancient magics.

Isolde and Donderdraak, unaware of Legion's challenge to the Elders, continue exploring the USSB Realm. They come across the newly constructed council chamber, which is buzzing with activity as representatives from various supernatural factions argue over the new laws and policies. The sight of their fellow leaders missing from the discussion fills them with a sense of unease, but they are quickly swept up in the whirlwind of diplomacy and politics.

As Legion waits outside the Kingdom of Elysium, his anticipation grows. He feels the power of the Kingdom's defenders gathering, and his heart beats with excitement at the prospect of an actual challenge. He knows that the Elders will not take his proposal lightly, and that they will come prepared. He smiles to himself, eager to see what these ancient beings can bring to the table.

The Elders of Elysium, after much deliberation, accept Legion's challenge. They understand the gravity of the situation and the potential benefits of an alliance with the USSB. They decide to fight with honor, believing that victory or defeat would be a testament to their strength and the will of the universe. The entire Kingdom prepares for battle, their spirits high and their determination unwavering.

Legion, meanwhile, just waits for them to approach him with all of their forces and everything they got.

Days pass, and the Elders of Elysium emerge from their mountain fortress, leading an army that seems to have stepped out of a myth. Creatures of legend and power, they march forth to meet Legion. The demon lord's eyes light up with excitement as he sees the diversity of his opponents. The Elders, flanked by their most elite warriors, approach Legion, their ancient spells weaving a protective barrier around their forces.

Legion then increases his size up to his maximum size of 666m tall, "LET THE BATTLE BEGIN!" Legion roars out in excitement.

The Elders look at each other, their expressions a mix of awe and trepidation. The youngest Elder whispers to the eldest, "We may have made a mistake." But the eldest's eyes remain firm, filled with the determination of ages. "We stand for Elysium," he declares, and with that, the battle begins.

The 666m tall Legion then wipes out all but the most elite of the basic forces with little more than just a wave of one of his hands.

The elite forces of Elysium, seeing the destruction wrought by Legion's simple gesture, tighten their formations, their eyes filled with a mix of fear and determination. They know they are facing a being of immense power, but they are not easily daunted. The Elders begin to chant in an ancient language, and the air around them crackles with energy.

Legion though, recognizes this language, and grins, knowing exactly what they are about to do.

The chanting reaches a crescendo, and the Elders unleash a combined spell, a blast of pure elemental power that could shatter mountains. The ground shakes as it hits Legion, but to their shock, he merely laughs, the energy dissipating around his form, not even leaving a single mark on him. "Is that all you got, old timers?" He taunts, his voice echoing across the battlefield.

Legion then punches the ground, creating an earthquake that can be felt all of the way in South Africa.

The earthquake sends shockwaves through the battlefield, knocking over the remaining structures and sending the lesser creatures and warriors of Elysium tumbling like leaves in a storm. The elite forces struggle to maintain their footing, their eyes wide with shock and disbelief at Legion's power. The Elders, undeterred, channel their magic into a more potent spell, one that could freeze the sun itself.

Legion this time decides to begin to clash with the spell with a torrent of Golden Light Hellfire and Lightning from his mouth.

The two forces of magic collide with a thunderous boom that lights up the sky. The very air seems to burn with the intensity of the clash, and the ground beneath them cracks and splits. The Elders, their faces strained with effort, maintain their spell, but it is clear that Legion's power is too much for them.

Legion then fires two identical but smaller torrents of Golden Light Hellfire and Lightning from his hands.

The twin torrents of Hellfire and Lightning streak through the air, homing in on the Elders with a speed that seems to defy the very laws of physics. The Elders, caught off guard by the sheer ferocity of the attack, are unable to react in time. The blasts hit them squarely, and the ancient beings are sent flying through the air, their robes in tatters.

Legion then actually begins to use his full power, as a massive and fiery aura emerges from him, disintegrating all of the elite forces of the Kingdom of Elysium, showing he could have reduced it to a battle between just him and the Elders of Elysium from the very beginning if he really wanted to.

"Now now, that can't possibly be all that you have to offer, can it? I was expecting so much more." Legion says, slowly beginning to chuckle.

The Elders, though grievously injured, refuse to admit defeat. They stand, drawing upon the last of their strength to face Legion once more. Their eyes glow with a fierce determination that seems to ignite the very air around them. They begin to chant an incantation that has not been heard in centuries, one that could summon the very essence of Elysium itself to their aid.

And on top of that, the Elders also all fuse together into the True Elder of Elysium.

Chapter 24: True Elder of Elysium

The fusion of the Elders into the True Elder of Elysium is a sight to behold. Their individual forms merge into one colossal being, a giant wreathed in the elements of earth, wind, water, and fire. The creature stands tall and mighty, its eyes burning with the power of the four elements. The very earth around it seems to come alive, the air crackling with electricity and the ground trembling beneath its feet.

The True Elder of Elysium then shrinks down to about 6 feet tall to condense its power, and Legion also shrinks down to his "normal" height of 6.66 feet tall to face this new fused opponent, hoping this will be less disappointing.

The battle resumes with a ferocity that could only be described as a dance of the gods. The True Elder wields the elements with the grace of a master artist, each strike a symphony of power that resonates through the very fabric of reality. Legion, ever the tactician, counters with his own mastery of the inferno, his every move calculated to exploit the Elder's weaknesses.

The True Elder calls forth a tornado of wind and flame, aiming to engulf Legion in its fiery embrace. But the demon lord merely laughs, his wings unfurling to absorb the storm. His eyes flash with golden light, and a bolt of hellfire pierces the heart of the tornado, dissipating it into ash and embers. The Elder retaliates with a blast of ice that could freeze the very soul, but Legion merely smiles, his fiery aura burning hotter, melting the ice before it can even touch him.

The clash of their powers is a spectacle that even the gods would envy. The very sky above them is torn apart, revealing a swirling vortex of colors as the fabric of reality bends to their wills. The air is filled with the roars of beasts and the whispers of ancient incantations. Yet, amidst this chaos, Legion remains unfazed, his movements fluid and precise.

The True Elder, a creature of the elements, fuses its attacks into a single, unyielding force. It hurls a boulder the size of a small mountain at Legion, imbued with the combined might of earth and fire. But Legion simply lifts his hand, and a pillar of hellfire erupts from the ground, meeting the boulder mid-air and reducing it to molten lava. The lava then cools into a spike and plunges back down towards the Elder, only to be deflected by a gust of wind and shattered into a shower of crystal shards.

The fight continues, each blow and spell more powerful than the last. The landscape of Elysium is reshaped by their battle, valleys carved out and mountains leveled. Yet, neither can gain the upper hand. The True Elder, though powerful, is bound by the limitations of its elemental nature, while Legion's demonic energy seems to have no bounds.

The True Elder, sensing its impending doom, calls upon the last of its strength to perform the ultimate sacrifice. It channels the very essence of Elysium into one final attack, a colossal beam of pure light that pierces the dark aura surrounding Legion. The demon lord roars in fury, his body momentarily illuminated by the divine energy.

Legion then casually absorbs it, "Really? Your last resort was to use a beam of pure light, on a demon, a being inherently of light?" Legion asks, genuinely dumbfounded at his opponent's idiotic choice for a last resort attack.

The True Elder, now drained of power, falls to its knees, its form starting to disintegrate. Legion approaches, his fiery aura dimming slightly, a look of boredom mixed with disappointment etched on his face. "You truly had potential," he says, almost pityingly, "But you wasted it on a battle you never had the power to win."

Legion then consumes the True Elder, absorbing its power into himself, before then unleashing a burst of infernal energy and magic from himself that completely disintegrates the entirety of the Himalayas.

The once majestic Kingdom of Elysium now lies in ruins, its inhabitants scattered or vanquished. The land itself seems to mourn the loss of its protectors, the very air heavy with the scent of ash and the cries of the dying. Only Legion remains standing, his form now radiant with the combined power of the Elder's magic and his own hellish might.

Chapter 25: Core of the Earth

Legion then uses his scan spell again, looking for something else interesting around the Earth of the Year 2220 to hopefully actually end his boredom this time.

The demon lord's eyes light up as he detects a new presence, something vast and powerful hidden deep within the Earth's core. His interest piqued, he descends into the planet's fiery embrace, the heat and pressure seemingly inconsequential to his supernatural form. As he approaches the core, he senses a beating heart of energy, a force that calls to him like a siren's song.

Legion disintegrated a hole about 1,800 miles deep and a mile wide into the ground, straight down to the Earth's core with a single usage of his Hellfire breath.

The descent is swift, the heat and pressure increasing exponentially with each passing mile, but Legion feels no discomfort.
