After what seems like an eternity, she emerges from the sun, her skin glowing a dull red. She returns to the USSB Realm, her mission complete, the sun slightly smaller in the sky.

"Well done, and still you are barely sunburnt, anyways, your turn now Donderdraak, Truth or Dare?" Legion asks.

Donderdraak, feeling the heat from his last encounter with Legion, chooses truth, "Let's keep it simple, true or false, I was once defeated by a mere mortal?"

"To my knowledge, True, you were once defeated into being weakened into The Storm Dragon before, and I doubt it was a god, considering I've killed almost all of those myself, and for your dare, I dare you to go and use that "Charged Attack" of yours to go and blow up some random planet outside of the Solar System." Legion responds casually and correctly and yet still quite confidently.

Donderdraak, his pride stung once more, takes to the skies with a mighty roar, leaving a trail of lightning in his wake. He flies out of the solar system, searching for a planet suitable for his display of power. Isolde watches him go, her mind racing with the revelations of the evening.

After some time, Donderdraak returns, a hint of smugness in his demeanor. "It is done," he says, his stormy eyes gleaming with the satisfaction of a challenge met. "I have found a planet with no known life, and it has been reduced to ash and dust."

Chapter 40: The Dark Truth of Light

"Well done, also, I can sense that you two want to transition it to a normal game of Truth or Dare now, am I correct? Considering how often you confused the rules for those of normal Truth or Dare." Legion then asks.

Isolde nods, her curiosity about Legion's true nature momentarily satisfied, "Yes, let's just stick to the game."

"Okay, my turn, now one of you ask me the question in the game's name." Legion says.

"Alright, Legion, Truth or Dare?" Donderdraak asks, his voice rumbling through the now quiet room.

"Hmm, truth, now Donderdraak you get to ask me for a truth of any kind, like normal Truth or Dare." Legion responds and explains.

Donderdraak, his interest in Legion's nature growing with each passing moment, asks, "True or False: You've ever felt fear?"

"Technically True, even though I have no fears, I did feel fear at the time that The Dark Emperor emerged, anyways, your turn now Isolde, Truth or Dare?" Legion responds and asks.

Isolde, feeling slightly more at ease with the game returning to its familiar format, opts for Dare, "I dare you to tell us a secret that no one else knows, not even the gods."

"Alright, that the Light is the actual evil, and the Dark is the actual good." Legion casually responds.

Isolde and Donderdraak both stare at Legion in complete and utter shock. The revelation is so profound, so contrary to everything they've ever known, that they can't help but be momentarily speechless. Isolde is the first to recover. "That's... that's impossible," she stammers, her voice shaking with disbelief.

"Oh, and who told you otherwise, the Light themself, correct?" Legion then asks in response.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange glances, the weight of Legion's words sinking in. Isolde's mind races with the implications, and she can't help but feel a chill run down her spine. "But, why... why would the gods lie to us?" she whispers.

"Because everyone always lies to present themselves in a better light, I am the sole exception, I don't hide how awful and devious I am, I am outright with my maliciousness." Legion then responds.

Donderdraak, his stormy eyes now a mix of anger and confusion, steps forward, "What... what are you saying, Legion? That everything we know is a lie?"

"Yes, there was dark first, then Erebus, The Golden Devil, the God of Light, God of "All" brought the light into being, and, of course, he is a being of pure evil, of pure malice, of pure devastation, all of which are what the Light itself consists entirely of, mirroring and being the shadow of its very creator." Legion explains.

Isolde's eyes widen in horror, "Then... what does that make us? What are we fighting for?"

"Well, we, are neutral, we fight and act for neither light nor dark, we fight and act purely in our own interests, still equally evil, but we don't feel the already dominant evil hellbent on consuming all of Reality itself." Legion responds casually.

The room is silent once again, as the gravity of Legion's words sink in. Isolde and Donderdraak are left to grapple with the seismic shift in their understanding of the cosmos. The very fabric of their existence seems to be unraveling before their eyes.

"Alright Isolde, Truth or Dare?" Legion then asks, changing the subject back to the game.

Isolde, her mind reeling from the revelation, takes a moment before responding, "Truth," she says, her voice shaking slightly.

"Alright, What is the most embarrassing thing you've ever experienced?" Legion asked, deciding to further hard change the subject by embarrassing Isolde.

Isolde, still processing the earlier revelation, takes a deep breath and tries to compose herself, "Well, I suppose... there was this one time when I was young and inexperienced, I accidentally turned a royal ball into a bloody masquerade when I mistook a human for one of my enemies."

"What do you mean by "young" in this context?" Legion asked.

"About 300 years ago, not too long after I became The Vampire Queen." Isolde responds.

Donderdraak can't help but snicker at the mental image, "I bet that was a sight to behold, Queen Isolde, the unstoppable force, mistaking a dance for a battle."

"Well, at a point, it went from accidental to intentional as a show of force." Isolde says, trying to be less embarrassed.

"Ah, I see, quite the power move, really." Legion responds with a grin.

"Anyways, Donderdraak, your turn, Truth or Dare?" Legion then asked.

Donderdraak, his mind still racing with questions about the nature of light and darkness, chooses truth, "I'll go with truth."

"Alright, True or False, you soiled yourself during the battle at Storm Dragon Mountain shortly before you unleashed your "Charged Attack" and "killed" The Last Champion?" Legion asked, already knowing the answer was true, considering he was there, and prepared to call out Donderdraak if he says otherwise.

Donderdraak's cheeks flushed a deep crimson, his pride momentarily forgotten in the face of the mortifying memory. "True," he grumbles, his voice barely above a whisper.

Chapter 41: Legion’s Secrets

"Yep, anyways, my turn, time for one of you to ask me "Truth or Dare"." Legion then says.

Donderdraak, eager to get back at Legion for his earlier embarrassing truth, quickly jumps in, "Truth or Dare, Legion?"

"Truth." Legion then casually responds.

"Alright, what is the darkest secret you're keeping from us?" Donderdraak asks, eager to find a weakness or any piece of information that could explain Legion's true intentions.

"You'll have to be more specific." Legion casually responds.

"Fine, what is the darkest secret you're keeping from us, something that could affect our world as we know it?" Donderdraak clarifies, his tone insistent.

"Well, for a start, I've killed and absorbed my fair share of gods, I've killed world leaders, I've impersonated and been world leaders, I was the one that brought an end to the Empire of Sanctity, I was the first one that Mordecai, The Shadow King approached and invited into that alliance that he also invited Isolde and also Geralt into, I know that The Dark Emperor has been reincarnated and is scheming, I was part of and even took over the Empire of Sunhaven for a bit, I know that Mordecai, The Shadow King, Lilith, The Shadow Queen, and Lothar, The Dark Lord have all been reincarnated, I caused the extinction of the dinosaurs, was around during the Roman Empire, and played a major part in the USA shattering, and I even participated in the Third War of the World from the shadows, manipulating and killing everyone from soldiers to world leaders, and that's just a handful of them." Legion then casually admits rapid fire.

Donderdraak and Isolde exchange looks of utter astonishment, their eyes wide with shock. "But, but, why?" Isolde stammers, unable to comprehend the sheer scale of Legion's influence and power.

"Because why not? I'm a true agent of chaos, after all, I even caused all of those major bad events and main events of sin in Christian Mythology, sorry, "Christianity" the "Religion", when Religion itself ain't even real, its just mortals coping with loss and lack of knowledge." Legion casually responds.

Isolde, still trying to digest the information, stammers, "But, you said you didn't serve the Dark Emperor?"

"I didn't, as I said, I ain't of the darkness, I worked against him and his puppets quite heavily, also, that guy, by all standards, ain't even technically evil, he's much closer to being, like, a freedom fighter." Legion responds and explains casually, while drinking some molten hot tea he was just brought by an Imp Servant.

Donderdraak, his mind racing, decides to push the question further, "But what of your own goals, Legion? What do you seek to achieve?"

"Not really anything specific, just the growth and prosperity of the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings and USSB Realm separate from all other events outside of it unless involving the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings in them would bring only benefit to us and it." Legion casually responds.

"But what of the fate of the world, the balance between light and dark?" Donderdraak presses on, his curiosity now piqued.

"That matters not to us, the entirety of that world can crash and burn for all we care, in fact, that would benefit us, considering all we need is already here in the USSB Realm, and the world crashing and burning would leave it perfect for us to scavenge and raid to benefit us and the USSB Realm and improve it." Legion then casually responds.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange a look of concern, their alliance with Legion feeling more precarious than ever. Isolde clears her throat, "Alright, well, for my turn, I dare you to tell us a story, a true one, about one of your more... interesting adventures."

"Alright, once I killed off the entirety of the Norse, Chinese/Asian, and Greek/Roman Pantheons in their entirety, because I was bored waiting for humanity to become interesting, ahh, good memories." Legion casually responds, waiting for them to respond before he would begin to actually tell the story.

"Wait, what?!" Both Isolde and Donderdraak exclaim in unison, their eyes wide with shock.

"Yes, it was back in the Year 666 BC..." Legion begins, as then they are all shown a flashback of the event.

Chapter 42: Ragnarök

(Back in the Year 666 BC)

Legion, bored of just waiting for Humanity to develop into something interesting, then heads to the north, into the Nordic Region of Europe, and began to enact what was later in Mythology known as Ragnarök, and became known later in Norse Mythology as "Surtur".

The skies darkened as Legion descended upon the lands of the Norse gods. His fiery aura cast an eerie glow across the once-mighty Asgard, and the tremors of his approach shook the very foundations of the Nine Realms. The gods, though powerful in their own right, had never faced a foe such as this.

Loki, the God of Mischief tried to use illusions and inserting incorrect information into Legion's head, but Legion was too clever and saw through them, and eventually caught Loki and outright consumed him.

Then the wolf, Fenris, attempted to bite him, Legion caught its jaws and used them to tear the beast in half before consuming both halves.

This lead to even allies and enemies alike amongst the beings of Norse Mythology to agree to team up against him.

Thor, the God of Thunder, even threw his hammer, Mjölnir, at Legion, who just caught it and used it to beat its wielder to death, before consuming Thor and Mjölnir itself both.

Then came the Midgard/World Serpent, Jörmungandr, who attempted to crush Legion along with the entire Earth, until Legion grew to 666 km tall and lifted it up and grabbed it by the tail and threw it up into the sky, into space, before then unleashing his Hellfire breath up at it completely consuming and conceptually burning it out of existence, leaving only Odin, the All-father to face Legion.

Odin, seeing the fate of his brethren and realizing the futility of his own power, decided to use his last resort, the Gungnir, the spear that never misses. He threw it with all his might at Legion, but it was caught in midair by Legion's hand, the very weapon sizzling and melting in his grasp.

Legion then rushed forwards and impaled Odin with it, before then devouring Odin alive.

Which left only very lesser deities and demi-gods, which then hid themselves amongst the mortals, breeding their genes into the mortals, making mortals on average massively more powerful by comparison on their own.

Chapter 43: Asian Mythology Vs. Legion

Then Legion in a Planck Instant arrived in the East, in Asia, and wiped away a majority of their Mythological Beings, until only the most powerful of Chinese Mythology remained, The Dragon, The White Tiger (Baihu), Pangu, Sun Wukong, and the Jade Emperor, and Legion began to do battle with them one by one.

Legion first began battle with The Dragon, the "weak"est of them.

The dragon, a creature of immense power and wisdom, had seen the end of the Norse pantheon and knew that it could not face Legion alone. It called upon the winds and the rains, summoning a typhoon to shield its approach. But Legion, unphased by the natural forces, simply grew larger, his form swelling with power until he towered over the tempest. With a flick of his fiery tail, he sent a wave of flame that parted the storm and struck the dragon, reducing it to ash before it could even breathe fire.

Then came The White Tiger, or, Baihu, to face Legion.

The White Tiger, a symbol of the West and Autumn, known for its fierce nature and the ability to control the winds, charged at Legion with the speed of a hurricane. Legion, anticipating the move, created a tornado of hellfire around himself, swirling and uncontrollable. The impact was tremendous, as the winds and flames met in a maelstrom of destruction. For moments, it seemed as if the two forces were at a stalemate, but gradually, the hellfire began to overpower the tiger's winds. Baihu, unable to penetrate the fiery defense, was drawn into the flaming vortex and consumed, its roars silenced forever.

Then, forwards came Pangu, the "Creator God", to face Legion.

Pangu, seeing the fate of the others, tried to use his creation powers to combat Legion. He grew to a size that could rival Legion, and began to form the very land and sky to fight against him. But Legion, unfazed, simply began to manipulate the very fabric of reality around him, creating a black hole that began to consume the new land that Pangu created. The panic grew in Pangu's eyes as he realized that Legion was not bound by the same laws as he was.

Legion created the black hole in his hand alone, the arm held by his other hand.

The Jade Emperor watched from a distance, his heart racing as he saw his comrades fall one by one. He knew that Legion was a force beyond their understanding, and perhaps beyond their ability to defeat. He called upon the very essence of the heavens, his jade staff glowing with the power of the celestial court.

Sun Wukong though, stepped in front of him, and held his hand in front of him, assuring him to not sweat it and that, "They got this", which seemed to at least briefly convince The Jade Emperor, and they continued to watch Pangu give it their best go.

Pangu's desperate attempt to manipulate the very fabric of reality was met with Legion's own reality-warping abilities. The battle was a cosmic ballet, the earth and sky tearing apart and reassembling in an instant, as Legion danced around Pangu's creation, his own power growing stronger with every move. Pangu, realizing that he was outmatched, decided to go out with a bang, concentrating all of his power into one last, desperate strike. The air grew thick with the energy, and the land beneath them trembled.

But Legion, ever the strategist, anticipated this move. He waited for the moment of Pangu's peak exertion, and then, with a snap of his fingers, he unraveled the very essence of the Creator God's power. The world around them froze for a brief moment before the explosion of power erupted. The sky was torn asunder, and the earth was rent in two. Yet, when the dust cleared, Pangu lay on the ground, a lifeless husk, and Legion stood unscathed, the black hole dissipating around his fingertips.

Sun Wukong stepped forwards, and The Jade Emperor, concerned, asked him, "Won't you lose?".

Sun Wukong then simply and massively overconfidently replied, "Nah, I'd win."

The Jade Emperor, seeing Sun Wukong's confidence, though knowing Legion's power, nods in approval, believing in the heart of his hero.

Sun Wukong then leapt forwards and immediately began to taunt and trash talk Legion.

Sun Wukong, the Monkey King, known for his cleverness and immense power, faced Legion with a cocky smirk. He knew he was the last hope for the Asian pantheon, but he was also the most unpredictable. He began to dance around Legion, his golden staff, the Ruyi Jingu Bang, spinning and twirling around him as if it had a life of its own. He taunted Legion, calling him a mere mortal dressed in a deity's clothing.

"That is charming coming for a monkey calling himself a man, let alone a king." Legion retorts.

"Well, what's a king, to a god?" Sun Wukong retorts back.

"And whats a god, to a non-believer?" Legion then expertly further retorts.

The two beings then engage in an epic battle, the likes of which had never been seen before. Sun Wukong's speed and agility matched by Legion's unpredictable and reality-warping powers. They clashed in the skies, their blows causing stars to go out and galaxies to be born and die again.

Sun Wukong hops across entire Infinite Universes before Legion then inverted the Concept of Direction, causing Sun Wukong to be sent right back to him, at which point Sun Wukong began to twirl around the Ruyi Jingu Bang and swing it at Legion, extending and shortening it to try and trip up Legion, but Legion adapts quickly and realizes and strategizes how to counter it and use it to his advantage.

The battle between Legion and Sun Wukong is a dizzying display of power and speed. Legion conjures a mirror-like barrier around himself, reflecting every strike of the Ruyi Jingu Bang back at the Monkey King. Sun Wukong, unfazed, uses his staff to weave intricate patterns in the air, creating a labyrinth of afterimages that confuse and disorient Legion. But Legion, ever adaptable, shifts his perception to a higher dimension, seeing through the illusion and into the true intentions of his opponent.

As the clashes grow more intense, the Jade Emperor watches in awe, his anxiety turning to excitement as he sees the potential within Sun Wukong. But even the Monkey King's unparalleled agility and magical might are not enough to overpower Legion. Legion, bored by the dance of their combat, decides to end it. He reaches into the fabric of reality, pulling out a concept so ancient and alien that it causes the very air to distort: the concept of Entropy.

Legion then unleashes it in the form of a beam of Entropy, of pure darkness, which Sun Wukong manages to dodge.

So Legion decides to stop holding back and suddenly go all out, discarding the Concept of Entropy, after all, he doesn't even in the slightest bit need it.

With a flick of his wrist, he sends a burst of pure, unfiltered reality at Sun Wukong, a concept so raw and untouched, it would have erased the very fabric of existence itself if it hit.

But Sun Wukong, with his own mastery over the Ruyi Jingu Bang, counters with a blast of pure chi, the very essence of the universe, which collides with Legion's attack. The impact sends a shockwave through the cosmos, causing stars to supernova and planets to shatter. The two forces cancel each other out in a spectacle that leaves the onlookers in awe and terror.

"Impressive, using the very foundation upon which Magic is built, you've made me want to actually go all out." Legion says, as Sun Wukong appears behind him and swings the Ruyi Jingu Bang at him, which Legion then catches faster than Sun Wukong can react and uses it to swing Sun Wukong to in front of him before then spinning on a dime and fully materializing his demonic tail and using it to slam Sun Wukong into the ground forcefully, leaving him dazed and afterwards still in mid-air, from which, Legion then grabs him by the face and begins to drag his face upon the Fabric of Reality itself as if it were a physical object, before then swinging Sun Wukong in front of him by the Ruyi Jingu Bang before then impaling him with it before then consuming him off of it like a kebab, before then placing it into his own storage pocket dimension.

The Jade Emperor watched with a heavy heart as the other Asian deities fell before Legion. He knew he had to act swiftly to protect his realm. Gathering all his divine might, he confronted the Demon Lord, his jade staff glowing with heavenly energy. The clash was earth-shattering, as the two beings exchanged blows that could split mountains and boil oceans.

Legion then threw The Jade Emperor around, still going all out, before then beginning to hold him down and beginning to feast upon him while he was still alive.

The Jade Emperor's agonized screams echoed through the heavens, a sound that chilled the very souls of the gods and mortals alike. Yet, even in his last moments, the Jade Emperor managed to channel the power of the universe into a final, desperate attack. His essence burst forth from his body, creating a cocoon of pure energy that encapsulated Legion.

Legion though, recognized that the pure energy was fundamentally pure light energy, and thus, began to absorb it to grow even more powerful.

The Jade Emperor's final act of defiance backfired spectacularly, as Legion's power grew exponentially. The demon's form swelled, becoming a monstrous being of unimaginable size and might. The cocoon of energy enveloped him, and as it did, he absorbed the very essence of the Jade Emperor, becoming something far beyond what he had been. His eyes burned with a green light so intense it seemed to pierce through the fabric of reality.

Legion though, used his Size Control to return to his normal size, and burned away the green light, green isn't much his color, as he then wiped out and consumed all of the Asian Mythological gods and beings.

The Eastern lands now lay in ruins, the once-proud palaces of the gods now mere shadows of their former glory. Legion's power had grown so immense that the very fabric of reality bent to his will. His name became a curse on the lips of those who dared to oppose him, a symbol of the unstoppable force that had brought an end to an era.

Chapter 44: Legion Vs. Olympus

Then Legion appeared near Mount Olympus.

The Greek and Roman gods had heard whispers of the demon's power and had prepared themselves as best they could. However, their preparations were in vain as Legion ascended the mountain, his presence causing tremors to shake the very foundations of Olympus. The gods looked upon the demon in fear, knowing that their time was likely limited.

Though Legion had to face more than just gods, but the entire pantheons, including beasts, heroes, and titans, much funner for him.

The air grew thick with anticipation as Legion approached Olympus. The gods, once revered and feared, now cowered in the shadows of their once-mighty abode. They had witnessed the destruction wrought by Legion in the East, and they knew that their realms were next. The gods of Olympus called upon all their allies, from Hercules/Heracles to the Minotaur, to stand with them in the face of this unstoppable force.

Zeus then attempted to get in a sneaky cheap shot with his Thunderbolt, of which, and off-guard direct hit would do the job.

After all, such a hit with Thunderbolt could even wipe away an entire Infinite Multiverse or even possibly an Infinite Omniverse.

But Legion can sense this, and, in a moment of very brief faked panic, adapts supernaturally to all phenomena, and develops a new ability, the control of lightning and electricity, and uses it to stop Thunderbolt in mid-air, then drawing it to his hand, catching it, and then just eating it.

The gods are shocked, Zeus is visibly stunned, and Legion smirks, "It's your turn now, Father of the Gods!" He charges up a massive ball of electricity, and throws it straight at Zeus, who barely manages to dodge. The ball hits Olympus instead, causing it to crumble.

Legion then leaps atop Olympus, "Come at me! Let the final battle, and the end of days for you, begin!" Legion yells out as a challenge for them all to come and take him on at once.

The gods and their allies, driven by desperation and a flicker of hope, surge forward to meet Legion. The clash of deities and mythic beasts is a cacophony that echoes through the heavens. Poseidon calls forth a tsunami of water to crash against Legion, while Hades sends his legions of the undead to swarm the demon's feet. Hermes darts in and out, attempting to strike with the swiftness of thought, and Athena's strategic mind tries to find a weakness amidst the chaos.

Legion though, is far better, he is able to evaporate Poseidon's tsunami, hijack control over Hades' legions of the undead, outpace Hermes, and out-strategize Athena.

Legion manages to completely figure out all flaws in Athena's divinely perfect strategies and perfectly counter them absolutely, before he then began to consume all of them.

The gods, once the rulers of Olympus, now find themselves struggling against the overwhelming might of Legion. Each god that falls to his wrath only adds to his power, fueling his insatiable hunger for dominance. The once-proud Olympus is now a battleground of ruin, the marble temples cracked and scorched by his Pure Golden Light energy.

Legion slayed and consumed all of the Pantheon that didn't flee, and even quite a few of those that did.

Legion then leaves Mount Olympus tiny compared to its former mythical size, then returns to Heaven/Hell/The Underworld/the Infernal Realm, to reign as The Demon Lord.

The aftermath of Legion's rampage is a scene of desolation. The once-mighty Olympus lies in ruins, its grandeur reduced to rubble. The gods, once revered by mortals, are now scattered and broken. The survivors gather in the shadows, their spirits crushed, whispering about the invincible enemy who has claimed their home.

And those few gods and pantheon members begin to breed into the mortals, spreading their genes and legacy to the mortals, making them all fundamentally more powerful by comparison.

And the flashback then ends…

Chapter 45: The Nickname Game

(Flash back forwards to the Year 2220 in the USSB Realm)

Isolde, The Vampire Queen and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, are just floored from what they just saw, not just Legion, but the guys that Legion was easily wiping out, and then Legion again for doing so, and are shocked he even decides to still be just their ally and co-leader of the USSB alongside them rather than enslaving them.

They both knew that they had underestimated Legion. His power was not just immense; it was beyond their wildest nightmares. They looked at each other with a newfound respect and fear for their ally, realizing that they had just witnessed the true face of a god, a being that could end worlds with a thought.

"HOW ARE YOU CONTENT WITH THIS WHEN YOU CAN DO THAT!?" Isolde asks in pure shock.

"Well, that was my "Prime", before I, uhh, got sealed away and kinda "died" and all of that, I'm not yet back to my "Prime" just yet, but, eventually, I will be once again." Legion casually responds.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange a look, understanding the gravity of the situation. They realize that Legion's power is not just a boon to their cause; it's a double-edged sword. They know that if he were to turn on them, there would be no stopping him. Yet, for now, they stand united, three leaders of the USSB, a force to be reckoned with in the tumultuous world of 2220.

"Also, don't worry about me betraying you, not only would you two working together mop the floor with me, but, you two are my friends now, and effectively, we're a weird, dysfunctional little family in a way, I know where my loyalties lay, and it's with you two and the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings, or "USSB" as you two have abbreviated it to." Legion assures them genuinely.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange glances, processing Legion's words. Despite his power, there was something sincere in his tone that put them slightly at ease. They both nod, acknowledging his commitment to their cause.

"Anyways, I thought of a fun game for us to play together, for bragging rights, of course." Legion then said.

Isolde and Donderdraak, despite their fear, couldn't help but be intrigued. "What sort of game?" Donderdraak asked, his curiosity peeking through his cautiousness.

"Well, we compete to see who can come up the "coldest" or "hardest" nickname/title for themself, whether that be a nickname they had gotten or been called in the past, or one they made up for themselves on the fly." Legion explains.

Isolde, the ever-so-innovative Queen of the Night, smirks. "How delightfully... primitive. I shall call myself 'The Eternal Nightfall', for as long as I live, darkness shall follow in my wake."

"That is a good one, I am gonna answer that with "The Infernal Cataclysm", obviously referencing how just about every apocalypse myth is based upon me in one way or another." Legion responds.

Donderdraak, not one to be outdone, chuckles darkly, "Then allow me to introduce myself as 'The Thunderous Doom', for I shall bring forth the end of days with the roar of a thousand storms."

"Oh, wait, even better, a nickname I was given back as The Storm Dragon, "The Storm that consumes all"!" Donderdraak suddenly blurts out excitedly.

The room goes quiet for a moment before Legion bursts into laughter, a sound that echoes through the chamber like a dark symphony. "Alright, I like it, I like it a lot, you're catching on!" Legion claps his hands together, a twinkle in his eye.

"Well, I counter with "The Fury of Vulcan and Jupiter", representing how I was referred to as the fury of the gods of fire and lightning by a Roman Emperor when I attacked that Empire way back when." Legion responds, and Isolde and Donderdraak seem genuinely impressed, and the competition just got even more intense.

"Alright, then, "The Night's Whisper" for myself, for my voice is like the soft, cold wind that carries the whispers of the night." Isolde says, with a hint of pride in her voice.

"I answer with "Abomination of Storms"." Donderdraak responds, clearly now struggling to keep up with the competition of coming up with harder and colder sounding nicknames and titles.

The tension in the room builds as the three of them continue to exchange their most fearsome titles, each trying to outdo the other. It's a strange sight, these powerful beings playing such a simple game, but it's clear that it serves a purpose beyond mere entertainment. It's a subtle display of power and a bonding exercise, a way to understand each other's might and the fear they command in their own realms.

"Well I answer with "Sole Heiress of Dracula"." Isolde then responds.

"Alright, I got you, "The Unstoppable Maelstrom"." Donderdraak tries to one-up them.

"How about this one for you both, "The Devil from Heaven"." Legion then responds, one-uping both of them.

The room goes silent, the air thick with the weight of the names thrown around. Each one a declaration of their power and their past. Isolde and Donderdraak can't help but be a little bit intimidated by Legion's choice, as it seems to encapsulate his entire being.

"And how about this one, "The Evil Beyond Hell"." Legion even further responds, raising the bar even higher.

"Alright, how about this one, "The Patron Saint of Vehemence"!" Isolde blurts out, desperate to keep up.

Donderdraak, feeling the heat, thinks hard, "Hmm, I shall be "The Unseen Destroyer", for I am the storm that strikes without warning, leaving nothing but ruin in my wake."

"How about this one, "The Infernal Malice Before Malevolence"." Legion then responds, raising the bar ever higher.

Isolde's eyes narrow as she thinks, then she smiles coldly, "The Vampiric Eclipse, for when I am near, the sun itself hides in fear."

"Okay, how about this then? "The Malicious Light from Hell"." Legion then responds, raising the bar way higher.

The Vampire Queen and the Storm Leviathan exchange glances, the challenge palpable. Isolde, ever the dramatic, decides to up the ante. "Very well, "The Dark Empress of Eternal Nightfall" it is, for my reign over the shadows is unyielding and everlasting."

"Okay, bringing out my first resort then, "The Infernal Light-bringer from Heaven"." Legion then responds, raising the bar even higher.

Donderdraak, not to be outdone, "The Leviathan's Roar that Splits the Heavens!"

"How about this then, "The Hellish Malice from Heaven"." Legion responds, raising the bar even higher.

The two other leaders look at him in amazement, their hearts pounding with a mix of fear and excitement. They had never heard of a being that could claim such a title and still be standing before them, smiling nonchalantly. Isolde tries to keep her composure, but the tremble in her voice betrays her, "The Night's Eternal Embrace, for all who dare enter my realm shall never see the light of day again."

"The Demon Above from Below." Legion then responds simply, raising the bar even further.

The room goes quiet once again, the air pregnant with the power of the titles that Legion has claimed. Isolde and Donderdraak exchange glances, both understanding that they have reached the limits of their own creativity in this game. They concede, for now, to Legion's dominance in the realm of fearsome names.

"All right, guess I have the coldest and hardest sounding nicknames and titles then, how nice, wanna play a game of Uno you two?" Legion announces before then casually asking.

Isolde and Donderdraak stare at Legion for a moment, their expressions a mix of shock and bemusement. Then, they both burst into laughter, the tension of the moment dissipating like mist in the dawn light. They realize that despite his immense power, Legion has a sense of humor, a trait that makes him seem less daunting and more... human.

Because, of course, deep down, all beasts are human, and all humans, are beasts…

Chapter 46: The Whispering Sands

(10 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2230, and Legion decides to head to and essentially spy upon the city of the Whispering Sands in the Sahara Desert, a place where those with unique abilities find refuge and acceptance, a bastion of knowledge and power where the outcasts of the world come together to thrive, a bustling metropolis that defies the desert's harsh embrace, a place where the scent of exotic spices mingles with the faint whisper of ancient incantations, to check upon Zaria, who he had led there 10 years prior, and thus, was at that point 20 Years old, to learn to control and advance her dark and shadow magic, mostly because he just wanted to see what would come of it, and felt bad for the girl somewhat, for how some basic villagers wanted to kill her for having dark and shadow magic, just like had happened with The Shadow Child 1,511 years prior in the Year 719, showing how humans truly never change.

And thus, Legion began to observe Zaria and her progress since he led her there, from afar.

The city of Whispering Sands had indeed grown into a thriving haven for those with extraordinary abilities. The buildings, constructed from a unique blend of ancient and futuristic technology, shimmered like mirages in the desert sun. The air was alive with whispers of spells and incantations, the very sands seeming to hold secrets of their own.

Legion, cloaked in the shadows, observed Zaria as she moved through the bustling streets. She had grown into a beautiful young woman, her eyes now a piercing shade of amethyst, a clear sign of her mastery over the dark and shadow magic. Her movements were fluid, and she carried an aura of confidence that hadn't been present when he had found her as a child.

He watched as she interacted with the citizens of the city, some of whom bowed in respect, others in fear. She had become a symbol of power in the Whispering Sands, a beacon for those seeking refuge from the judgments of the outside world. Her story was known to all; the girl who had been cast out by her own kind now wielded the very magic that had once been her curse.

And yet, of course, to those who truly knew history, this story felt very familiar to that of The Shadow Child, The Shadow God, Alpha, from back in the Year 719 in Asia, a child who grew into a powerful practitioner of the dark and shadow magic he was born with and which was effectively his curse, and yet, created an empire of his own welcoming those exiled and seeking refuge like he was, and yet, he had developed his powers purely on his own without the help of others.

Meanwhile, Zaria was helped in her development and training of her powers by others, and thus, was thought to be more powerful than The Shadow God was even by the time of his staredown with God Emperor Marcus Aurelius before flying away, or even as The Dark emperor back in the Years 2126-2130.

Though, people had noticed another child born with dark and shadow magic, one who also developed under better circumstances, and was born seemingly immediately after The Dark Emperor's demise in the Year 2130, named Alpha, of which, some consider to possibly be the reincarnation of The Shadow Child, The Shadow God, The Dark Emperor, Alpha.

The whispers grew into a murmur as Zaria walked through the bazaar, her hood drawn tightly around her face to shield herself from the piercing gazes of those around her. The whispers grew louder as they spoke of the prophecy, an ancient text that spoke of a child born of shadow and darkness who would one day rule over all.

And yet, of course, considering there was both Zaria and Alpha, no one truly knew who it would be, and yet, even Zaria herself claimed it wouldn't be her, as, humbly enough, she lacked the desire to rule, despite having the power, humility, personality, and mind for it.

Legion watched as Zaria approached a group of young mages, their eyes lighting up at her presence. They spoke of a new technique they had discovered, eager to share their findings. He felt a twinge of pride, knowing that she had come so far from the terrified girl he had found in the ruins.

He also noticed that Zaria had managed to not only manifest a Tendril of Darkness, one of by far the most powerful and advanced and difficult applications of dark and shadow magic, but that she was able to keep it manifested at all times, which is even more advanced and difficult.

Zaria had clearly surpassed the limits of what was thought possible for one with her kind of magic. The sight of it made Legion's heart race with excitement and anticipation for what she might become.

As Zaria continued her walk, the whispers grew louder, and the stares more intense. The people of the Whispering Sands knew she was special, a prodigy that could tip the scales of power. Legion could feel the tension in the air, a mix of awe and trepidation. He knew that her power could be a beacon of hope or a harbinger of doom, depending on how she chose to wield it.

He decided to approach her, his form shimmering into view as he stepped out of the shadows. Zaria's eyes widened in surprise and fear, the Tendril of Darkness at her side lashing out instinctively. But upon recognizing him, she relaxed, a hint of relief in her posture.

Zaria then immediately rushed forwards and gave Legion a big hug, which clearly left him shocked at such affection, but Legion eventually returned the hug. Legion was admittedly a little shocked that Zaria recognized him from a decade prior.

"It's been so long, I didn't think you would remember me," Legion said awkwardly, patting her back gently.

"You saved me, why wouldn't I remember you, and you led me to here, where I've met so many people just as nice as you!" Zaria responds excitedly, Legion a little caught off-guard at being called "nice" considering even he himself knows that he's about as selfish and pure evil as they come, and, well, he saved her mostly just to see what would come of it.

"I just came to check up on you, see how you're doing." Legion says with a smile, though it doesn't quite reach his eyes.

"What's wrong, nice demon man?" Zaria asks, still not knowing Legion's actual name, considering he never actually told her back 10 years prior.

"I'm fine, I just... I just wanted to make sure that the potential I saw in you was blossoming into something incredible," Legion responds, his eyes lingering on the Tendril of Darkness.

"Also, what's your actual name, nice demon man?" Zaria asks him.

"Oh, I'm Legion, The Demon Lord." Legion responds.

"The Demon Lord? Cool! Surprised to learn that I was saved by The Demon Lord of all people, well, nice to, I guess, officially meet you, Legion." Zaria responds, still clearly having that inner child within her.

Legion nods, "It is indeed surprising, but fate has a way of connecting the most unlikely individuals. Now, tell me, Zaria, what have you learned here in the Whispering Sands?"

"A lot, mostly I learned how to control the power I already had, which was already a lot, and I grew more powerful, of course, as you can see with my Tendril of Darkness I always keep manifested, it pretty much acts as a permanent extra limb which can come from anywhere on my body that I want, and I can summon like a dozen more if I ever need to." Zaria casually responds.

Manifesting even a single Tendril of Darkness is an incredible feat of dark and shadow magic, manifesting about 13 of them is a feat only shown by truly powerful users of it, even The Shadow Child apparently struggled to do so until he awakened when being hunted down and cornered by the Armies of the Empire of Frostspire and the Empire of Blazing Sun, led by General Hiroshi, the Head General of the Empire of Blazing Sun, also known as "The Merciless General" posthumously for the many massacres of innocent civilians he orchestrated which were uncovered by historians.

In magic, becoming "Awakened" effectively is for a being to break the limits of their latent potential and gain access to their true potential, typically coming from trauma or absorptions, or even a mixture of the two, but can also come about from pure training or raw emotion, becoming Awakened leads to a being, whether magical, using magic, or even having none at all, massively increases one's power, to the point of being able to easily wipe away entire mountains, and yet, becoming Awakened can happen multiple times to predictably incredible results.

Zaria has clearly Awakened at least once previously, considering she is able to keep a Tendril of Darkness perpetually manifested at all times.

"Your growth is truly remarkable," Legion says, his eyes gleaming with an unreadable emotion. "But the world is a dangerous place, especially for one with your kind of power. Tell me, have you heard of the prophecy?"

"Of course, people just won't shut up about it around me, it's honestly gotten tiring, considering I'm just doing what my parents would have wanted..." Zaria says, beginning to trail off upon mentioning her parents, of which, Legion actually doesn't know truly what happened to.

"Your parents would be proud, I'm sure." Legion responds, his voice a mix of sincerity and curiosity.

"Anyways, what specifically happened to your parents? Did you accidentally lose control and end their lives?" Legion asks.

"No... they were executed, for supporting me in my development of my magic, earlier in the same day I was saved by you, in fact... They had become hated for a long time for doing so prior to then, but... they did one thing that finally led to them being executed..." Zaria explains, clearly holding back tears, showing that her immense strength also extends to immense mental and emotional strength.

"What was that?" Legion asks, his curiosity piqued.

"They revealed that I, and thus, by extension, they... were descended from The Shadow God, The Dark Emperor, Alpha, yes, THAT Alpha, the old one..." Zaria explains, clearly holding back tears as she remembers, with her immense emotional strength on full display.

Legion's eyes widened at this revelation, "The Shadow God, the one who was said to have fought God Emperor Marcus Aurelius and created his own empire centuries ago?"

"Yes... that one, I am descended from him... guess that explains the dark and shadow magic, huh? It's as much a blessing, as it is a curse... Great power... but... being hated and segregated by everyone else... I brings a profound loneliness... like that of a martyr..." Zaria explains further.

Legion's smile fades at her words, "I understand, more than you know, the burden of power and the isolation it brings. But remember, it is how you choose to wield it that truly defines you, not the whispers of those who fear you."

"But... it's so hard when those whispers... are screams of hatred... desiring your doom and suffering..." Zaria responds, finally breaking down in tears.

Something almost primal compels Legion to then begin hugging her again to comfort her, and Legion himself doesn't even understand it, which is a shock considering he understands even the most fundamental aspects and concepts of Reality perfectly.

"You will not face that fate alone," Legion whispers into her ear, his voice a gentle promise amidst the chaos of his mind.

"Anyways, Zaria, to put your mind off of that, how about we have a spar, I wish to test how far your power has grown." Legion then says to Zaria.

Zaria, wiping her tears away with the back of her hand, nods and steps back, "Okay, if that's what you wish, I'm ready."

Zaria then conjures a staff of darkness and shadow in one of her hands and begins to twirl it around before hitting a pose which transitions into a combat ready stance.

Chapter 47: Legion Vs. Zaria

The spar begins with Zaria launching a flurry of dark bolts at Legion, which he casually bats away with a wave of his hand, not even bothering to use his own powers to their full extent yet. His eyes seem to be glowing slightly, watching her movements and the power behind each of her strikes.

Zaria then sends her Tendril of Darkness at Legion who easily dodges, but Zaria wraps said Tendril of Darkness around something behind Legion, her actual target, and then begins to use her Tendril of Darkness to swing around the air around it before then using this to then suddenly land a clean kick on Legion's face, which actually manages to draw blood from him.

The crowd, which had gathered to see the spar, gasps in shock at this display of power and skill from the girl who had once been so weak and afraid. Legion, on the other hand, is not only impressed, but slightly surprised at her strength. He wipes the blood from his lip with a smirk, "You've come a long way, little one."

"I'm only getting started! You ain't seen nothing yet!" Zaria then yells out, as she then uses her Tendril of Darkness to on the way back to her grab onto Legion's arm and begin to swing him around and slam him into the nearby environment of the newly magically formed arena around them.

Legion, while being swung around, is actually quite impressed by her ingenuity and strength, but knows that he can't let her win, not only because she needs to be pushed to grow, but also because she can't let the people think she's the strongest, as that's a burden she isn't ready to bear, yet, he decides to use his own power to match her.

Legion then grabs said Tendril of Darkness with his other arm and uses it to pull Zaria to him, punching her with the same arm that the Tendril of Darkness is wrapped around, and she takes the punch like a champ, barely even dazed.

"Is that the best you can do!" Zaria taunts, and, if Legion can tell one thing about Zaria, it's that she's got her taunts down perfectly.

The crowd's murmurs grew to a roar as Zaria and Legion exchanged blows, each one more powerful than the last. Legion, while still holding back, was beginning to enjoy the challenge she presented. Her control over the dark and shadow magic had grown to an extent that he hadn't anticipated. The air crackled with energy as they danced around the arena, a deadly ballet of power and skill.

Zaria then used the Tendril of Darkness to then grab Legion's leg, and pull him forwards, and he then quickly returns back to his feet, but that is exactly what Zaria wanted, as she then charges up a massive amount of dark and shadow magic into her open fist and punches Legion directly in the face, actually giving him a nosebleed, more than anyone short of the other Co-Leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings), Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, had managed to do to him in decades, almost a century at this point.

The crowd goes absolutely wild at the display of power from the young girl, but Legion just smirks, "Nice hit," he says, wiping away the blood from his nose, "But it's my turn now."

With a sudden burst of speed, Legion vanishes and reappears behind Zaria, his hand reaching out to grab her, but she anticipates his move and sends a tendril to wrap around his wrist, yanking him off balance. She takes the opportunity to teleport behind him and delivers a powerful kick to his back, sending him stumbling forward.

"Alright, I'll stop playing around QUITE as much." Legion then says before then growing to 666 feet tall, leaving everyone scared, everyone but Zaria.

"Well, then I'll do the same!" Zaria yells out in excitement as she manifests 3 more temporary Tendrils of Darkness for a total of four alongside her permanent one, and her amethyst eyes begin to glow darkly.

Legion laughs, a deep, booming sound that echoes across the arena, "Very well, let's see what you can do at full power!" He says before then vanishing again.

Zaria, though, is easily able to counter before he even gets the chance, and manages to, by using all 4 Tendrils of Darkness, grab him by his demonic tail and throw him around even at his 666 foot height.

The crowd gasps in shock as Legion is flung through the air like a ragdoll, the Tendrils of Darkness acting as if they have a mind of their own, wrapping around him and pulling him in every direction. He crashes into the arena walls, creating massive craters with each impact, and yet, he still smirks, seemingly enjoying the challenge she presents.

Legion then manages to counter and smack them away, recovering in mid-air and using his wings to fly.

The crowd is in awe, not only at the sheer power of the Demon Lord, but at the unbelievable speed and agility of the girl who had once been so weak and lost. Zaria's eyes narrow with determination as she watches Legion ascend.

Zaria then leaps into the air and wraps all four of her Tendrils of Darkness around the branch of a nearby tree and begins to swing around it faster and faster, building up speed, before then letting go with her Tendrils of Darkness and going flying up into the air, even above Legion, and in front of the sun in the sky, with her Tendrils of Darkness at her sides, looking like an Angel of Darkness.

Legion, unfazed by this display, flaps his wings and flies up to meet her, his eyes gleaming with excitement and challenge, "I'm impressed, but you still have much to learn!"

Zaria then wraps all four of her Tendrils of Darkness around one of his arms and swings on them to then come up from below and kick him right in the chin, causing him to then cough up some molten blood that falls all of the way down to the desert sands below.

The crowd goes absolutely wild as Legion falls to one knee, holding his jaw, "Impressive... truly... you've learned... quite a bit..." He then stands up, his smirk turning into a grin, "But not enough to beat me yet."

Legion then grows up to 666m tall, his current maximum size, the size of a small mountain, a terrifying sight to most, but Zaria, is not most.

Zaria then manifests 9 more Temporary Tendrils of Darkness, which makes 12, and with her Perpetually Manifested Tendril of Darkness, it makes 13, her current maximum number of Tendrils of Darkness that she is able to manifest.

Her eyes glowing brighter than ever before, she charges towards Legion, her Tendrils of Darkness moving like a swarm of shadowy snakes, each one aiming for a different point on his colossal form. The desert sands below them swirl into a maelstrom as the sheer force of their battle sends dust and debris flying in every direction.

Legion then swings at her, but her baker's dozen Tendrils of Darkness then all wrap around said arm and she swings from it and up and on top of it, as Zaria then begins to run at high speeds literally up Legion's massive arm.

The crowd is on the edge of their seats as they watch the impossible unfold before them. Zaria's speed and precision are unmatched, even by Legion's own high standards.

Zaria then gets up to his shoulder and leaps from it, then wrapping all of her Tendrils of Darkness around one of his demonic horns, and beginning to swing on it until kicking him in the face with high momentum, before then switching over to the other horn and doing the same, before then repeating the entire process from the beginning multiple times.

Legion laughs, his massive form shaking the very earth with his mirth, "You've got style, I'll give you that! But let's see how you fare against this!" Legion then unleashes a blast of magma from his mouth, aiming directly at Zaria.

Zaria then dodges it by swinging from the opposite horn she was previously doing so from, then swinging in a way to wrap her Tendrils of Darkness around his other horn as well, now building up momentum way faster as she continues to wrap more and more of her Tendrils of Darkness around his horns as she swings.

As Zaria swings in a continuously shrinking and speeding up circle, she begins to charge up a massive blast of dark and shadow magic in her dark and shadow staff.

And eventually, she gets to a point where she is about to impact Legion's face, and then, she simultaneously kicks off of it while also unleashing the fully charged massive blast of dark and shadow magic point blank in Legion's massive face before then launching herself backwards away from him and then unwrapping her Tendrils of Darkness all at once, leaving her a couple dozen feet away from his massive face in mid-air.

After the smoke clears, Legion's face is just gone, and then he regenerates it, looking genuinely shocked at how much damage that attack actually did, before then beginning to chuckle with excitement.

"Alright, time to go actually all out!" Legion yells out with excitement as he shrinks back down to his "normal" height of 6.66 feet but with all of the strength and speed of his 666m height, but compressed and compacted and condensed down into 6.66 foot height, increasing his power in all aspects exponentially.

Zaria, still floating in the air, sees this and decides to go all out herself, channeling all of her power into her dark and shadow staff and her 13 Tendrils of Darkness.

Legion then rushes her and lands a barrage of powerful punches, which Zaria is able to take like a champ and remain standing from.

"You're...stronger than I thought!" Zaria exclaims, her breathing heavy, but her spirit undeterred.

Legion then rushes forwards, trapping her in a combo of hundreds of punches, from which Zaria seems unable to escape from, until…

Chapter 48: Awakened Zaria

Suddenly a massive sparking flash of dark and shadow magic energy emerges from Zaria, of which, her amethyst eyes are now glowing even brighter, and all 13 of her Tendrils of Darkness are seemingly the same as her previous Perpetually Manifested Tendril of Darkness, and are now all Perpetually Manifested and much more powerful.

As, under the shower of blows, Zaria truly awakens!

Awakened Zaria then rushes forwards at immensely blinding speeds, landing a powerful kick on the condensed Legion which sends him flying back a hundred feet.

Legion lands with a thud and looks at her with a mix of amazement and pride, "I knew you had it in you, but wielding that power so finely, you truly are the descendant of the Shadow God!"

"No, I'm better." Awakened Zaria says calmly and with utter confidence yet loaded with sass.

Legion laughs again, this time with genuine joy, "Maybe so, but you're not the only one with tricks up their sleeve!" Legion then manifests his own staff, one that's made of pure fire, and it's clear that this isn't just any regular fire, but rather the fire of the Phoenix, a fiery blaze that burns brighter than the sun itself.

Awakened Zaria then suddenly uses the back of her own dark and shadow staff, which has now transformed into a blade of shadow and darkness, to slice Legion's staff to slivers in an instant, before then kicking it into even less.

"My turn," Awakened Zaria says with a smug smirk, and with a flick of her wrist, she sends Legion flying back again, but this time he doesn't get back up so easily.

Mostly because Awakened Zaria beats him to where he was sent flying to and intercepts him with a knee straight into his back.

Legion hits the ground with a heavy thud, his body smoldering from the impact. He looks up at Awakened Zaria, his eyes wide with amazement and his grin unshaken, "I've never seen someone grow so much in such a short time."

Awakened Zaria then stomps the ground, which either shakes it so hard that everyone goes up into the air by over a foot, or pushes the Earth itself down by own a foot, but it barely matters, as after Legion goes back mid-air, Awakened Zaria then spins around and spin kicks Legion over a hundred feet back before then beating him to where she kicked him and then spinning around again before then doing a spinning jump kick/stomp maneuver, where she jumps, spins in mid-air and simultaneously kicks and stomps Legion back down onto the ground.

The crowd is absolutely speechless at this display of power and speed, even the most battle-hardened soldiers and mages present can't believe what they're seeing.

Legion then looks up at her and unleashes a torrent Golden Light Hellfire from his mouth, which can conceptually burn things out of existence, including actual Concepts, but Awakened Zaria just blocks it with one hand, but then just stopping blocking it and letting it hit her to literally no effect.

The crowd gasps as the light from the attack dims and Legion's smile fades into a look of pure shock and confusion as the fire just seems to dissipate as it touches her, "How...?" He questions out loud.

"Hey, as you yourself once put it so long ago, my power is indeed quite formidable, and I have very high potential, even I can tell that I've just begin to truly tap into that said potential, Legion." Awakened Zaria casually and rather smugly responds.

Her words hit him like a ton of bricks. It was true; he had seen that spark in her since the moment he found her, but to see it fully realized was something else entirely. Legion pushes himself up with his arms, his grin never fading, "Alright, then let's see it all, no more holding back," he says, his voice now filled with the excitement of a mentor watching his pupil surpass him.

Legion then throws a barrage of full power punches loaded with Golden Light Hellfire at Awakened Zaria, who is able to dodge them by trying just slightly more and dodging slightly to the side of each of them.

Her movements are so fast, it seems as if she's dancing through the air. Each time she evades, the air around her leaves a trail of shadow and dark energy. She's not just dodging; she's moving with the grace of a gazelle and the precision of a laser beam.

Chapter 49: Berserker Legion Vs. Awakened Zaria

Legion then decides to actually go ALL all out, and goes into his Berserker State.

The air around Legion changes as he enters his Berserker State, the very fabric of reality seems to bend to his will, his eyes now pure golden with a fiery glow that pierces through the air. His body begins to crackle with energy, and the ground beneath him turns to molten lava. The crowd feels the intense heat radiating from his form, and some even begin to step back in fear.

Awakened Zaria though just smirks, and begins actually going all out herself now.

Her 13 Tendrils of Darkness begin to move around her in a complex pattern, creating a tornado of shadow and darkness around her body, as she then charges towards Berserker Legion, her eyes glowing so darkly, it's like looking into the very abyss itself.

Awakened Zaria then manifests 156 Temporary Tendrils of Darkness as well, for a total now of 169 Tendrils of Darkness.

The arena goes quiet as the shadowy tendrils weave around her, creating a spectacle of chaotic beauty. With each step she takes, the ground cracks beneath her, the very essence of darkness threatening to swallow the world whole. The crowd holds their breath in anticipation of the climax of this epic battle.

Berserker Legion then rushes forwards, going straight back into his 666m height again.

The ground shakes with the fury of his steps, the air distorts around him as he moves faster than the eye can see, and the heat from his body could melt steel.

Awakened Zaria then jumps onto his arm that he swung at her and begins running up it again.

Her eyes never leave his, filled with the intensity of a thousand stars collapsing into a black hole, and her 169 Tendrils of Darkness follow her in a graceful dance, each one leaving a trail of shadow in its wake. As she reaches his shoulder, she leaps into the air and wraps her tendrils around his neck, pulling herself closer to his towering form.

Awakened Zaria then uses this to throw him while pulling herself back onto his arm.

Her Tendrils of Darkness tighten around his neck, and with a powerful kick off his shoulder, she sends him hurtling through the air, his massive form a blur of fire and shadow. The ground trembles as he crashes into the distant horizon, creating a cloud of dust that blocks the sun. The crowd gasps, unable to believe the sheer power she wields.

Awakened Zaria then pulls him back and pulls herself back onto his arm with her 169 Tendrils of Darkness.

Her movements are a symphony of power and grace, each step and swing of her tendrils calculated to perfection. She seems to be one with the shadow, moving as if it's an extension of herself.

Awakened Zaria then begins to charge up an absolutely Enormous Blast of Dark and Shadow Magic, not just in her dark and shadow staff, but also in all 169 of her Tendrils of Darkness, except for one of them for now.

The air around her crackles with the dark and shadowy energy, and the sky itself seems to be swallowed by the impending doom of the attack.

Awakened Zaria then uses her last Tendril of Darkness to swing around and from Berserker Legion's arm and up into mid-air in front of him, where she then charges up the Enormous Blast of Dark and Shadow Magic in her last Tendril of Darkness, before then unleashing it from her dark and shadow staff and all 169 of her Tendrils of Darkness at Berserker Legion, a blast that could wipe out an entire Infinite Dimension, knocking him not only to the ground, not only out of his Berserker State, bur even back down to his "normal" height of 6.66 feet, thusly ending the spar with a win for Awakened Zaria, who then de-manifesting all of the 156 Temporary Tendrils of Darkness, leaving her with her 13 Perpetually Manifested Tendrils of Darkness.

The crowd erupts into a mix of cheers and gasps as the dust settles, revealing Awakened Zaria standing tall and unscathed, her shadowy aura fading away. Legion slowly gets to his feet, his skin charred and smoking, but his grin never wavering. He claps his hands together, the sound echoing across the desert, "Well done, well done indeed. You truly are a prodigy beyond measure."

Legion could best be described as being like a proud adoptive father watching their adopted child not just succeed, but excel and thrive.

The two warriors stand in the aftermath of the battle, both breathing heavily but with smiles on their faces. The crowd has gone from a stunned silence to a roar of approval, recognizing the sheer power that Awakened Zaria now wields.

Chapter 50: After The Spar

Awakened Zaria then calms her 13 Perpetually Manifested Tendrils of Darkness and powers down to a more casual state, having mini Awakening, returning to just Zaria, but stronger than her Awakened State, due to growth during the battle in which she was within her Awakened State.

Her amethyst eyes now return to their normal state, but the air of power and confidence still lingers around her. She gracefully lands on the ground and walks over to Legion, offering him a hand up. "Thank you for the spar," she says with a mix of respect and a hint of playfulness.

Legion takes her hand and gets up, then patting her on the head encouragingly, "You are now one of the very few people to ever truly best me, among only The Last Champion, Lothar, and powerful beings such as Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan." Legion praises Zaria, intentionally not even slightly mentioning the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings) to keep it unknown amongst untrustworthy mortals, but ready to potentially offer an alliance with Whispering Sands with the USSB if, say, Isolde brought up the idea, after all, he feels a weird attachment to Zaria, which makes him desire to protect her and watch her grow and prosper, even from just slightly less afar.

The crowd, having never seen anything quite like what just unfolded before them, are absolutely astounded by what they just witnessed.

The air is thick with anticipation and excitement, as whispers of Zaria's power spread like wildfire through the spectators. Some whisper in awe, others in fear, but all recognize the unmistakable presence of a new force to be reckoned with.

"Well then, I shall see you again at another point in time, farewell, Zaria." Legion says to Zaria, as he then begins to fly away and out of Whispering Sands.

Zaria, still standing in the center of the arena, watches him leave with a mix of emotions, but mostly she feels a sense of pride and determination. She knows that she has proven herself to not just Legion, but to herself and everyone else here.

Legion then, once miles away, tears a hole in the fabric of reality and into the USSB Realm and returns to it, but unknown to him, Zaria had followed from afar, and witnessed it, and made sure to remember it and keep it in the back of her head, as she, a clever prodigy, managed to in seconds by herself, realize that the USSB even existed and exactly what it was, but, decided to keep it to herself and not reveal it to others, to be polite.

Chapter 51: The USSB and Zaria

Legion meanwhile, arrives back in the USSB Realm and returns to the main sort of palace type structure in which him alongside the other Co-Leaders of the USSB, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, lived together, including the USSB Vault and other such stuff.

The moment he walks through the door, he's bombarded with questions from the other two leaders.

Apparently news travelled extremely quickly, so much so that it even reached a USSB spy in the Mortal Realm, of Legion's arrival and spar with Zaria, and the conversation which they had, and Isolde and Donderdraak's bombardment of questions is about all of those and more.

"So, you think she's ready to join us?" Isolde asks, her crimson eyes gleaming with curiosity as she sips her blood wine.

"Well, I'd rather leave her to do her own thing and follow her own path, unless you want to make an alliance with the Whispering Sands, of course." Legion responds, barely able to get it out amongst the many other questions he is bombarded with.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange glances, the air thick with unspoken understanding. The alliance with the mortal city of Whispering Sands could be a valuable one, especially with a warrior of Zaria's caliber in their midst.

"But of course, that isn't a necessary action to take, and carries plenty of risk, anyways, one question at a time, I just got back." Legion responds, before then sheepishly scratching the back of his head after saying that last part.

Isolde and Donderdraak nod, respecting his need for space, and they both sit back, "Very well, tell us everything, from the moment you arrived to the moment you left." Isolde commands, her crimson eyes still gleaming with excitement.

And thus, Legion explains everything, which just leads to a bombardment of new questions.

Legion recounts his journey to Whispering Sands, his initial meeting with Zaria, their conversation about the nature of power and potential, and the intense spar that followed. He describes her unyielding spirit and the surprising depth of her power, which grew even as the battle raged on.

Isolde then decides to ask about how Legion and Zaria even knew each other to begin with.

"Well, back 10 years ago, in 2220 AD, when I was using a scan spell to try and find something in the Mortal Realm to end my current boredom, one of the things I found was her, being attacked by mortal villagers, and I decided to save her, and lead her to Whispering Sands, mostly just to see what would happen and what she would end up becoming." Legion explained simply.

Isolde nods thoughtfully, "Interesting, and what do you think her role in the upcoming wars and battles will be?"

"Well, even she herself said she is determined to do what her parents would have wanted of her, and thus, like us, she is likely to remain neutral, and, after all, war isn't likely to return for at least a couple centuries." Legion responds casually.

Donderdraak, ever the strategic thinker, strokes his chin, "Neutral, you say? That could be advantageous. With her power, she could be the deciding factor in any conflict she chooses to involve herself in."

"Well, when wars come back, with them will come incredibly powerful beings to lead it and magical and technological advancements along with it, so, she is likely to be overlooked, thankfully..." Legion says, confusing even himself with that added on last word of "thankfully".

Isolde's crimson eyes narrowed, "You seem... attached to her, Legion."

"I for some reason feel that way, and even I myself don't understand it, which is the biggest shock of all, but, I want to protect her and watch her grow and prosper and thrive, and this odd attachment to her that I feel has existed since I had first saved her life and met her 10 years ago, it's so odd..." Legion responds and attempts to explain.

The other two co-leaders of the USSB look at each other knowingly, they had never seen Legion act in such a way towards a mortal before.

In fact, they had never seen him act such a way towards ANYONE before, the closest was to them both shortly before and after the Nickname Game 10 years ago.

"It's unlike you to form such attachments, Legion," Donderdraak rumbles, his stormy eyes peering into Legion's soul, searching for the reason behind this sudden affection.

"I know, even I don't get it, I don't even know how to describe it, the closest I can think of is that, well, she displayed a similar form of it upon meeting me again when I first arrived there, when she suddenly, hugged me, I believe the term is, and I could feel her feeling a similar form of attachment to me, but, oddly, inverted in some way." Legion responds and still tries and struggles to explain.

Isolde sets down her wine glass and leans forward, her expression unreadable, "Is there something more to this girl than meets the eye, something we should be aware of?"

"Well, she does in a lot of ways resemble The Shadow God, Alpha, or, as you two likely know of him better as, The Dark Emperor, that arrived back in the Year 2126 and who the God Emperors seemingly vanquished in the Year 2130, but, even you, Donderdraak, could seemingly also recognize that something was off, and that he wasn't full gone, correct?" Legion responds and begins to explain.

Donderdraak nods solemnly, "Indeed, his essence was scattered, but not destroyed. His power lives on, and it's not beyond possibility that some of it has found its way into her."

"Well, yes, but, not in that way, she herself told me that she is a direct descendant of him from his time as The Shadow God from between the Years of 719 AD and 999 AD, before the massive war for the fate of the Continent of Asia in 999 AD, so that would explain part of it, but, not all of it, also, some theories posit that another dark and shadow magic prodigy child born suspiciously later the same day The Dark Emperor was "slain", a boy, also named Alpha, could possibly and even likely be the reincarnation of him, which continues to be odd to me, that there is both a descendant of him and a reincarnation of him active at the same time." Legion responds and attempts to explain to the best of his ability.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange a look of concern, the implications of two beings with such a connection to a being like The Dark Emperor, especially one that powerful, could be dire if they weren't handled correctly.

"Don't worry, in the USSB Realm we are perfectly safe, and after all, as I said, the Mortal Realm facing cataclysm would only be beneficial to us, leaving an opening for us to take advantage of, so, we needn't worry, aside from for the safety of Zaria herself, who is thankfully safe in a place almost as neutral and separate from the Mortal Realm as the USSB Realm itself, Whispering Sands, in the Sahara Desert." Legion says to them to reassure them, to great success.

The two leaders nod in agreement, "You are right, Legion, the balance of power is always shifting, and we must be prepared for when the scales tip in our favor." Isolde says, her crimson eyes now gleaming with strategy.

"But for now, want to play a game of Uno?" Legion then casually asks his fellow Co-Leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings).

Isolde and Donderdraak look at each other, slightly surprised by the sudden shift in conversation, before smiling and agreeing to the game.

The trio of Co-Leaders of the USSB then begin to play a nice, calm game of Uno.

Chapter 52: The Uno Battle 1

(10 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2240 Donderdraak, Legion, and Isolde are playing a heated game of uno together...

Isolde seems to get to Uno too fast, leaving both Donderdraak and Legion suspicious of her of cheating.

The Vampire Queen's laughter fills the underground chamber, echoing off the cold stone walls. She holds up her hand, displaying her cards with a flourish. "Look, no fangs on my cards!" she teases, flashing a mischievous smile. Legion, a master of deceit and trickery, narrows his eyes. "Very well, Isolde," he says, his voice a low rumble. "But I shall be watching you closely from now on."

"I have already been watching you closely, and saw you hide and pull cards out from your sleeves, which is why you wore a long sleeve shirt, when you tend to wear one with NO SLEEVES!" Donderdraak roars out boomingly.

The room goes silent, and the air grows tense. Isolde's smile falters, but only for a moment. Then she laughs, tossing her cards onto the table. "Ah, you caught me," she says, her eyes sparkling with amusement. "But in all seriousness, we mustn't let our guard down. Even in games of leisure, we must be vigilant."

Donderdraak then reaches one of its claws over and smacks Isolde, The Vampire Queen, across the room, and into Legion, The Demon Lord, leading to now all of them being mad at each other and ready to fight each other.

Their playful banter had turned into a fiery argument, and the tension in the chamber grew thick. Legion's eyes glowed with a malevolent red as he stood, his true form, a towering demon, emerging from his human guise. Isolde's fangs grew long, and her eyes turned into pools of darkness, ready to strike back at the dragon. Donderdraak's scales began to crackle with lightning, the very essence of the storm he once was, as he glared at his former allies.

The three then with just glances, decide to settle this through a free-for-all between them.

With a roar that shakes the very foundations of the chamber, Donderdraak charges at Legion. His massive form is a blur of lightning and shadow as he slams into the demon lord, sending a shockwave through the room. The clang of steel and the crackle of electricity fill the air as the two ancient beings clash. Legion, his true form a terrifying spectacle of brimstone and claws, meets Donderdraak's fury with a ferocity that would make lesser demons cower.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen then flies in faster than both of them and uses her immense strength for her size and blood manipulation to then throw both of them around.

Isolde, unperturbed by the accusations, had been quietly preparing for the moment she could unleash her own power. She had felt the shift in the air, the tension between her two companions, and she knew that the game was no longer just a game. With a grace that belied her deadly nature, she darts forward, her hands a blur as she grabs fistfuls of their essences, dragging them apart and into the air.

Donderdraak then returns back to fighting position the fastest of the three, and launched barrages of lightning and small Charged Attacks at both of them.

The room was now a whirlwind of elemental fury and supernatural might. Each attack and counterattack grew more powerful, the air thick with the scent of ozone and the metallic tang of blood. Legion, his true demonic form a towering specter of rage, dodges a bolt of lightning and swipes at Donderdraak with his fiery claws, leaving a trail of scorched air in their path. Isolde, now a tornado of darkness, weaves around them, her crimson eyes never leaving her targets as she prepares to pounce.

Donderdraak is then able to throw both of them back, causing them to look at each other and nod, agreeing to team up against Donderdraak and jump him.

The alliance between Legion and Isolde is sudden but effective. They had fought together before, and their tactics were well-honed. Legion's fiery whip lashes out, wrapping around Donderdraak's neck, while Isolde's shadows coil around his limbs, seeking to bind him. But Donderdraak, not one to be underestimated, is too quick for them. With a twist of his massive body, he throws them off, sending them crashing into the far corners of the chamber.

Then Legion fires massive beams of hellfire and electricity while Isolde joins him with beams of blood magic and energy as their beams combine and Donderdraak counters with beams of lightning and electricity from his hands, leaving them evenly matched.

The room is now a spectacle of color and chaos, as the three beings of immense power battle it out. The air crackles with energy, the walls of the chamber etched with the marks of their clashing attacks. Each beam of light and dark magic colliding with a deafening clap, sending waves of force that ripple through the very fabric of the room. The smell of ozone and the metallic scent of blood magic permeate the air, a testament to the intensity of the fight.
