Donderdraak then charges up tons of energy in his mouth and fires yet another beam of lightning, electricity, and energy and magic of both and storms to his beam, overpowering both of them, as he looks at his single Uno card in his hands and in the madness a card of the correct color landed in the middle and he slammed his card down and roared out in victory to the universe itself, let alone his now defeated and just disoriented opponents, "I AM VICTORIOUS!"  Donderdraaks shrieks out in victory and satisfaction.

Legion could tell that Donderdraak had definitely overtaken both of them and that, even entering his Berserker State wouldn't have changed anything this time.

Legion himself meanwhile, still feels a bit butt hurt at losing the game of Uno.

Chapter 53: Donderdraak’s Treasure

"So, what do you two wanna play next? Another game of Uno?" Donderdraak then casually asks Legion and Isolde.

The room is still reverberating with the aftershocks of their power, the air thick with the smell of ozone and the faint metallic tang of blood. Legion, his true demonic form slowly dissipating, lets out a low, rumbling chuckle. "Perhaps another time," he says, his eyes gleaming with a hint of mischief. "But for now, let us rest and recover. After all, a battle of wits is as tiring as one of fists and fire."

"Of course, of course, that is to be expected from weaker beings like you two." Donderdraak taunts and teases, alluding to how he is still very much full of energy.

Isolde rolls her eyes at Donderdraak's ego as she begins to get up.

Isolde, brushing off the dust from her clothes, straightens up with an elegant flourish, her vampiric grace restored. "Perhaps you should reserve some of that energy for our next encounter, Donderdraak," she says with a smirk, her eyes flashing with a hint of competitiveness. "After all, I'm sure we'll find other, more... challenging games to play in the future."

"Says, you, I'm still fully rested and energized, that was barely a warm-up." Donderdraak says, casually chuckling, despite being a dragon the size of a mountain or even a mountain range.

Legion meanwhile does feel quite tired out actually, unlike Donderdraak and even Isolde seemingly.

Legion envies their seemingly limitless staminas despite their physical flesh and blood forms.

Legion and Isolde exchange a knowing glance, both knowing that Donderdraak's ego had been slightly bruised by their brief alliance against him. But they also knew that their friendship was strong enough to withstand a little rivalry.

"Oh, and that victory reminded me, I'm going to go and collect all of the treasure from my treasure horde back in Storm Dragon Mountain, considering I'm now too big to live there, I'm bigger than Storm Dragon Mountain itself." Donderdraak then says, chuckling before then opening a hatch in the ceiling and flying out of it and to Storm Dragon Mountain faster than the return stroke of a lightning bolt, which is 70% the Speed of Light, or, 70% to countably infinite speed.

The two of them watch the dragon vanish into the night sky, their eyes reflecting the flicker of the city lights below. "Well, that was... unexpected," Legion says, rubbing his bruised ego with a smirk.

Legion can't not remember Storm Dragon Mountain, that was probably the most short term "fear" and "panic" he's ever felt in his over 444 billion year existence.

Donderdraak then flew to Storm Dragon Mountain and collected all of the treasure from his old treasure horde, all of the gold, and gems, and artifacts and such, storing it all in his mouth, before then returning to the USSB, and then dropping all of it into the USSB Vault, which then causes it to overflow due to just how much treasure there is.

The dragon's treasure hoard was so vast that it filled the vault to the brim, spilling out into the corridors and hallways of the underground fortress. Coins of gold and silver rolled along the floors, and precious gems glittered like stars in the dim light. Artifacts of untold power lay scattered among the piles of wealth, their ancient auras humming with the echoes of long-forgotten magic.

Isolde and Legion are just shocked at how much treasure Donderdraak actually had, it was more treasure than any singular empire, country, or kingdom ever had, let alone a single being, equivalent to effectively infinite money, more than could be calculated for any historical currency.

The Vampire Queen's eyes widened, and Legion's jaw dropped to the floor as they stared at the mountain of gold and jewels that now filled the once-spacious chamber. "I suppose we're going to need a bigger vault," Isolde murmured, her voice barely audible over the clinking of coins and the glint of light bouncing off the gems.

Legion wiped his eyeholes filled with Hellfire as he stared at the effective mountain of varying treasures.

"Of course, a majority of Storm Dragon Mountain was dedicated to the treasure hoard, after all." Donderdraak casually comments, and, yeah, that explains it, this is dang near a mountain's worth of treasure.

Legion, his fiery eyes gleaming with greed, quickly regains his composure and claps his hands together. "Indeed, we must secure these riches," he says, his voice now a mix of awe and excitement. "This wealth could fund our operations for centuries to come!"

Isolde just rolls her eyes at the pure greed of both of her co-leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings), until something then catches her eye among the pile.

Isolde then notices an artifact she actually recognizes, "The Sword of Dracula! I haven't seen this in literal centuries!" Isolde says, and that reminds Legion of something.

"Isolde, you've mentioned Dracula a lot, what is your relation to him?" Legion asks, honestly curious, considering Isolde even used "Sole Heiress of Dracula" as one of her nicknames during the Nickname Game 20 years prior.

And yeah, she had brought up Dracula a lot, one of her favorite exclamations was literally "What in Dracula's name!?".

"Oh, he was my dad." Isolde casually responds, which left Legion utterly shocked, but Donderdraak couldn't care less, he was just now asleep within the overflowing USSB Vault, amongst all of the varied treasure.

Legion does like 5 double takes, trying to process what Isolde just said, her dad was THE Dracula, the Vampire so dominant and powerful he was called a demon, and who could move 1260 times the Speed of Light itself, and could have wiped out an entire country in its entirety, including the landmass, with a wave of his hand if he so chose.

"Wait, what?!" Legion exclaims, his eyes going wide with shock. "The original Dracula? The one who was said to rule over the vampires of Europe?"

"Yep, until he died, I avenged him and succeeded him though, it's why I'm "The Vampire Queen" after all." Isolde casually responds, still looking at the Sword of Dracula in her hands, and the perfect condition it's still in even after centuries.

Legion, still in shock, tries to regain his composure, "Ah, I see, well, quite the lineage you have there, Isolde. Now, let's get back to the matters at hand, we must organize this... treasure," he says, his mind racing with the implications of their newfound wealth.

Legion is still struggling to process that he never knew that Isolde was literally the daughter of Dracula himself.

"My mom was actually human also." Isolde comments, before then turning her attention back to what Donderdraak could be doing.

Legion notes that, and wonders if "hybrid" vampires also just had higher potential as well.

Donderdraak then literally pushes on the ground and walls of the vault, using magic as well, and expands the vault over a 11 miles out and deeper down, sufficient for storing all of the treasure and yet with room for way more, "Done." Donderdraak casually says after doing so, just leaving Legion and Isolde just kinda dumbstruck at the ease that Donderdraak just did that, due to how much power the magic necessary to do something like that is.

The two of them exchange a look of astonishment before Legion finally finds his voice. "I-I never knew you had such control over space and earth, Donderdraak," he stammers.

Legion is shocked, he now recognizes that Donderdraak and Zaria, who at this point is 30, yet he heard is still physically in her late 20s, implying her stopping aging upon hitting her physical prime, are both more powerful than him now, and possibly even Isolde as well, literally everyone is just getting more and more powerful, even mortal humans are growing more and more baseline powerful with each and every one of them born.

"It just requires enough magical power, you should have expected that after the power shown in our fight earlier." Donderdraak just casually responds while getting comfortable on top of the effective Lake of Treasure.

The sight of the expanded vault left Isolde and Legion speechless for a moment, but the practicalities of the situation soon took hold. "We must protect this wealth," Isolde says firmly, placing the Sword of Dracula back with its rightful place among the treasures. "It could be used for great good, or fall into the wrong hands."

Isolde does seemingly wonder how the Sword of Dracula got into the treasure piles, as she then also quickly picks it back up and stashes it away, likely to store elsewhere.

"Or, we could just keep it and use it for ourselves and the USSB alone, leave the great good for others, we are a separate society from them." Donderdraak casually responds.

Legion, his mind racing with the possibilities of such power, nods in agreement. "Indeed, we must ensure that this treasure remains our secret, a tool of strength for our own ends. We shall create the ultimate fortress here, one that no one can ever penetrate."

Legion at this point is definitely embodying greed, and yet, even in that moment embodying greed, his doesn't even come close to comparing to Donderdraak's.

"Just wish Geralt decided to join us, but he said he "had better things to do", such a grouch." Donderdraak comments.

Legion thinks on how he also feels that same way as well.

"Ah, yes," Isolde says with a knowing smile, "Geralt and his... priorities. He's always been one to follow his own path."

"Just happy we got Icefang the Ice Dragon, the Ice Elemental, and stuff like that, even Archdruid Malthus and the Druids of Brocéliande decided to join us, an entire forest down here is always a good thing, especially a magical one." Donderdraak further comments.

Isolde nods, "Indeed, having allies such as them will be invaluable. The protection they can provide is priceless."

"Though Legion, you seem to have beef with the Ice Elemental, is it something to do with both being over 600 foot tall beings summoned during immensely major battles, only to different effect, in which you lost while it won, or is it about it being 34 feet taller than your regular full size, at 700 feet tall, whatever the reason may be, you seem to have beef with it." Donderdraak comments while just chuckling to himself.

Isolde can't hold herself back from laughing at this.

Legion starts to blush fiercely, his already fiery red and charred black skin looking like its about to erupt.

Legion's eyes narrow slightly, his fiery aura flickering in irritation, "It is not about size, dragon," he says, his voice a low rumble. "It's about respect. But that is a matter for another time. For now, let us focus on securing our newfound wealth."

This just causes Isolde to laugh even harder.

Chapter 54: Frostspire Treasure Raid

"Why not go and just raid some ruins of other countries for even more wealth? I heard the Empire of Frostspire's ruins in Korea is full of some amazing treasure, including useful stuff like research, prototype Sacred Relics, and tons of Celestial Jade and blueprints for stuff, though the Empire of Blazing Sun in Japan is somehow still around, and calling itself the Phoenix Empire of Blazing Sun, so, yeah, best to avoid them." Donderdraak suggests.

Legion can immediately just sense how much treasure there is to claim, considering he was once sealed in the Sacred Relic in the possession of the Empire of Frostspire.

Isolde considers the idea, her eyes gleaming with excitement at the prospect of new challenges and rewards. "It could be a valuable source of knowledge and power," she says, her mind already racing with the potential of what they could find.

"And better, they were known to be extremely wealthy, so, tons of gold and gems and such, even old weapons." Donderdraak adds on, emphasizing the gold and gems aspect.

Legion nods, his fiery eyes alight with the idea of new conquests. "We shall send scouting parties to the Frostspire ruins," he says, his tone decisive. "But first, we must fortify our defenses here. We do not want any... uninvited guests."

Legion just said this to not sound too greedy.

"I mean, barely anyone even knows we exist, and, no one, outside of people who live here, even know how to get here, let alone where the entrances are, it'll be fine, also, we can just go there ourselves, we could even make a game out of it, who can collect the most treasure, it could be." Donderdraak says.

Legion can definitely get behind that Idea.

Isolde tilts her head to the side, "A... treasure hunt within the ruins of an ancient empire? That could be quite entertaining, and potentially beneficial." She muses.

"Of course it will be, and I know, I'm a dragon after all, I collected all of my old treasure horde myself, at least, most of it, like, 1% of it was donations from people worshipping me as "The Storm that Consumes All" way back when, shortly after I claimed Storm Dragon Mountain as my own." Donderdraak adds on.

Legion chuckles, "Indeed, a title quite fitting for one with your... scale." He says, still not over the fact that he's friends with a dragon that could literally just end him with a sneeze.

Legion also feels quite humbled and realizes he just accidentally and unintentionally made a dragon pun with the word "scale".

"I mean, of course mere humans had worshiped me as a god of harvests and destruction, and I filled that role, even at my weakest as The Storm Dragon." Donderdraak adds on.

"But let's not get ahead of ourselves, first we need to deal with the more pressing issue of the ruins of Frostspire." Legion says, trying to get back on topic.

Legion wants to get going already.

"Of course, last one there is a mere mortal!" Donderdraak then opens the hatch and flies out and towards Korea, where the ruins are, getting a head start through playful manipulation and deception.

Legion and Isolde, though slightly annoyed by Donderdraak's antics, can't help but smile at his childlike competitiveness. They both decide to take their own paths to the ruins. Legion teleports himself to the outskirts of the city, leaving a fiery trail behind him, while Isolde opts for a more subtle approach, using her vampiric speed to traverse the land unseen by mortal eyes.

Donderdraak gets there first, followed by Legion who is followed effectively instantaneously by Isolde.

Legion feels embarrassed that he only came in second, and only just barely ahead of Isolde, when both of the others were traveling physically while he was teleporting.

The ruins of Frostspire lay sprawled before them, a testament to the grandeur of the once-mighty empire. The three friends, now turned treasure hunters, spread out, each eager to claim their share of the spoils. Donderdraak's massive frame casts long shadows as he lumbers through the ancient corridors, his eyes glinting with greed. Legion, on the other hand, moves with the grace of a panther, his fiery aura lighting up the darkened chambers. Isolde flits from shadow to shadow, her vampiric agility allowing her to cover ground swiftly and silently.

They of course play their game, and it doesn't end until they've completely emptied out the ruins of any and all treasure.

The game of one-upmanship continues as they explore the ancient corridors, each discovery met with cheers and laughter that echo through the once-silent halls. The ruins, long abandoned to the ravages of time, hold secrets that have been waiting for centuries to be uncovered. Among the treasure, they find ancient tomes filled with knowledge of the lost arts of Frostspire, weapons of unparalleled beauty and power, and jewels that whisper of ancient pacts and forgotten deities.

The game eventually ends once the ruins are fully cleaned out of any treasure, and Donderdraak got multiple times more than the other two combined, due to his natural dragon senses for finding treasure.

"I can still smell treasure, somewhere." Donderdraak says, continuing to smell around, until finding the source, and Donderdraak manages to trigger a very complicated process and open a hidden massive trap door, and the smell of treasure grows way stronger, leaving Donderdraak salivating.

"What have you found, Donderdraak?" Isolde calls out, her curiosity piqued by the dragon's sudden excitement.

Legion chuckles in his mind, Donderdraak is pretty much a bloodhound for treasure and all things valuable.

And Legion wouldn't want it any other way of course.

"Heaven, I think this might be the ancient vault!" Donderdraak then shrinks down to the same size as them and rushes in unprompted.

The chamber that lay beneath the trapdoor was vast, filled with the gleaming treasures of the Frostspire Empire. The three friends looked around in amazement, their eyes widening at the sheer amount of wealth that surrounded them. It was a hoard that would make even the most stoic of dragons weep with envy.

Donderdraak then goes absolute buck wild, and even in this second round, the two collectively are only able to grab 1% of the treasure, as Donderdraak swiftly collected the other 99% of it.

"Oh come on, Donderdraak, that's not fair!" Isolde protests with a laugh, watching the dragon hoard his treasure with the enthusiasm of a kid in a candy store.

"Too bad, you aren't dragons, I'm naturally better at this! This is heaven!" Donderdraak just screams out in joy, as Legion then teleports all of them, along with all of the treasure they have collected, back to the USSB base, and they put all of it into the USSB Vault, and it is approximately equal to half of what Donderdraak had in his original treasure horde in Storm Dragon Mountain.

Legion really thinks about how it puts into perspective just how much treasure Donderdraak was able to horde in that mountain of his, while weakened, all by himself, of which, Storm Dragon Mountain is then also brought into the USSB Realm itself, and now they have a massive mountain in their realm, increased in size beyond twice the height of any regular mountain, at over 11 Miles tall, as part of the landscape of the USSB Realm, alongside the Forest of Brocéliande and his old Demon Lord castle gotten straight from Heaven/Hell/the Underworld/the Infernal Realm, and even all of Transylvania, and the Dracula Kingdom, both of which Isolde obviously ruled.

The vault now looked like a miniature mountain of gold, glowing with the soft light of countless gems and artifacts. It was a hoard that could make any dragon's heart sing with joy, and it was all theirs.

"Well, we should sort through this and get out all of the Artifacts to a separate area, considering their importance." Legion says, and, a second later, Donderdraak had dived in, and found and collected every artifact and placed them onto the ground in front of them.

Legion chuckled to himself at Donderdraak literally diving in.

Isolde looks through them, "Some of these are incredibly powerful, and some... are just useless trinkets with no real power, we should separate them."

Donderdraak then further separates them, and one of the trinkets Legion recognizes, "Oh, it's my old Demon Lord Crown, nice!" Legion says as he puts it onto himself with glee.

Isolde then looks at the crown, "Why do you have a crown for a demon lord?"

And Legion remembers, oh yeah, compared to himself, the other two aren't quite as intelligent of informed at he is.

"I'm The Demon Lord, remember, I lost this thing back when Emperor Marcus of Sanctity sealed me away in the Celestial Jade back in the Year 400 which would later become the Core of the Sacred Relic, not to be confused with the Core Sacred Relic." Legion explains.

"Ah, yes, I remember now," Isolde says, her eyes sparkling with mirth, "How you lost your title and crown to a mere human, quite the embarrassing tale for a being of your stature."

"Not the most embarrassing instance of that..." Legion says, blushing hard.

"Oh yeah, like how after you got distracted by Emperor Thorek I of Frosthaven in the Year 1000 in the Battle of Vatican City you got killed by a volley of arrows?" Donderdraak says, teasingly.

"Shut up you giant lightning lizard!" Legion screams at Donderdraak, blushing even harder.

Donderdraak just laughs, "It's all in good fun, Legion, also, I've always wondered, why do you get so embarrassed about that?"

"Because I died to a volley of arrows, just normal arrows... from MORTAL HUMANS!" Legion screams out, blushing even harder.

Isolde couldn't hold her laughter, "But you came back stronger!" She says, trying to cheer him up.

"Weaker, actually, I only recently regained that lost power, much like the lightning lizard who got stabbed into being over 387 times smaller did." Legion says, also throwing a jab at Donderdraak to make himself feel better and less embarrassed.

Legion hides the fact that he hasn't regained nearly all of that power yet, hoping the other two just forget that he had told them so two decades earlier.

Chapter 55: Uncursed Beasts

The banter between them fills the chamber, but Isolde's gaze remains fixed on the artifacts laid before them. "We must be careful," she says, her voice growing serious. "Some of these relics are rumored to be cursed, or hold powers that could be used for ill intent. We must ensure they do not fall into the wrong hands."

"We are a Massive Storm Dragon, The Demon Lord, and The Vampire Queen, I doubt any of the curses could actually affect us, for multiple different reasons." Donderdraak responds.

"True, but we cannot be too careful," Isolde says, her eyes scanning the artifacts with a mix of caution and curiosity. "We must ensure that these relics do not bring harm to the mortals we swore to protect, or worse, fall into the hands of those who would seek to use them for destruction."

"Also, how would they, they likely aren't ever leaving this place, because, why would we ever bring them out? We definitely aren't going to be giving any of them to any mere mortals, they don't deserve them, and any of those that would come to take them and use them for destruction, would just get destroyed by us or other members of the USSB." Donderdraak responds.

"You're right, Donderdraak," Legion says, his tone turning more serious. "But we must still be cautious. The less the outside world knows of these artifacts, the better. We will keep them safe and hidden within our vault."

Legion in truth just doesn't want any mortals touching his treasure with even just their sight.

"Even if they knew of them, no one knows where our base is, or where the entrance is, or that the USSB even exists." Donderdraak responds.

"Indeed," Legion nods, "But secrets have a way of slipping out, especially with so much power at stake. We must ensure that we are always vigilant."

"Who would the secrets slip out to, any mortals that know get marked for inevitable death, and any beasts thusly join the USSB and are sworn to secrecy, the only way one could find it is through literally impossibly powerful telepathy, or through one of us revealing it directly, and, like that even has a risk of ever happening." Donderdraak further responds, finally putting Legion and Isolde's worries to rest.

Isolde mostly because she recognizes just how truly secure the USSB Realm is to infiltration and espionage and robbery and such.

They spend hours examining the artifacts, some of which are covered in ancient runes that even the scholarly Isolde struggles to decipher. One artifact, in particular, catches her eye - a delicate necklace made of Celestial Jade, adorned with intricate ice crystals. It hums faintly with the power of Frostspire's Empress Sora, and she can feel a sense of ambition and determination emanating from it.

Legion though is able to resonate with it, due to having been sealed within Celestial Jade for almost 300 years, which later became the core of the Sacred Relic.

Isolde, intrigued by the necklace, brings it closer to examine the intricate ice crystals. As she does, the room grows cold, and the air crackles with the power of ancient Frostspire. Legion, sensing the potency of the artifact, approaches with caution. "This... this was Empress Sora's personal seal," he murmurs, his eyes wide with a mix of awe and trepidation.

Legion would recognize it well, considering his very prison he was sealed in had that personal seal for over 281 years.

Donderdraak then suddenly begins laughing about something, leaving the others confused.

"What's so funny, Donderdraak?" Isolde asks, her curiosity piqued.

"I just realized, that, throughout all three major eras of war, there has always been one of the two empires which was not only ice related, but had frost in the name, Frostspire back in the Years 719-999, Frosthaven in the Years 1000-1004, and Frostreach from the Year 2124 to now, it's just kinda funny to think about and realize." Donderdraak explains while still chuckling.

Isolde smiles slightly at his observation, "Indeed, it seems destined, almost poetic, doesn't it?" She says, her eyes still fixed on the necklace.

Chapter 56: The Dark Light Truth

"Yes, like light and dark fighting, speaking of which, I have heard some rumors recently, that morality is the reverse of the truth, and that The Light is actually the True Evil, and that The Dark is the True Good, what are your thoughts on this idea?" Donderdraak asks Isolde and Legion.

Isolde strokes the necklace, her eyes lost in thought, "Rumors are but whispers in the wind, Donderdraak. The truth is often more complex than such simplistic dichotomies. Good and evil are choices we make, not destinies bestowed by the heavens."

Legion recognizes an opportunity to break their minds with the revelation again and decides to take it.

Legion then suddenly and abruptly speaks, "The rumors are true." Legion says abruptly, to the point of catching Donderdraak and Isolde off-guard.

They both turn to him with shock etched on their faces, "What do you mean?" Donderdraak asks, his humor forgotten.

"I originate from Hell, as most Demons do, but most don't know, it is also Heaven, there is no difference, and, in fact, it is the Light Realm, and is ruled by Erebus, The Golden Devil, the God of Light, God of All, a pure evil, sadistic, and malicious being, and the embodiment of the nature of The Light, and the creator of it, and, well, if the embodiment and creator of The Light is Pure Evil, then that must mean that The Dark is, in truth... Pure Good... only acting in opposition to The Light..." Legion explains, seeming to make realizations as he is explaining them towards the end of it.

Isolde and Donderdraak exchange glances, the gravity of his words sinking in. The concept of their entire existence being based on a fundamental lie was too much to comprehend. They had always known the struggle between light and dark, but to think that the very nature of their being could be reversed was a revelation that shook their core beliefs.

"Well, it doesn't really matter to us, considering we are separate from that conflict and neutral in relation to it, we are separate from the outside world, in fact, the USSB is effectively it's own separate realm within the Infinite Dimensional Universe from the Living/Physical Realm, it is, I guess, the USSB Realm, so, we care not for the trifles of those outside of our realm, all we care about in the Living/Physical Realm are the beasts, mythological creatures, and wealth within it, for us to have either join us or for us to claim for ourselves." Donderdraak then says.

Isolde nods slowly, "Indeed, our allegiance is to the USSB and our mission. But these revelations do give us much to think about. It seems we have stumbled upon truths that could shake the very foundations of the cosmos."

"Yes, but, well, we should keep our realization to ourselves, as currently there is peace on Earth due to the True Sacred Relic's existence, but, I can sense, even that is only temporary, and that, while over a century from now, that peace, and the True Sacred Relic, will come to an end, all for the greater good, I can also feel." Donderdraak explains, "So, let's enjoy our neutrality while we have it." Donderdraak further adds on.

Legion nods solemnly, placing his crown back into the treasure pile, "We shall keep our eyes open and our hearts pure, ready to protect our newfound family and home, regardless of the chaos that may come." His words hang in the air, a reminder of the ever-present balance between light and dark.

Legion ponders on how war will of course arrive when in truth that balance is broken, if in the favor of the Light, all will cease to be, but if in the favor of the Dark, true peace will become inevitable

"Anyways, wanna play Go Fish?" Donderdraak asks, "You know, to de-stress a bit?" Donderdraak adds on.

Isolde smiles and nods, "I'd love to, it's been a while since I've played a card game." She says, and the three of them sit down at the grand round table in the USSB's main hall, setting aside their concerns for the moment.

Chapter 57: The Bigger USSB

(10 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2250, and Legion, The Demon Lord once again checks on Zaria from afar, who was by now 40 but still in her physical prime, un-aging and likely immortal, watching her growth, but also trains with Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, to help all three of them grow more powerful, mostly through extremely intense spars, some planned, and some that come about due to games of Uno that become too intense, and more beyond those were also used in the training between the three Co-Leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings).

The USSB had grown substantially since its founding, and with the arrival of new members such as the Werewolf Alpha, Fenris, and the Kraken, Leviathan, they had become an unstoppable force in the Infinite Dimensional Universe.

Legion does note how Fenris has somehow returned despite Legion himself tearing him in half and consuming both halves in his "Prime" back in 666 BC.

The sparring sessions between the trio are fierce, pushing each other's limits and honing their skills. Legion, having once been the most feared creature in existence, is thrilled to see his companions grow stronger with each passing day. Their bond, forged in the heat of battle and the pursuit of power, has grown into a true friendship.

In fact, more so almost like a strange and dysfunctional found family unit, but Legion does note how both Donderdraak and Isolde have both by far surpassed him, and internally plans to in some way change that at least somewhat and at least somewhat close the gap.

The USSB has become a beacon of protection for the mythical creatures of Earth, and their influence extends far beyond their hidden realm. They have established a network of safe havens across the globe, where beasts and myths can live without fear of persecution from humans.

But in essence, these safe havens are effectively pocket dimension sub-realms of the USSB Realm, with direct access to the USSB Realm itself.

"So, Legion, when do we get to use our new abilities in a real fight?" Isolde asks, a mischievous glint in her eye as she examines her claws, which had grown significantly sharper and stronger.

"Hopefully never need to other than simple fights in hidden areas in the Mortal Realm, but, conflict is likely to not break out again for at least over a Century and Three-Quarters or so." Legion casually responds.

"But you know how much I enjoy a good fight," Isolde says with a grin, her fangs gleaming in the dim light of the chamber.

"Then go out and use a scan spell to find hidden interesting stuff and potential opponents yourself then, I did so 3 decades ago, which led to that African Scientist Bunker, the Continent of Neo-Atlantis, the Arctic Bunker, and that Mythical Jungle being transported from the Mortal Realm to the USSB Realm, so, yeah, if you're really that bored, go look for a possible fight to end that boredom." Legion casually responds.

Isolde chuckles, "Alright, alright, I'll go stir up some trouble, I guess." She says, standing up from her seat.

"Yeah, you go and do that then, Isolde." Legion responds casually and offhandedly truly, before then returning to his own training.

Donderdraak watches Isolde leave, a knowing smile on his face. "I think she enjoys it more than she lets on."

"I don't doubt that, I swear that we all are rubbing off on each other to an extent." Legion responds.

Donderdraak nods in agreement, "But it's a good thing, we're becoming more versatile and unpredictable, and that's what makes us so formidable."

"Or it could be the fact that we all are effectively deities on top of naturally having immense power, and who train on top of that, that could also be it as well." Legion responds snarkily.

Donderdraak chuckles in response, "True, but it's the chaos we bring that truly sets us apart. Now, if only we could get Zaria to spar with us, we could see just how much she's learned since we first found her."

"Since, I, first found her, you mean, neither you nor Isolde have even seen nor met Zaria in any way shape or form." Legion responds uncharacteristically harshly about such a small thing.

Donderdraak nods, his smile not fading, "True, but I can feel her power from here, it's grown exponentially since she's been with us, and it's only a matter of time before she's ready to join us in battle."

"That would be if she even wanted to join us, and, yeah, if she doesn't want to, then she doesn't want to, and that would be final." Legion says, uncharacteristically very respecting of Zaria's desires to the point of not wanting to force her to ally with them for the benefit of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings), which is otherwise all that Legion usually cares about.

"Ah, I know she will, she's just... different, that's all, she's not like us, she's more human in a way, more... tender." Donderdraak says, trying to find the right words.

"She actually IS Human, compared to us, a Demon, a Storm Dragon Summoned using a ritual, and a Vampire, and she doesn't have to be anything else, and to imply otherwise is to take things too far." Legion responds, uncharacteristically seemingly to desire what Zaria wants most over all else.

Donderdraak nods solemnly, "I understand, she is special, and we should respect her boundaries."

"You definitely got that right, her choices matter more than our wants." Legion says, super uncharacteristically placing the choices and wants of someone else even over his own.

The two powerful beings continue their training in the vast chamber of the USSB Realm, each pushing the other to new heights of power. Their friendship, though unconventional, is a strong bond that has withstood the test of time.

Legion though, thinks of something, and constructs a special magical arena, of which, will restore both combatants afterwards, allowing for fights to the death within it with no downsides or risks possible.

The arena is a marvel of dark magic and ancient technology, a place where beings could clash without fear of permanent harm. It's a place where they could unleash their full might and see just how powerful they truly are.

Legion also creates a spell to created a simulated reality for them to fight in including with their truest power and potential without risking destroying all of not just reality, the fabric of reality, but Reality, of which Existence and all of the infinite layers of constructs far above and below their Infinite Universes, through their combat, but they don't feel a need to use that yet, so it lays, waiting to be used.

Their training sessions in the arena become legendary, even among the other members of the USSB. Word spreads of their epic battles, and soon, other powerful beings within the society begin to request to spar with them, eager to test their might against the trio.

This satiates boredom for a while, leading to just peace, and of course, Legion continuing to observe Zaria and her growth from afar.

Chapter 58: Legion Vs. Ice Elemental

(30 Years Later)

The Year is 2280, and Legion sets up a pretty much wrestling type event for the battle between him and The Ice Elemental, to truly prove himself to be greater than it, despite being 34 feet shorter at his tallest height.

Then, after a bit of prep, many people arrive in the seats of the Colosseum constructed as decoration around the Arena for people to observe from, and Isolde and Donderdraak finally arrive to the stands, Isolde with a bucket of popcorn, Donderdraak with one 10x the size of the Roman Colosseum, to enjoy while they watch the battle.

Legion, The Demon Lord, then grows to his basic full size of 666 feet and stare at the 700 foot tall Ice Elemental, until both then grow even further to Legion being 666 meters tall and the Ice Elemental being 700 meters tall, as Legion then further emerges massive demon wings from his back, as well.

The arena is a frozen wasteland, a testament to the power of the Ice Elemental, but Legion, with his newfound power and size, is unfazed. He cracks his knuckles, a sound that echoes across the battlefield like the tolling of a doomsday bell. The Ice Elemental, a being of ancient and primal cold, locks eyes with Legion, its icy gaze a stark contrast to Legion's fiery one.

The Ice Elemental, is a now 700 meter tall beast of ice and snow, most resembling a massive golem of ice, with very primitive intelligence, yet powerful ice manipulation abilities and immense brute strength, and even regenerative capabilities via freezing air around it at extreme speeds due to is far below zero aura to reform parts of it, and originates from sometime around the Year 1124.

The arena, originally the size of the Roman Colosseum, has been magically expanded to accommodate the towering forms of the two titans. The air is frigid, but Legion's fiery aura keeps the immediate area around him warm. He cracks his knuckles once more, the sound echoing through the icy arena.

Legion, in his prime size as the Massive Demon, is a currently 666 meter tall demon lord, with massive bat and dragon like demon wings, massive and curved goat like horns, and a body which is muscular but also resembles volcanic rock, with his face resembling a mix of a human, goat, and dragon's skulls, with him having incredible size changing abilities, powerful control over fire and lightning, and the ability to breath hellfire so hot it shouldn't be physically possible, far beyond even the Planck Temperature, and he is immensely quick, agile, and determined to win this match to finally end his insecurity about the Ice Elemental.

The two behemoths charge at each other, the ground trembling with each step they take. The Ice Elemental swings a massive arm made of compacted snow and ice, aiming to crush Legion. Legion, with his lightning-fast reflexes, dodges the attack with ease, leaving a trail of fire in the ice's wake. He then unleashes a torrent of hellfire from his mouth, the flames dancing and weaving as they streak towards the Ice Elemental.

The Ice Elemental roars in fury, raising its other arm to shield itself from the fiery onslaught. The heat from the flames causes the ice to sizzle and steam, but the creature's regenerative abilities keep it from taking significant damage. It retaliates by slamming its fist into the ground, sending a wave of freezing air towards Legion. Legion, anticipating the move, takes to the air on his mighty wings, soaring above the frigid blast.

The spectators, which include various members of the USSB and some of the more powerful mythological creatures they've encountered, watch with bated breath as the two titans continue their epic duel. The air is filled with the scent of burning ice and sulfur, a peculiar mix that makes the hair on the back of everyone's neck stand up. The clash of fire and ice lights up the night sky, casting an eerie glow over the landscape.

Isolde, meanwhile, munching on her popcorn, can't help but feel a little guilty for egging Legion on. She's never seen the Ice Elemental in action before, and the creature's power is truly terrifying. Yet, she can't deny the excitement that thrums through her as she watches the Demon Lord she's come to trust and respect over the centuries. Donderdraak, on the other hand, is practically bouncing in his seat, his enthusiasm for the fight palpable.

The battle is a symphony of destruction, each strike and counter-strike a crescendo of power. Legion's hellfire clashes with the Ice Elemental's freezing blasts, creating a spectacle that would be the envy of any mortal artist. The air shimmers with heat and cold as the two beings push each other to their limits. The ground beneath them cracks and splits, the very fabric of the land reshaping itself to accommodate their titanic struggle.

Donderdraak, unable to contain his excitement, cheers Legion on, "Go, go, go! Show that chunk of ice what for!" His laughter booming across the frozen wasteland. Isolde, though more reserved in her enthusiasm, can't help but feel the thrill of the fight, her heart racing as she watches the two behemoths clash.

In a sudden burst of speed, Legion dives towards the Ice Elemental, his wings folding in to become a blazing meteor hurtling towards his icy adversary. The Ice Elemental, noticing the incoming threat, raises a towering spike of ice from the ground, aiming to impale Legion as he approaches. Legion, ever the tactician, uses his fiery breath to melt the spike before it reaches its peak, causing it to shatter into countless shards that rain down upon the battlefield.

Then, suddenly, Legion shrinks down, much to the confusion of the spectators as to why he would make such a move, then he rockets up at the Ice Elemental with his fist extended.

Having shrunk himself down to compact and condense his power down to a smaller surface area though with the same weight and area covered overtime in speed.

The Ice Elemental attempts to block it with its arm, but to the shock of everyone short of Legion himself, that just results in said arm exploding, and the Ice Elemental moving back to regenerate said arm.

Isolde is left perplexed until she pieces it together, "He's the same weight and has the same power, so, in shrinking, he's just compacting the power of his punches, genius!" Isolde says, as if she just had a breakthrough herself.

Donderdraak nods, his eyes wide with excitement, "Yes! That's it! He's basically punching like a 666-meter giant but with the speed of a 666-meter giant into a 700-meter giant, but in a smaller form, which is just insane!"

The Ice Elemental swiftly regenerates its arm then swings it at Legion, who then vanishes completely until there's just a speck left, much to the shock of everyone, especially the Ice Elemental, until then, said speck impacts the Ice Elemental and it's entire upper body just explodes, of which, it quickly regenerates, then Legion returns back to his 666-meter size and begins to charge up energy in his fist for a massive punch.

Isolde then realizes what he's about to do and begins popping off.

"Oh, he's going to punch it again!" She exclaims, her popcorn momentarily forgotten as she watches Legion's fist grow hotter and hotter, the heat from his hellfire enveloping it like a sun.

Then Legion dodges the Ice Elemental's punch by shrinking to his "Normal" size of 6.66 feet tall with his punch still charged up, and begins to fly at the Ice Elemental at the Speed of Light itself, at infinite speed essentially.

And the punch lands, and such a powerful force, compacted down to such a small area, then causes the Ice Elemental to literally explode into shards all over the place, still alive as the shards, but definitely defeated, and the punch landing and the shockwave from it causes shaking equivalent to as if a moon just exploded right in that spot.

Despite it having force itself closer to that of a Supernova.

The spectators, including Isolde and Donderdraak, watch in awe as the dust and ice settles. The arena, once a frozen battleground, is now a crater of molten earth, the heat from Legion's punch having melted even the most ancient of ice. The air is filled with the scent of burnt ice and ash, a testament to the power that was just displayed.

The Ice Elemental then regenerates at its new normal size of 777 feet tall, having lost the battle, but, as it is 77 feet taller normally, it clearly Awakened from its loss.

The once-mighty Ice Elemental stands in the center of the now molten crater, its form reforming from the scattered shards of ice. Its power, though not diminished, is clearly drained from the intense battle. Legion, on the other hand, stands tall and triumphant, his fiery eyes gleaming with the thrill of victory.

Chapter 59: Elara Humiliates Legion

Legion still feels super fired up and ready to fight again.

Then Elara, Isolde's Head Maid, arrives and gives Isolde a wine glass filled with blood, and Legion notices her, and, still fired up from that previous fight, immediately grabs her and pulls her in the arena and forces her to then also fight him.

The sudden turn of events leaves Isolde and Donderdraak dumbfounded. Elara, a vampire with centuries of combat experience, reluctantly takes a stance opposite Legion. She is petite and graceful, a stark contrast to the hulking form of the Demon Lord. Yet, her eyes glow with a fierce determination that belies her true strength.

Legion then rushes at her, expecting this to be extremely easy, but then, she vanishes from the sight of all but Isolde, as the then pulls out her trusty broom and smacks Legion from all angles with it, even landing a quite cheap shot on him with her broom, all faster than he can even react.

Legion is left extremely dazed, still trying to process what just happened.

The crowd, and even Donderdraak, are stunned into silence as they witness Elara's unrivaled speed and agility. Her movements are a blur, each strike with the broom packing the power of a freight train. Legion, though caught off-guard, laughs boisterously, his eyes alight with the challenge.

Elara then bonks Legion on the head with just her trusty broom, before then bringing out a feather duster and dusting his face, then repeatedly bonking him with a dust tray, all faster than he can even hope to react.

Legion is left now very confused with his face thoroughly dusted now.

The crowd, which had been silent, now erupts into laughter at the sight of the mighty Demon Lord being smacked around by a Head Maid with nothing but a broom and dust tray. Donderdraak is in stitches, holding his belly as tears roll down his cheeks. Even Isolde has to cover her mouth to stifle a giggle, the tension from the previous fight dissipating in the face of such an unexpected and comical turn of events.

Legion is left feeling quite embarrassed at how he is literally getting cleanly swept by a god dang MAID.

Elara also barely seems to care, and barely seems to even be trying in the slightest bit, and yet multiple times in a row she has Legion on his behind on the ground, all within less than a minute.

"Alright, alright, I get it," Legion says with a grin, his cheeks reddened slightly, "I guess you win this round." He then picks himself up, brushing off the dust from his body. "But, don't think you've seen the last of me, I'll train harder, and I'll be back for a rematch!" He laughs heartily, his good nature never dimming, not even when he's on the receiving end of a good-natured whooping.

Elara then bonks Legion on the head again with her trusty broom, to the point she smacks him into the ground, where only his head is coming out of it, before Elara then goes back over to Isolde, The Vampire Queen, "I apologize immensely about that interruption, your highness." Elara says to Isolde, sounding very embarrassed at having been pulled away and distracted from serving her.

Legion, meanwhile pulls himself out of the ground.

Isolde just laughs and pats her on the head, "Don't worry Elara, you did well, you can take a break." Isolde says, her smile warm and kind.

"That is greatly appreciated, your highness." Elara says while bowing very deeply.

The crowd is still in a state of disbelief and laughter as the battle between Legion and Elara ends. The tension from the previous fight has given way to light-hearted amusement. Legion, unfazed by his defeat at the hands of the vampire maid, stands up and stretches. "Now, who's next?" He roars, looking around for another challenger, his fiery aura seemingly undimmed by the embarrassing turn of events.

Chapter 60: Demon Lord Vs. Archdruid

Archdruid Malthus, leader of the Undead Druids of Brocéliande, then enters the arena as well, "I accept your challenge, Lord of Demons." Archdruid Malthus says to him.

The crowd goes quiet, as they have not seen the Archdruid in quite some time, and even Isolde seems surprised, but she knows that Malthus is one of the strongest members of the USSB, so she is not too concerned.

"Alright then, you apparently led the Elemental Avatars during the Siege of the Temple/Church/Tomb of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius and defended it quite well for quite a long time, so, this should be rather interesting." Legion responds to Archdruid Malthus.

The Archdruid stands tall and proud, his skeletal frame adorned with ancient druidic symbols and garb made of vines and leaves. His staff, crafted from the heartwood of a long-dead oak tree, glows with a green light that seems to pulse in time with his own life force. The air around him is charged with the power of nature, and the ground beneath his feet begins to shift and writhe with roots and vines as he prepares to engage the demon lord.

Legion can't help but be reminded of his battle with Zaria 40 years earlier.

"Let the power of the eternal forests be my weapon!" Malthus intones, and the very air around him seems to thicken with the scent of earth and decay. With a wave of his staff, he calls upon the most ancient and powerful of all the druidic powers, summoning the Great Ent of the Broken Realm. The colossal tree creature stands tall, its wooden form writhing with vines and thorns, and its eyes burning with the fury of nature itself.

The Great Ent of the Broken Realm is about 600 meters tall, and Archdruid Malthus then teleports on top of it's head, and sinks into the head of it, and begins to watch from through it's eyes.

Legion, intrigued by this new development, spreads his fiery wings and takes to the sky, eyeing the colossal Ent with a mix of caution and excitement. He knows that Malthus is a formidable foe, and that controlling an elemental being such as this is no small feat.

The Great Ent of the Broken Realm then extends one of its arms up and into the air and grabs Legion by his leg and then slams him against the ground before then winding the other fist up for a massive punch.

Legion, unfazed by the initial grab, twists and turns in the Ent's grasp, dodging the punch with ease. He then shrinks again, and this time, aims for the Ent's core, the heart of its power. As he does so, he leaves a trail of fire in his wake, the vines and thorns of the Ent's body catching fire. The Ent roars in pain, the flames spreading rapidly across its wooden form.

The Archdruid, feeling the Ent's distress, channels more of his power into it, causing the Ent to regenerate the damaged areas. But Legion is not deterred. He uses his compacted punch to bore through the flaming vines, aiming for the staff that is the source of the Ent's power. If he can destroy the staff, he knows he can weaken Malthus's control over the creature.

Archdruid then absorbs The Great Ent of the Broken Realm and its power and strength into himself from within it, then swings his staff around as then ruins and sigils appear as Archdruid Malthus then completes the spell, and then, he becomes Awakened, and his power increases immensely.

The crowd gasps as the air around Malthus changes, the green light of his staff now enveloping him in a whirlwind of leaves and vines. The ground shakes as the power of the Ent surges through his bones, his eyes now glowing with an emerald light that pierces through the night. Legion, seeing the change in his opponent, knows that this will be no simple fight.

Legion now seriously can't help but reminded of his spar with Zaria from 4 decades prior.

Awakened Archdruid Malthus then rushes and flies forwards and smacks Legion with his staff.

Legion, caught off-guard by the sudden surge in power, is sent sprawling across the arena. The impact leaves a trail of flaming leaves and scorched earth in his wake. He gets to his feet, brushing himself off, a smirk playing on his lips. "Now, this is a challenge!" He says, his fiery aura growing brighter, his fists crackling with hellfire.

The Archdruid Malthus, now a terrifying amalgamation of man and nature, advances towards Legion. The vines from his staff lash out like whips, each one aimed to ensnare and entangle the demon lord. Legion, however, is quick to respond. He shrinks again and darts around the vines, his movements a blur. He then expands, his fist now the size of a small mountain, and swings at the Archdruid.

Awakened Archdruid Malthus uses a simple and casual spell and reverses the damage dealt now to being dealt onto Legion instead of himself.

The vines snap back, recoiling from the fiery fist of Legion, and the demon lord feels a strange sensation as the power of his own attack is redirected back at him. The air around Malthus crackles with energy as he bends the very fabric of the battle to his will. Legion, surprised by this counterattack, stumbles backward, his flaming skin now marred by the bite of the vines' power.

Chapter 61: Awakened Vs. Awakened

Legion then suddenly begins to rapidly power up, "Turn up the heat! Turn UP the heat! TURN UP THE HEAT! BECAUSE THIS IS A FUNERAL FOR THE LIVING DEAD!" Legion screams out as he also becomes Awakened and his fiery aura explodes out into becoming immensely greater and more intense.

Legion is now seriously feeling that good heat of battle at this point.

The crowd is now absolutely silent, not a single soul daring to move as the two powerhouses stand before each other, the very air crackling with the energy of their auras. The arena is bathed in the stark contrast of Legion's fiery red and Malthus's verdant green.

Malthus's eyes narrow as he feels the demon lord's power rise to new heights. He knows he must act swiftly to maintain the upper hand. With a flick of his wrist, he sends a barrage of thorns the size of spears hurtling towards Legion. Each thorn is imbued with the power of the Ent and the wrath of the forest.

Legion, now fully awakened, meets the thorns with a blast of hellfire, the two forces colliding in a fiery explosion that lights up the night sky. The heat is so intense that it causes the very air to ripple and distort. The thorns disintegrate upon contact, but the Archdruid is not deterred. He calls upon the spirits of the forest to bolster his strength, the vines around him thickening and coiling into a living cage.

Awakened Demon Lord Legion then briefly shifts into his true form, a figure of pure hellfire, so fast he doesn't even more, just decides where he will next be then is, and then appears right next to Awakened Archdruid Malthus before then going back into his normal form and then throwing a point-blank punch at Awakened Archdruid Malthus.

The impact of the punch sends shockwaves through the arena, knocking over the spectators, the ground around them cracking like glass. The Archdruid, caught by surprise, is sent hurtling through the air, his staff flying from his grip. The vines and thorns that made up his cage dissipate into the night as he crashes into the far wall of the arena.

Awakened Demon Lord Legion then appears right in front of him, with another punch charged up and wound back, before then throwing it, with an especially immense amount of force.

He had prepared and invented a new move from Awakening.

The fist, now a meteor of pure hellfire, hurtles towards Awakened Archdruid Malthus, who is still recovering from the first blow. Malthus, with a roar of defiance, channels the last of the Ent's power into a shield of living wood, the bark hardening into a nearly impenetrable barrier.

Awakened Demon Lord Legion then actually goes all out and his charged punch smashes right through the shield of living wood, uninhibited in the slightest, and continues its course towards Awakened Archdruid Malthus himself.

Malthus, his eyes wide with a mix of anger and fear, knows he must act quickly. He reaches out with his mind, connecting with the very essence of the forest, and pulls the roots from deep within the earth, sending them shooting towards Legion with the speed and precision of a hundred arrows.

Awakened Demon Lord Legion's awakened aura of hellfire alone burns them all to ash instantly.

The roots come into contact with Legion's fist, but instead of stopping it, they merely disintegrate into a fine mist of carbon, the sheer power of the demon lord's attack unimpeded. Malthus watches in horror as the fiery fist bears down on him, knowing that his shield has failed.

But as the punch is about to land, something unexpected happens. A surge of power, something that even Legion could not have anticipated, bursts forth from the ground beneath Malthus. The Archdruid's eyes glow with the light of the ancients as the spirits of the forest, enraged by the desecration of their sacred lands, enter his body.

Awakened Demon Lord Legion's awakened aura then instantly disintegrates the spirits of the forest themselves on a conceptual level.

The Archdruid's body convulses as the ancient spirits are consumed by Legion's inferno, their essences winking out of existence with a soundless scream. The fist of hellfire slams into Malthus, and for a moment, it seems as if the druid will be erased from the mortal plane.

Awakened Archdruid Malthus then tries to call upon every concept he can think of, space, time, nature, reality, infinity itself, any of them, and yet, they all immediately are burnt away by Awakened Demon Lord Legion's awakened aura, as the heat of which is absolute, and burns away even concepts, and the only things that aren't burnt, are things he doesn't want burnt for one reason or another.

Malthus's body is now a smoldering ruin, his skeletal frame barely visible through the flames that engulf him. Yet, his will remains unbroken, his eyes still shining with the green light of the forest spirits. He knows that this battle has gone beyond a mere show of strength, it has become a clash of worlds, of the living and the dead, of fire and nature.

And then, after the punch has finished landing, with a massive explosion of hellfire, and Legion has invented a new move for himself, the Hellfire Punch, Awakened Archdruid Malthus is defeated, but not killed, only badly injured and knocked unconscious, and he fully regenerates and regains consciousness only moments later.

And upon landing the Hellfire Punch, it causes Awakened Demon Lord Legion to Awaken yet again, under a shower of sparks and Hellfire.

The crowd is absolutely silent, the sheer power displayed by both combatants leaving them stunned. Even the vampires and werewolves of the USSB are at a loss for words, their eyes wide with amazement and fear at what they've just witnessed.

Chapter 62: Donderdraak Stomps Legion

Donderdraak then leaps into the arena excitedly, "I want to go next!" Donderdraak roars out excitedly, as the Arena begins to reset itself again magically.

And Isolde, The Vampire Queen, just rolls her eyes at Donderdraak's antics while smirking.

Legion, still basking in the aftermath of his victory, turns his fiery gaze towards the dragon. "You wish to challenge me, little one?" He asks, his tone one of amusement rather than arrogance.

"Well, only 40 Years ago, we fought over an Uno game, and I won, wouldn't you like to get your get back?" Donderdraak taunts, talking down in a baby voice towards Legion.

The crowd erupts into laughter, breaking the tension that had settled over the arena. Legion's smirk widens into a full-blown grin. He knows Donderdraak is not to be underestimated, despite the dragon's playful demeanor.

"Very well," Legion says, his deep voice carrying over the cheers. "Let's see if your scales are as thick as your skull!" He then returns to his full size, his fiery aura pulsating with excitement.

Donderdraak though, upon returning to his full size is, um, significantly bigger, being 5.5 miles long while Legion is only 666 meters tall, and that's not to mention Donderdraak's 11 mile wingspan.

Legion now feels very inferior.

The crowd gasps in awe as the dragon's true size is revealed. The arena, which had once seemed so vast, now appears almost too small for the colossal creature. Donderdraak's scales shimmer in the light of Legion's flames, a dazzling display of emerald and gold. He opens his jaws wide, revealing teeth that could crush the very bones of the earth, and roars a challenge that echoes through the night.

Donderdraak then casually flicks Legion with a single claw.

Legion, caught off-guard by the dragon's sheer size and speed, is sent tumbling through the air, his fiery aura flickering momentarily. He lands with a thud, his grin never leaving his face despite the clear disadvantage he now faces. "So, you've been holding out on me, have you?" He quips, rising to his full height once more.

Donderdraak then casually backhands Legion with one of his front limbs.

The force of the blow sends Legion careening through the air, his body a fiery comet streaking across the night sky. He crashes into the arena wall, the impact sending a shower of stone and dust down upon the spectators. The demon lord is unfazed, his skin reforming almost immediately, but he knows that the dragon's strength is not to be underestimated.

Legion then rushes forwards, and, once he gets close, Donderdraak whips him with his tail in such a way that he gets hit by and along his entire long and sinuous tail from beginning to end.

The speed and power behind the tail strike is so intense that it sends Legion spiraling through the air like a ragdoll, leaving a fiery contrail in his wake. The demon lord crashes into the ground, his body skidding across the arena floor, leaving a trail of smoldering earth. He quickly recovers, his eyes now a deep, burning crimson.

At this point all of the spectators are aware that they are just watching Legion get thrown around like a toy by Donderdraak, who is over 13x his size.

Legion's grin turns into a fierce snarl, his eyes now glowing with a rage that could burn the very fabric of reality. He charges at Donderdraak, his body now a whirlwind of hellfire. He knows that brute strength alone will not be enough to take down the dragon. He must use his wit and cunning.

Donderdraak then casually plucks Legion out of mid air between two of his claws.

The dragon holds Legion aloft, examining him as one might a particularly troublesome insect. Legion's aura flickers, but he remains focused, his mind racing with strategies to counter this new threat. The crowd holds its collective breath, unsure of what to expect next.

Donderdraak then smashes Legion into the ground and crushes him, defeating him effortlessly, "Very disappointed..." Donderdraak says before returning to his seat in the spectator stands, as Legion's fiery soul then recreates his body and he returns back to being good as new, "Anyone else, wanna challenge me?" Legion then asks.

Legion feels nearly done with battles in the Arena for today.

Chapter 63: Legion The Smoothie

Isolde, The Vampire Queen, then moves so fast she just vanishes before then appearing in the arena, "I'll do so, why not." Isolde, The Vampire Queen then says casually.

Her movements are so fast that no one can track them, not even the most trained of eyes, and before anyone can react, she's already standing in the arena. She cracks her knuckles, a smile playing on her lips as she regards Donderdraak, who has returned to his seat in the stands.

Legion then immediately tries to use the Hellfire Punch on her, but she then vanishes, and is in the air flipping behind him, before he is then hit by millions of punches she landed on him in that extremely short time, and he couldn't even react.

Legion then unleashes a massive stream of hellfire from his mouth and directly towards Isolde, and she vanishes within it.

Then some of Legion's own, impossibly spicy, lava like blood begins to fly out of some cuts Isolde's initial speedblitz attacks left on him, and into the hellfire, and then, Isolde begins to walk out the hellfire, now completely and utterly immune to and unaffected by it.

The crowd gasps, their eyes wide with astonishment. Isolde, The Vampire Queen, had just walked out of Legion's most powerful attack without so much as a scratch. She winks at the demon lord, her fangs gleaming in the flickering light of the dying flames.

Isolde then appears behind Legion, commenting "You got some spicy blood." somewhat jokingly seductively, before then Legion is slashed from all over, seemingly his body now registering her having slashed him seemingly innumerable times in that Planck Instant.

Then Isolde then lazily motioned at him and then a ranged magic slash hits him and cuts him in half, before then Isolde continues her gesturing and Legion is further cut in more halves, until Isolde's hand turns into a blur and Legion begins to simulate fruit within a blender, until Isolde stops, Legion's physical body is blended goop, and his soul is now beginning to reform his body, Isolde having defeated him in, predictably considering her infinite speed, record time.

And after Legion reforms himself, he then begins to speak as blended goop while still regenerating his body, "Okay, okay, I'm done now." Legion says as he finally exits the arena, and then everyone of the spectators also begin to trickle out, and afterwards, the three leaders of the USSB, Isolde, Legion, and Donderdraak then meet back up and reunite.

The three leaders of the USSB stand together, their auras of power mingling in a way that seems almost comical given the stark contrast of their personalities. Donderdraak is still in his giant dragon form, his tail swishing back and forth with excitement, while Isolde remains her usual poised self, a hint of mischief in her eyes.

Chapter 64: The Simulated Battle

"Wanna go and make a simulated realm where we can all three fight each other going truly all out?" Isolde then asks Legion and Donderdraak.

The two of them look at each other and nod in agreement. It's clear they've done this before, a private game between them to test their limits without causing havoc in the real world. They step into a magical circle that appears in the center of the now-desolate arena, each of them smiling in anticipation of the battle to come.

Then they enter a magical simulated realm, in which, none of them can actually truly die, which is the true main concern of a fight where all three of them go truly all out.

The simulated realm is a vast, ever-changing landscape, a canvas for their boundless power. Legion's hellfire clashes with Donderdraak's lightning breath, creating a maelstrom of fire and lightning that lights up the sky. Isolde darts through the chaos, her speed a blur as she searches for an opening.

Then Donderdraak is the first of them to go truly all out, calling upon the maximum power he can have with a charged attack and having that level of power and readiness to unleash Charged Attacks at a moments notice.

Then Isolde ascends into Vampire Goddess Isolde, where she is at her basic peak, and her power kind of breaks Existence a bit.

Then Legion is the second to go truly all out, as he becomes his true form, Demon God Legion, with fire that could burn all of Existence beyond conceptually with a single thought.

The arena, once a battleground for mere mortals, is now a playground for the gods themselves. Each clash of their titanic powers sends shockwaves rippling through the fabric of the simulation, distorting the very air around them. The ground beneath them cracks and shifts, forming and reforming as the three beings dance around each other in a deadly ballet of destruction.

Demon God Legion is so fast, he is effectively Omnipresent, giving him a massive edge against Isolde, meanwhile Donderdraak at this point has just been left completely and utterly in the dust, only able to at his current peak destroy the Infinite Universe, of which, both Vampire Goddess Isolde and Demon God Legion could do with a single thought, and with negative effort.

The two deities clash, their auras of fire and darkness swirling together, creating a tempest that could swallow galaxies. The very fabric of the simulation groans under the weight of their power, as they fight in a place where the very laws of physics are bent to their will.

Vampire Goddess Isolde, then, after Demon God Legion has caught her and is conceptually burning her, then rips her own heart out of her chest, and consumes it, the action required to activate Queen's Bloodfury normally, but in this case, it turns her into Blood Goddess Isolde, and just her transformation alone makes Reality itself unstable and viable to collapse, as then, beings from other realities can be heard repeatedly chanting, "BLOOD FOR THE BLOOD GODDESS!" louder and louder as the transformation slowly progresses until full completion.

And Blood Goddess Isolde is so fast, her speed is even beyond Omnipresence itself, and every act she takes threaten's to shatter Reality itself, and not just "reality" the concept, but, Reality, of which, all of Existence is contained within, and within it, the Infinite Spatial Dimensions, and Infinite Omniverses within each of them, and the Infinite Multiverses within each of them, and the Infinite Universes within each of them and the Infinite Nested Realms and Infinite Dimensions within each of them.

Blood Goddess Isolde is also so powerful that, whatever she decides, just, is, and thus, if she decides an attack or even opponent or such never was, then that is so.

The moment she completes her transformation, she looks at Legion, her eyes now a crimson red, and says, "You're mine, Demon." And with that, she reaches out and grabs hold of his fiery form, and then she pulls him into her, and with a burst of crimson light, Legion's flaming body is absorbed by her, and then, she is standing there, her aura now mixed with his hellfire, but still very much herself.

And then Blood Goddess Isolde sends a blast of blood magic at Donderdraak, instantly disintegrating him, before then unleashing a burst of power, destroying the Simulated Realm itself, and thus ending the private battle between the three leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings), and of course returning herself into her own real body, returning Legion into his own real body, and returning Donderdraak into his own body, all within the empty USSB Arena.

The three of them, panting and smiling at each other, know that they had just pushed themselves to the absolute brink of their abilities. The air in the arena is thick with the smell of ozone and burnt oak, a testament to the power they had just displayed. They share a look that speaks volumes, an unspoken acknowledgment of the respect and camaraderie that comes from surviving such a battle.

Legion again still feels inferior, knowing that Isolde herself could so easily by far surpass him, and that power was even greater than his "Prime".

Chapter 65: USSB Grows

(60 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2340, and Legion still is observing Zaria, who is definitely and confirmedly immortal and un-aging, considering she should by that point be 130 Years old and yet she physically looks to be only in her late 20s still, and yet, her power has grown a fitting amount considering her age and how she has continued just learning and growing in power.

Even though the Co-Leaders of the USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings), Legion, The Demon Lord, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, have all been training and growing more powerful themselves, Zaria if she ever for some reason wanted to could just solo the entire USSB as a whole all on her own rather easily.

Well, all of the mortal humans are birth by birth growing progressively baseline more powerful, and at an alarmingly exponential rate.

To the point that if, say, Reality were reset, the Mortal humans would surpass literally everything from the old Reality.

Which is a terrifying thought, but anyways, currently Legion is still observing Zaria's growth and progress with her natural dark and shadow magic from afar within the USSB Realm, while she is of course still in Whispering Sands in the Sahara Desert in the Mortal Realm.

Legion himself though, remained mostly bored over this time, feeling an emptiness that he couldn't quite figure out what it was, but whenever he observed Zaria, he felt an internal desire to protect her and help her grow and thrive and prosper, directly, like, a father, oddly enough, and he felt that emptiness to an extent filled, but couldn't understand why himself...

Chapter 66: Zaria, of Whispering Sand

(30 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2370...

(POV Switches to following that of Zaria)

Zaria looks at herself, taking in a deep breath of that good old overly magical Whispering Sand air, a woman, no, a girl, perpetually in her 20s, in her physical prime, even though by now she is 160, either due to her usage of dark and shadow magic or her descending from The Shadow God, Alpha, who was also known to not age past his physical prime. As she stares up at the night sky, so pitch black, so peaceful, the embodiment of what the Dark truly is and represents, that calm chaos, and truest peace.

Zaria can see a true reflection of herself in it. Truly only desiring peace, exactly what her parents would have wanted, but conflicted with the bits of light that people have forced upon and into her, despite it truly being an all consuming and ruining plague.

Zaria still remembers that sight of her parents being burnt alive, that image has ironically been burned into her mind. After all, fire is the ultimate representation of the Light, all consuming, utterly selfish, not stopping until it has either consumed all there is to consume or is extinguished.

Zaria, though, remembers him, Legion, The Demon Lord, for being a demon, a being of pure Light, he is truly nice, he clearly desires chaos, of all kinds, including that truly calm kind under the Dark. Zaria just lies down on her back and looks up at the night sky, and up at the moon, which one day just vanished and got replaced with a new look alike, Zaria just internally assumes that either some aliens or maybe even Legion did so.

Either way, Zaria then gets up, and gets back to training her dark and shadow magic, as she then brings out her now 20 Perpetually Manifested Tendrils of Darkness, and that isn't even all of them, any more would just be cluttered and look ugly and awful in her eyes. Also it would take up way too much space.

The tendrils move as if alive, as if they had a mind of their own, which they do, but not quite, they're just a part of her own, and she can feel everything they do and see everything they do, they're like extensions of herself.

Zaria summons her staff of shadow and dark, essentially just a creation of hers, and from it shoots a large beam of pure black dark and shadow magic into the night sky, and she swore that afterwards she saw a star disappear from where she fired it at.

Her power had grown so much over the centuries, that she could manipulate the very fabric of reality to such a degree, it was as if she had a cheat code to existence itself, and she hadn't even tapped into a fraction of her true power.

Ever since Legion had found her as that poor, scared, in danger 10 year old orphan, she had deep down knew that she had effectively infinite potential, enough to likely far outstrip even that of The Shadow God, Alpha, who at his Full Potential flew at 40 octo-decilion times the Speed of Light, and not the Refraction Speed, which is Mach 874,030, but the Speed of Light, which is truly infinite in speed and beings that surpass it can by choice cross between the Infinite Spatial Dimensions of the Infinite Universe they exist within, let alone the Infinitely more complex Realms nested among them.

Zaria shakes her head to snap herself out of her small Cosmology Trance, she'd been doing way too much reading and research on Cosmology in her spare time than is in any way normal. But, then again, she had never been in any way truly normal anyways. So why try and fake being normal now, at 160 Years old, and yet looking an 8th of that.

The tendrils of darkness that surrounded her retract back into her, and she smiles to herself, feeling the power surge through her, feeling like she could take over the world if she so desired. But she didn't, she had never wanted power for power's sake, she just wanted peace, and she had hoped that by mastering this power she would be able to protect those she cares about, like the mortal humans who she had come to care for deeply over her centuries of life.

And yeah, she'd heard of the prophecy of a child born of darkness coming to rule the world, and how it could oh so easily be her, but, well, she feels no desire to rule or to lead, she would rather be led by said person. And considering she had heard of, and even once met the new Alpha, who she instantly deduced that the rumors of being the reincarnation of The Shadow God/The Dark Emperor were true, she's oddly perceptive and good at figuring stuff out like that. Of course, when she and this new Alpha met, they had a very nice and natural conversation, and even consider each other as friends nowadays.

Zaria can tell, Alpha is, will be, and always was meant to be, "The Chosen One" of that prophecy, even since long before the prophecy was made, he was meant to be the one to lead the world to true peace, under darkness. He even once admitted his idea to her, to gain immense power, and, once he had enough, to wipe out this current Reality, saving everyone from it by storing them into a Realm. Then creating a Reality of true peace, with literally no war, segregation, suffering, or pain, then move all of those of the old Reality, into this Reality of peace, this New Reality.

Zaria, of course, supported his plan and told him, well, more power to him. Even if "traditional morals" would argue with him, said morals are lies anyways, and, well, traditional morals barely even apply. It's war, and always has been underneath the surface, between the Light and the Dark.

And so she decided, she would continue her own path of growth and learning, to be ready to support Alpha, when he made his move, or to be ready if she had to protect her own little slice of peace in the ever-growing chaos of the world.

Chapter 67: The Shadow Goddess

(The next morning)
