Zaria was just walking through the Whispering Sands marketplace, getting her normal suspicious stares, ever since more people arrived there and were welcomed there, the Light has begun to spread its hold even over Whispering Sands, but Zaria has been able to subtly, through her presence and aura alone, keep it subdued and mostly away and under the surface.

The stalls were bustling with activity, traders shouting out their wares, and the smell of exotic spices wafted through the air. She passed by a stall selling ancient tomes and scrolls, and her eyes caught a glimpse of something that made her pause. A tome titled "The Shadow God's Legacy" was displayed prominently among the others, its cover adorned with intricate, shadowy designs.

Zaria then entered the store, accompanied by the chime of a bell from her entrance.

The merchant, a wizened old man with eyes that had seen centuries of secrets, looked up from his dusty tomes. Recognizing the power that emanated from her, he offered a slight bow, his hands trembling with respect and fear. "Welcome, my lady," he murmured, his voice like sandpaper on ancient parchment.

"Hello, nice to see you, I'd like to purchase that tome there, the one titled "The Shadow God's Legacy", both for practical and personal purposes.

The merchant's eyes widened, and he studied her for a moment, as if weighing whether she was worthy of such knowledge. Then, with a slow nod, he handed her the tome, the leather-bound cover feeling surprisingly warm to the touch. "I trust you know the significance of what you hold," he said gravely.

"Of course, the title of it would actually, fun fact, apply to me, he was my ancestor, yeah, neat right?" Zaria casually comments while chuckling warmly and kindly, and it catches the attention of everyone nearby.

The merchant's eyes bulged out of his head, and his trembling grew even more intense, if that was possible, "Your ancestor?! The Shadow God himself?!" He was about to fall over in shock, but he managed to keep his composure.

"Yeah, you couldn't tell from my insane proficiency with dark and shadow magic and easy usage of so many Perpetually Manifested Tendrils of Darkness?" Zaria jokingly asked while chuckling, as she then gave the man more than enough Whispering Sands currency to pay for her purchase, and continuing to warmly and kindly chuckle as she began to walk away.

The merchant just nodded his head, absolutely speechless, his mind racing with the implications of what he had just heard, and who he had just served. He had heard whispers of a child born of darkness that could potentially surpass even The Shadow God, but he never thought he would actually meet her, let alone serve her.

Then, suddenly, out of nowhere, a nearby person then suddenly pulled out a Knife of Light and slashed Zaria across the face with it, from right above her left eyebrow, across her face, and down to the right side of her jaw.

Zaria then reached up at her face, and looked down at the blood now staining said hand, her own blood, as she then looked up at her assailant, of course one of those Light Fanatics, and she noticed there were plenty of others nearby. So she just picked up the book, ironically enough with the hand covered in her own blood, descended from that of the titular focus of the book, and attempted to just walk away from the situation.

But the Light Fanatic, seemingly on a religious fervor, yells after her, "You're a monster, a blight on this world! The Darkness must be cleansed!"

And the other Light Fanatics around her, which then form a barrier around her, begin to chant that as well, slowly boxing her in.

Zaria can't help but have rapid flashbacks to watching her parents execution...

(Flash back to the Year 2220 from the perspective of a 10 Year Old Zaria)

Zaria could do nothing but watch, as the maniacs dragged away her parents, and she followed as fast as her legs could carry her, but, all she arrived to, was her parents over a bright, golden fire of light, slowly being burnt alive, while being whipped with Whips of Light, and screamed at and insulted. "You are monsters, a blight on this world! The Darkness must be cleansed! And YOU are a key part of this! And must be cleansed immediately!" the people screamed, insulted, and even chanted at Zaria's poor innocent parents as they were whipped with Light and slowly cooked alive. All for being descended from The Shadow God, a being that created a utopia of darkness for outcasts, but, that wasn't how history was recorded by the Light.

Zaria couldn't do anything but watch, after all, what could she do, she couldn't even slightly control her dark and shadow magic, which she in that moment, and ever since, cursed herself for.

Then, an hour later, the execution was completed, with all of the people clearing out, leaving the mostly charred bodies of Zaria's poor parents behind on the ground like trash, too worthless to even bury, and Zaria then slowly began to approach the lifeless bodies of her parents, and, she can swear her mother is, smiling... and Zaria remembers that while watching, Zaria noticed that her mother saw her and began to smile warmly and comfortingly at her, her last expression, her last thoughts...

(Flash back forwards to the Year 2370)

Zaria is now grabbing at both the side of her head and the slash from top left to bottom right of her face. As she now begins to cry profusely, plenty of her salty tears going straight into her wound.

But, she doesn't fight back, she just stands there, crying, the light from the Knife of Light having burned her, and not just physically, but emotionally and mentally too, it's like the knife had sliced into her very soul, and she couldn't do anything about it.

"leave me alone..." Zaria said weakly to start.

The Light Fanatics, driven by their fervor, closed in tighter, their eyes gleaming with a mix of righteousness and madness. One of them, a man with a scarred visage, raised his hand, and a beam of light shot forth, aimed directly at Zaria's chest.

"Leave me alone." Zaria said again, with slightly more energy and power this time, somehow.

But the Light Fanatics did not relent, their chants of purification growing louder. They had not seen the horrors of war, had not felt the pain of loss, had not lived in the shadow of the very darkness that Zaria had made into her home. They saw only the fear and the propaganda that the Light had fed them.

"Leave me alone!" Zaria managed to yell out, dark and shadow magic beginning to harshly and intensely spark from her, as if about to explode.

The Light Fanatics took a step back, their chanting faltering as the very air grew colder and the shadows grew darker around her. They had never felt such a powerful presence of the Dark before, not even from their own leaders and their holy weapons.

"LEAVE ME ALONE!" Zaria screamed out as, in a massive sparking explosion of dark and shadow magic from within her, like a metaphorical nuke, she truly Awakened.

Zaria then stood up, as her body itself began to transform into consisting of Tendrils of Darkness, and her amethyst eyes began to permanently glow.

As she then twists her body into looking much darker, while still definitely looking human, but one as twisted physically as she has been emotionally and mentally by these Fanatics of the awful and truly pure evil Light.

Zaria's body though, shifts back to mostly that of herself as she is the same, but she consists of the Tendrils of Darkness themselves, and yet, she isn't a monster and never was, those of the Light always were.

"LEAVE ME ALOOOOOOONE!!!" Zaria then screamed out again, as then out came a pulse of pure dark and shadow magic from her body, representing how she has truly Awakened.

And unknowingly to her, this was almost exactly like when her own ancestor, Alpha, became The Shadow God, 1,651 Years prior, and yet, Zaria, the quick learner and analyst she is, realizes this.

"You can now call me The Shadow Goddess, Shadow Goddess Zaria, descendent and heiress of The Shadow God, Alpha, and much like him, I will bring justice against you light obsessed pricks!" Shadow Goddess Zaria then stated and screamed out.

The Light Fanatics, their eyes wide with terror, realized too late the magnitude of their mistake. The power that now stood before them was not one to be trifled with, not even by the mightiest of their own order. They had provoked a sleeping giant, one that was now fully Awakened.

Shadow Goddess Zaria then made them realize that immediately, as she then perception blitzed the Light Fanatic that slashed across her face, of which, there is now a scar, and then slammed him into the ground holding his face with her hand, then wrapping Tendrils of Darkness also around his head, and with her hand and Tendrils of Darkness, crushing his head to a pulp against the ground, slowly and painfully, exactly as he deserves, considering he tried to kill her completely unprovoked first.

The other Light Fanatics, shocked and horrified by the sudden display of power, froze in place, their chants of purification silenced. They had never seen such a brutal and efficient use of dark magic.

"Don't think you all just get off scot-free either." Shadow Goddess Zaria said coldly and darkly before then disappearing from sight, and then, in the next instant she appears, she had pierced a Tendril of Darkness through the brains of every single Light Fanatic, killing them all, but not instantly, making sure to avoid vital parts of the brain, to leave them bleed out, which happens rather quickly, mostly due to their very well deserved fear.

The marketplace had gone silent, the only sounds were the last gasps of life from the dying fanatics, and the sound of Zaria's own heart, which was beating much faster than usual due to the sheer anger she felt.

Shadow Goddess Zaria then, picked up "The Shadow God's Legacy" book, and put it away, before then beginning to clean up the mess left behind, along with cleaning up herself, until the marketplace looked just as it had before, before then returning to her normal, mostly cheery self.

Shadow Goddess Zaria then turned towards the merchant she bought the book from and gave double what she originally paid for it (which was already double the cost), to the merchant, "This is for the trouble I unintentionally caused there, sorry about that, hope this accounts for it." Shadow Goddess Zaria says kindly, softly, and warmly.

The merchant took the additional currency with trembling hands, and could only nod in response, his eyes unable to leave the sight of the newly Awakened Shadow Goddess, his mind racing with what he just saw, and the fear and awe that she had just shown the true power of The Dark.

Chapter 68: Breaking News

Shadow Goddess Zaria then returned to her own humble yet deluxe little home (she was gifted it, and actually requested it be smaller, previously it was a mansion, but she wanted to be more humble, and thus, it was made smaller, but she couldn't negotiate away it being so deluxe, no matter how hard she had tried) and she took a shower before then getting into her bed and beginning to relax, and turning on the Magic TV in her bedroom, to see what's on.

The news came on, and the news reporter was talking about the recent event at the marketplace, "Breaking news, an incident at the Whispering Sands marketplace today, involving a young girl, who's been confirmed to be the descendant of the Shadow God, Alpha, has Awakened and killed multiple Light Fanatics. It appears she is now going by the name of The Shadow Goddess, Zaria. More on this story as it develops..."

"This just in, we got this recording of the event, and it paints the story quite the opposite you had probably expected!" the news reporter said in shock, as it then played a video that someone clearly captured on their phone. And it shows Zaria shopping, not a big surprise, people like to record her doing just about anything. And the video shows her paying double the price for the book, an incredibly generous and kind thing to do. Then it shows the first Light Fanatic suddenly assaulting Zaria and slashing her across her face, and beginning to verbally abuse her, then others nearby joining it, and her having what could best be described as a PTSD attack. Then her weakly just asking them to leave her alone, with increasing intensity, as they just continue to refuse, and continue to attack her. Until Zaria then Awakens, becoming The Shadow Goddess, and even do her own transformation to, as she had intended to back then, show that she wasn't a monster, she was just a person. Before she then, clearly in self defense, kills the Light Fanatic, before then doing so to the rest. Before then calming down, cleaning herself off of the blood all over her, both of others and her own, before then respectfully cleaning up and disposing of the bodies and the blood all over the area. Before then approaching the merchant and paying him further double what she already paid him earlier to make up for any damages and disturbance and chaos that she caused. The Recording then ends, cutting to the news reporter with their eyes wide and their jaw dropped at what they just witnessed.

"Wow, it's clear to see now that the Light Fanatics are truly the monsters here, not the girl they so viciously attacked," the news reporter exclaimed as the video concluded. The public's perception shifted, the whispers of a gentle girl with dark magic turning into a rallying cry for justice.

"This just in, AGAIN, riots all across Whispering Sands are calling for any and all Light Fanatics to be banished and exiled from the city, considering it's common correct knowledge that all of them follow the same belief and would thus do the same thing again if given the chance! Multiple petitions asking for such are getting signed by literally everyone in the city! This has gotta be a big blow to the Light, them losing the credibility of the hidden city of magic in the Sahara Desert!" the New Reporter announces in shock.

Shadow Goddess Zaria, watching the news, couldn't believe her eyes. The people she had once called neighbors and friends were now demanding justice, not just for her, but for all those who were vilified by the Light. The video had spread like wildfire, and the public's anger was palpable.

The video even apparently spread outside of Whispering Sands, and caused global outrage, which even threatened shattering of the True Sacred Relic which maintains forced peace, but does shatter all of it's barriers and defenses, leaving it defenseless to anyone who might want to shatter it in the future.

The Light Fanatics had gone too far, and now, the entire world was watching. Governments and leaders from every corner of the globe were forced to take notice of the injustice that had occurred.

Of which, there weren't many of, there were very few separate nations outside of Island Countries, and a handful in each of the continents, and, as is usual, the isolationist Phoenix Empire of Blazing Sun has remained completely neutral on the matter.

And obviously, their long destroyed rival Empire of Frostspire is unable to comment on it due to being destroyed since the Year 999, 1,371 Years ago.

But many small countries did split off of the Empire of Frostspire, old lands of the Empire of Blazing Sun at their peak before rebranding as the Phoenix Empire of Blazing Sun, and even of the Empire of Shadow once ruled by The Shadow God, Alpha, the ancestor of Shadow Goddess Zaria, before his flight off into space. before returning 1,222 Years later in the Year 2126, before being seemingly vanquished by the God Emperors, Marcus Aurelius and his own descendant Aldric, before they had ascended beyond the Mortal/Physical Realm/Plane, leaving behind the True Sacred Relic to hold the forced peace in the Year 2130. And Alpha is thought to have reincarnated into the one modernly also known as Alpha, who was born the same Year the old Alpha was seemingly vanquished.

Alpha, the reincarnation of his former self, saw the chaos and upheaval and rioting as perfect set up, and thus, plans to finally initiate his plan in a matter of decades, to let it fester for a bit longer.

Meanwhile, back with Shadow Goddess Zaria herself, back in her bedroom, relaxing, watching the news covering most of all of this going down.

Shadow Goddess Zaria's thoughts were racing as she watched the riots and chaos unfold on the Magic TV. The anger and pain of her past now had a focus, a clear enemy to channel her power towards. She knew that she couldn't just sit back and watch the world burn because of the ignorance and cruelty of the Light Fanatics.

Shadow Goddess Zaria though, in truth, kept that down, as she just continued to go through her normal life, of course, amongst the massive amount of paparazzi and interviews she is hounded by.

But she didn't let it get to her, she knew she had a mission, a purpose, and she wasn't going to let the fame get to her head.

Chapter 69: USSB Reaction

(POV switches finally back to Legion, The Demon Lord, in the USSB Realm)

The USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings) Co-Leaders, Legion, The Demon Lord, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, were just holding a casual meeting, when every single USSB Spy in the Mortal Realm pretty much at once reports on the events transpiring, including the merchant that was in the Whispering Sands, who was a USSB Spy in disguise, who reports directly on the immediate events involving The Shadow Goddess, Zaria.

The reports flooded in, each one more shocking than the last. The Awakening of the Shadow Goddess had not only brought chaos to the city but had sparked a global movement against the Light.

Legion can't help but feel oddly paternal pride towards Zaria, having now begun to truly tap into the potential he saw within her when he saved her as a 10 Year old orphan back in the Year 2220, even he could have never planned for her to end up pulling off something like this.

Legion, while he doesn't recognize it, feels like an adoptive father watching their adopted child succeed, not just like, on a simple level, but on a global celebrity level scale.

The other USSB Leaders looked at each other in astonishment as they took in the news. The balance of power had shifted, and it was clear that the Light Fanatics had underestimated the strength of the Dark.

Legion could feel that conflict would arrive in only a few decades, and then, their opportunity to raid the ruins of Earthly Mortal Civilizations would arrive.

(POV Switches back to Shadow Goddess Zaria)

Zaria can feel that she has become more than human, on top of, like all people born after the Year 2129, being part Neohuman, she has become on top of that truly beyond human, beyond mortal, beyond deity...

(POV Switches back to Legion)

The meeting of the USSB Co-Leaders, Legion, The Demon Lord, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, continues.

Legion, with a knowing smile, turns to Isolde, "It seems our little bird has spread her wings," he says, his deep voice resonating through the chamber.

Isolde is just confused, "Why do you refer to her like that." Isolde asks.

"Even I don't know, it just, felt right to me, then again, just MY little bird feels like it would have felt even more right, and I have no clue why." Legion explains honestly.

Isolde nods, understanding the sentiment. Donderdraak, the ever-stoic and calm one, speaks up, "Regardless, we must prepare for the storm that is to come. The Shadow Goddess's Awakening will not go unnoticed by those who wish to control or destroy her."

Legion bursts out laughing for an unknown reason, confusing Donderdraak and Isolde.

"What is it?" Isolde asks.

"You really think that anything exists that COULD!? If she wanted to, she could wipe out of civilization without lifting a finger! Nothing exists that COULD control or destroy her, even if any were ballsy enough to try!" Legion says, still cackling.

Isolde, slightly concerned, "What do you mean?"

"I have observed her for a long time, as you both know, and even just 3 decades ago, if she wanted to and knew of the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings she could singlehandedly wipe us all out, and that was before 3 decades of training and growth, an Awakening clearly, and a transformation into that kind of state she is in." Legion explains, still laughing.

Isolde, visibly nervous now, "But what if she isn't as benevolent as we hope she is, what if she decides to use her power for evil?"

"You saw the recordings they showed on the News, and unlike you two, I have actually talked to her, and even engaged in the ultimate connecting action with her, a spar, I know her to the deepest depths of her soul, and she is completely opposite of someone like me, of a being of the Light, of Pure Malice and Evil, she doesn't have an ounce of evil within her, she is a being of the Dark, of Pure Peace and Good." Legion further explains.

Isolde nods, but still looks concerned, "But what if she is corrupted by power, by the Dark itself, what then?"

"You clearly have never listened to me, if you still thing that the Dark is a corrupting force, no, that would be the Light, the Dark is a force of true pure good, and, she has always had great power, but lacked control over it, until recently, her form still resembling a human despite consisting of Tendrils of Darkness, proves she is incorruptible and Truly and Purely Good." Legion further explains.

The room goes quiet for a moment, as the gravity of Legion's words sinks in. The Vampire Queen, Isolde, nods thoughtfully, "Then we must do everything in our power to guide and protect her. The world is not ready for a being of such power to be left to the whims of fate."

"It wouldn't be nearly the first time this has occurred, and yet she is the most powerful and truly pure to ever exist, and she is Truly Pure. To any that seek to oppose her, that is the most terrifying thing possible, a truly pure person, one who is sure in that what she is doing is right, and only unsure, in that she WANTS to do so. For only she is powerful to decide what she does, all that exists are her likes and dislikes." Legion further explains.

The room nods in understanding, knowing that their role in this was not to control, but to guide and protect. Donderdraak speaks up, "We must ensure that the USSB is prepared for any eventuality. We will need to bolster our defenses and alliances, especially with the mortal world now in turmoil."

"No, we simply wait, for in 3 decades, conflict will truly return, and then, once the opportunity presents itself, we take it, and raid the ruins of mortal civilization on Earth once they ascend to the stars to battle amongst them." Legion says, with utter finality in his voice.

Isolde and Donderdraak nod in agreement, understanding the patience that their kind is known for. The USSB had always been preparing for the time when the mortal realm would be weakened by their own conflicts, and that time was approaching swiftly.

Chapter 70: Legion and Zaria

(15 Years Later)

The Year is 2385, and Legion, The Demon Lord, decides to pay Zaria, The Shadow Goddess, aka, Shadow Goddess Zaria, a nice little visit again, just to be friendly.

Fifteen years have passed since the Awakening of the Shadow Goddess, and the world had indeed changed. The Light Fanatics had been driven into the shadows, their influence waning as the people of the world turned towards a new beacon of hope: Zaria. Her legend had grown, and she had become a symbol of justice and peace.

But Zaria had directed them towards Alpha (The reincarnation, not the original), stating he had the same goals and ideals as her, but better, and with the drive and intention to directly act towards them, and thus, a new City was founded of the shadows and darkness, named Shadowstein, led by Alpha as their Emperor, as Shade Emperor Alpha.

Shadowstein grew in power and influence, as the Dark and Neohumans alike flocked to it's banner, seeking refuge from the tyrannical grip of the Light Fanatics. The city grew into a bastion of technology and magic, a fusion that the world had never seen before.

Meanwhile, Legion, The Demon Lord decided to pay Zaria a visit in her deluxe humble home in Whispering Sands, where she still lives, for old times sake, after all, they had last seen each other 155 Years earlier, and Zaria was, despite still being physically in her 20, 175 Years old at that point, so, they hadn't seen each other in quite a while.

The door opens and the two lock eyes, the warmth of their reunion palpable. Zaria, now a legendary figure, stands tall and proud, her eyes glowing with the power of the Darkness that she wields so gracefully.

ShadowGoddess Zaria then immediately rushed forwards and tackle hugged Legion, knocking him over.

Legion, despite all of the time that had passed, can't help but still see Zaria as that 10 Year old orphan with dark and shadow magic that he had randomly decided to save in the Middle East and lead to Whispering Sands, back in the Year 2220.

"Hey there, long time no see!" Zaria exclaims, her voice filled with genuine joy as she pulls away from the embrace.

"The same for me, wow, you have grown, and I must admit, if we had a spar right now, I would be defeated in an instant, want to quickly test that? For old times sake?" Legion asks.

Shadow Goddess Zaria laughs, "Maybe another time, I'm not sure you're ready for this power," she says playfully, her eyes twinkling with mischief.

"Hey, I want to at least see and at least slightly test that power, how about it? Just for old times sake?" Legion then asks again.

Shadow Goddess Zaria, still smiling, nods, "Alright, but remember, I've had over a century of practice and experience, I might go easy on you."

"I won't go easy on you." Legion then says instantly, before then going straight into his Berserker State, Awakening, remaining condensed, and even preparing a Hellfire Punch, going truly all out.

And... without even trying, Shadow Goddess Zaria immediately perception blitzes him and grabs him by the face and smashes him into the ground, immediately knocking him out of his Berserker State, out of using Hellfire Punch, and just knocking his Awakened power into his Base State, all in literally zero seconds.

Shadow Goddess Zaria stands over Legion, smiling down at him, "It's good to see you too."

"Wow, just as I expected, and I went ALL out, held back literally nothing, and yet you defeated me faster than even I could perceive without even trying, just as I had expected of you." Legion then casually responded.

Shadow Goddess Zaria offered her hand to help Legion up, her smile never fading. "You've always had faith in me, haven't you?"

"Of course, ever since I first saved you, 165 Years ago now, if I'm not mistaken." Legion casually responds.

Shadow Goddess Zaria nods, "I know, and I'm grateful, but I've come so far since then, I've had to, with the world the way it is now."

"Of course, I have noticed, and the world has always been this way, even since it's creation, I would know, I have watched it, and was in the distant past part of the problem itself, after all, I'm opposite to you, while you are the Truest and Purest Good, I am the Truest and Purest Evil, you of Pure Darkness, me of Pure Light, me a demon, a devil, and thusly, by those flawed ideas, you, an angel." Legion responds.

Shadow Goddess Zaria, still smiling, nods in understanding. "The world is indeed a complex place, and I've had to adapt, to become something more, something that can truly bring peace."

"I have had to do so from time to time as well." Legion responds.

"Oh, with that USSB, which, if I'm not mistaken, means Underground Society for Supernatural Beings, you are part of, correct?" Zaria then responds and asks, leaving Legion flabbergasted.

Legion's eyes widened, "How do you know of the USSB? That is not common knowledge, even for someone of your stature!"

"Way back when after our spar, I sneakily followed you and watched you open the hole in the fabric of reality, and saw through it the name "USSB" and then, I pieced together that you must be a part of it, then took into account that you mentioned Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, who both sound like leader supernatural beings, and I then deduced that USSB must stand for "Underground Society of Supernatural Beings" and from there, figured out that it must be an entire hidden other Realm led by you and the other two, populated by supernatural beings, am I right?" Zaria casually explains.

"... By Erebus's name you're smarter than me..." Legion responds, just completely shocked to confusion and confused into shock.

Shadow Goddess Zaria laughs, "Well, I have had a lot of time to learn and observe, and I have always been curious, I just never had the opportunity to ask you about it."

"I mean, from literally just 4 letters and some subtle information from me you deduced the name, what it is, the structure of it, and the purpose of it, from pretty much nothing. You are a prodigy in more than just dark and shadow magic apparently, because I have manipulated entire nations in the past, and yet, even I likely couldn't deduce that, let alone THAT easily." Legion responds, now slightly chuckling.

Shadow Goddess Zaria smirks, "Well, I do have my moments of cleverness."

"Oh, also, were you at the Battle of Storm Dragon Mountain back in 2126?" Shadow Goddess Zaria randomly asked.

"Yes? Why?" Legion then responds in confusion.

"In the Whispering Sands school long ago they covered it and I got really interested in it, and after our previous meeting way back then, and afterwards, I began to deduce that The Demon Lord involved in the battle must have been you, and thus, The Vampire Queen, was the "Isolde, The Vampire Queen" that you mentioned, and The Storm Dragon, must have been the "Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan" that you also mentioned, and thus, The Last Champion was "The Last Champion, Lothar" that you also mentioned, is all of that right?" Zaria responds and asks.

"Astoundingly so, yes, that is correct, you are truly a prodigy of perception and deduction, and now, a Goddess of the Shadow and Darkness, I am proud of the child I once knew, now standing before me." Legion says, with a smile that is genuine and filled with pride.

"Then who was The Shadow King, was he "Mordecai" the old Necromancer of the Empire of Sanctity, and speaking of which, were you the "Massive Demon" summoned by said necromancer and defeated during the Battle of Vatican City?" Zaria asks curiously.

"Yes and... yes..." Legion responds, embarrassedly for the second yes.

"That is so cool! What else were you involved in?" Zaria then asks.

"Well, I was "Mr. Black" during The Dragon's Heart Wars from 1843-1845." Legion then responds.

"That is so cool!" Shadow Goddess Zaria says, now slightly fangirling.

"I suppose it is, but, we are not here to talk about me, we are here to talk about you, and your City of Shadow, Shadowstein." Legion says, steering the conversation back to the more important matters at hand.

"Oh, don't know where you heard that, that's Alpha's city to rule, not mine, me and him both agreed on that, and I mean the Alpha born back in 2130, not the one taken out in 2130, I'm talking about his reincarnation." Shadow Goddess Zaria responds.

"Ah, the young one with the same name, I had heard rumors of his existence," Legion says, his tone intrigued.

"Well, he also is dealing with some Lich King named Geralt and made him his advisor, and apparently has been for give or take my age." Zaria casually comments.

"Oh, so that's what Geralt has been up to all this time and why he keeps rejecting to join the Underground Society of Supernatural Beings, that explains a lot actually." Legion also comments.

Shadow Goddess Zaria nods, "Yeah, Alpha is quite the character, he is a lot like you in his own way, except for the whole 'being a demon' part of course."

"I doubt he is truly like me, I heard he is more like you, a prodigy of darkness, Pure-hearted, Pure Good, seeking peace, definitely not me, a being of the Light that seeks chaos, even if that is against the order that the Light itself seeks, also, I'm pure evil, he's pure good, you and him might as well, be indirect identical twins with how similar you both are to each other." Legion then responds.

"You're not all chaos and destruction, Legion," Zaria says, placing a hand on his shoulder. "You have your moments of compassion and understanding, even if they're hidden behind that demonic exterior."

Legion feels as if his very soul is being probed by Zaria, and quickly stops that happening, to stop her from learning of his hidden even from himself paternal feelings in relation to her.

The room goes quiet for a moment before Legion laughs it off, "You give me too much credit, I assure you, I'm just a demon with a sense of humor and a knack for strategy."

"No, I can tell you are so much more, maybe at one point you were, but you aren't just that anymore, you aren't Pure Evil either anymore, I can tell, even if you can't." Zaria responds.

"You're too kind, Zaria, but let's not get ahead of ourselves, I still have a long way to go before I could claim such a title," Legion says, his eyes holding a hint of sadness and self-reflection.

"Anyways, it's been fun, but, you should probably return to that USSB thing now, I hope to see you again eventually though." Zaria then says.

"Yes, I must, the world of the supernatural is ever changing, and we must be ready to adapt to it's whims. I will surely visit again when the time allows for it, I'd love to see what other mysteries and secrets you've uncovered." Legion responds, a hint of curiosity in his eyes.

"Bye bye!" Zaria says, sounding cheery and excited as she waves goodbye.

Legion nods, "See you soon," he says before opening a portal back to the USSB headquarters.

Legion returns back to the USSB headquarters, and can feel that they will meet again soon enough, but still, probably not until conflict truly returns, and even then, not for a good bit…

Chapter 71: Sacred Peace Shattering

(15 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 2400, and while the trio of USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings) Co-Leaders, Legion, The Demon Lord, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, are just counting their vault, Donderdraak then suddenly perks up and begins speaking, "IT, has happened..." Donderdraak says eerily.

Isolde and Legion look at each other in confusion before turning to him, "What has happened?" They both ask in unison.

Legion is confused, considering they were just counting the USSB Vault, immeasurable, like always.

"The True Sacred Relic... has been shattered, the forced peace which has existed for 270 Years, has come to an end." Donderdraak then explains.

The room grows cold with the weight of his words. The vault, filled with the treasures of the ages, seems to fade into the background as the gravity of the situation sets in. Isolde's hand clenches around the Sword of Dracula, her vampiric instincts on high alert. Legion's eyes glow a piercing blue, the power of the ice within him rising in response to the sudden tension.

"Conflict will soon come, but, I can sense, that it will not occur on Earth, but, among the cosmos themselves." Donderdraak further adds on.

The two leaders exchange a knowing glance. They understand that the balance they had fought so hard to maintain is now at risk of being disrupted. The peace that had held for centuries was a fragile thing, and the shattering of the True Sacred Relic could only mean one thing: War was on the horizon, and it would be on a scale they had never seen before.

"We should calm, there is a very high chance the conflict will never affect the USSB, due to the conflict occurring outside of Earth, which in truth presents us with a phenomenal opportunity when that comes to acquire all of the wealth on Earth short of that of the Kingdom of Lionesse and the Phoenix Empire of Blazing Sun." Donderdraak says, presenting the likely benefit to the USSB.

Isolde nods, stroking the hilt of the Sword of Dracula, "True, we must be prepared for any outcome. We've built an empire here, we must ensure its survival, and perhaps, expand it further."

"Of course, but calm yourself, we should take the time to prepare, which I can sense should be about 5 years, and, once Earth has been left, we can start to scavenge and raid everything left behind." Donderdraak says, having since become the de-facto main central leader of the USSB, even amongst Legion and Isolde.

Mostly due to Donderdraak having the most raw base power and the most drive among them.

The room falls silent as the gravity of the situation sinks in. The shattering of the True Sacred Relic is not just a war between empires, but a war that could potentially reshape the very fabric of existence. Yet, amidst the looming chaos, the USSB remained steadfast in their mission to protect the mythical creatures and amass wealth.

Legion can sense that the opportunity is approaching, and the true final war between the Dark and the Light to determine the fate of Reality itself is approaching alongside it.

Chapter 72: USSB Takes Advantage

(5 YEARS LATER)

Two empires emerge from the chaos and develop space travel technology, and go up and into space, the opportunity Donderdraak, Legion, and Isolde were waiting for to make their move and begin to scavenge and raid all of the treasure left behind all around the Earth short of the Kingdom of Lionesse and Phoenix Empire of Blazing Sun.

The Earth is in a state of turmoil as the two spacefaring empires clash in the heavens. Cities are abandoned, and the once bustling lands are now a playground for the mythical creatures that roam freely. The USSB, now stronger and more unified than ever, sets its sights on the treasures of the forgotten world, the spoils of war left untouched.

Donderdraak even locates the old Sacred Relic Fragment he used to own, the one that enhances power and attracts treasure, and that comes massively in handy for the USSB.

The trio of USSB (Underground Society of Supernatural Beings) Co-Leaders, Legion, The Demon Lord, Isolde, The Vampire Queen, and Donderdraak, The Storm Leviathan, now seasoned treasure hunters, navigate the abandoned landscapes with ease. The fragment, glowing with an ethereal light, pulls them toward hidden caches and ancient vaults filled with gold, jewels, and artifacts of untold power. They move swiftly and silently, like shadows in the night, taking what they want without leaving a trace.

Donderdraak, predicting he won't be able to keep the Sacred Relic Fragment forever, imprints its treasure attracting power into himself.

As they venture deeper into the deserted lands, the fragment's power within Donderdraak grows stronger. It's as if the very earth itself is guiding him to the most hidden and valuable treasures. His eyes begin to glow with a faint golden hue, and every step feels lighter, as if the weight of his greed has been transformed into boundless energy.

Donderdraak's power eventually grows to being capable of wiping out a Solar System, and even an entire Galaxy with his Charged Attack if he so wanted to, and that's just the area of effect of it, the actual power of the blast is Infinite Dimensional at the very least.

The USSB's wealth and power grow exponentially as they claim treasure after treasure, their hoard becoming the stuff of legend. Yet, with each victory, Donderdraak's hunger for more grows stronger. His eyes, once a gentle gold, now burn with an insatiable desire for wealth and power.

Eventually though, some being arrives onto Earth, unleashes Tendrils of darkness, retrieving all Sacred Relic Fragments and associated Sacred Artifacts, including Donderdraak's, and brings them all to it, before then fusing them all together, repairing the Sacred Relic, and then corrupting it, effectively now having the Dark Empire have full control over the Planet Earth, as opposed to half of it, as they had previously had, but this barely affects the USSB, considering Planet Earth is mostly left alone still.

The being is Shadow Emperor Alpha, the Emperor of the Empire of Shadowstein and reincarnation of The Dark Emperor, but seemingly, for him, he seems to not currently have any more want to do with Earth, let alone the USSB.

The corrupted Sacred Relic, now known as the Dark Sacred Relic, pulses with a malevolent energy that echoes across the planet. Yet, amidst the chaos, the USSB's hidden fortress remains untouched. Donderdraak, his eyes now dimmer, watches the events unfold from afar, his thoughts racing.

Legion can tell that this was that very Alpha, and that the reincarnation essentially just brought back that very same Alpha, just with a new body and new perspective.

And things from there are normal, until, one day, a female, known as Dark Queen Lilith arrives in the British Isles and reforms the Kingdom of Scotland into now controlling them, becoming Darkest Queen Lilith.

Legion realizes she was the reincarnation of Lilith, The Shadow Queen, herself.

Then, a man, known as Dark King Mordecai arrives in the United States of America and unites them under himself within a single afternoon, then begins apparently looking for something, and news eventually arrives that he is specifically looking for Legion, The Demon Lord, and Isolde, The Vampire Queen, themselves, and thus, they let him into the USSB Realm, and are shocked to discover that he is the reincarnation of The Shadow King himself.

Legion then wisely enough quickly bows, meanwhile Isolde foolishly tries to attack, but even Legion is shocked by how Dark King Mordecai is able to easily outspeed Isolde, and defeat her in an instant, leading to her bowing to him.

Then he becomes Darkest King Mordecai, before then turning and looking at Donderdraak, and beginning to speak.

"You are needed in Europe." Darkest King Mordecai says to Donderdraak, which is good enough reasoning for him, who then does so, flying off at the speed of the return stroke of lightning.

Legion is shocked to see Mordecai again, especially after all of these years, and upon hearing of what happened to him, is quite interested overall.

Chapter 73: The God Emperor’s Trial

Then, the Dark Ones, now the Darkest Ones, return to the Shadow Planet, now accompanied by Donderdraak, The Dark Leviathan.

Upon their return, they are greeted with awe and respect from the Shadow Legions, as their newfound power and might have made them even more formidable than before. Donderdraak, The Dark Leviathan, in particular, is revered for its strength and loyalty to Darkest Lord Lothar.

Legion then recognized Darkest Lord Lothar as having once been The Last Champion, Lothar, and briefly freezes up.

Meanwhile The Lich King, Geralt, The Demon Lord, Legion, and The Vampire Queen, Isolde, follow them as well.

Legion notices Geralt, identifying that, yep, this was where he was all along, as the shadow advisor of Alpha.

As they approach Darkest Emperor Alpha, he can sense the power that has been awakened within the new Dark Leviathan Donderdraak, as well as the presence of the newly ascended Darkest Lord Lothar. He greets them with a respectful nod, acknowledging their victories and the newfound strength they bring to the Shadow Empire.

And then, they are all shocked when Darkest Emperor Alpha absorbs the dark magical power of the Shadow Planet itself with his tendrils of darkness and becomes seemingly infinitely more powerful before their very eyes.

"Welcome back, Darkest Lord Lothar," Darkest Emperor Alpha says, his voice echoing throughout the universe. "Your deeds have not gone unnoticed, and your newfound power is most impressive. The Shadow Empire is forever in your debt."

The Darkest Ones, Darkest Lord Lothar, Darkest King Mordecai, and Darkest Queen Lilith, all bow before Darkest Emperor Alpha.

Dark Leviathan Donderdraak also bows along with them, seemingly sensing the truly infinite power of the being before it.

Legion then hastily does so as well, slightly fearing for his very existence.

"We thank you, Supreme One," Darkest Lord Lothar says. "Your power and wisdom are beyond measure. It is an honor to serve at your side."

"Of course, but now, it is time to truly finish our unfinished business." Darkest Emperor Alpha says before then summoning both God Emperor Marcus Aurelius and God Emperor Aldric, ready to get some well needed payback on the two God Emperors.

The God Emperors appear before them, with God Emperor Marcus Aurelius looking rather confident and arrogant as always, while God Emperor Aldric looks somewhat wary and cautious. "Ah, you must be Darkest Emperor Alpha. I have heard much about you and your legions." God Emperor Marcus says with a sneer. "I suppose you wish to challenge me for dominion over this universe?"

"I have already long since surpassed you, this, is simply to get revenge on you for all of the people you have hurt and even killed in your lust for more and more power." Darkest Emperor Alpha says quite casually.

"Ah, yes, your legions. They have grown impressive, I must admit." God Emperor Marcus says, motioning to Darkest Lord Lothar and the others. "But I fear you underestimate the power that I have accrued since our last encounter."

Legion then subtly chuckles to himself, heh, Legions.

"Oh, but you see, I have also accrued that said power, your precious Sacred Relic was fused with the Darkstone to create my own Dark Relic, which was absorbed into myself." Darkest Emperor Alpha reveals, to the shock of both of the God Emperors.

"What?!" God Emperor Marcus and God Emperor Aldric cry out in unison.

Legion himself is also shocked, though does still believe that Zaria likely at least have the potential to surpass Alpha, but even Darkest Lord Lothar probably surpasses her current level, he probably was stronger before his obvious Awakening to become that "Darkest" state.

Then, suddenly, in an instant, Darkest King Mordecai suddenly stole power from God Emperor Aldric, which formed into a Sacred Relic piece, which Darkest King Mordecai then absorbed into himself, restoring his necromantic abilities that were stripped from him by Emperor Thorek I of Frosthaven back in the Year 1000 on Earth.

"What?" God Emperor Aldric cries out, shocked and furious. "You traitor! You were supposed to be our ally!"

"You gave up that chance when you turned me away after my power was stripped from me, and thus, I became for a time your greatest enemy! You God Emperors seem to think that you are above consequences, even before your ascension." Darkest King Mordecai screams at the two God Emperors.

"This is treachery!" God Emperor Aldric cries out.

Darkest Emperor Alpha then speaks up, "Everything you both have ever done is treachery, from claiming to be truly good..."

Darkest Queen Lilith then continues what Darkest Emperor Alpha is saying, while taking some power from God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, "To betraying and forcefully controlling both your own allies and even those you had nothing to do with..."

Darkest Lord Lothar then continues it as well, while taking some power from God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, "To not even trying to help or even warn your own allies when they were at threat..." Darkest Lord Lothar says, venom in his voice especially pointed towards the man who was his own son in his previous life, after having not only been left to die, but also after having learned of the many awful things his former son had done over the millennia.

Darkest Emperor Alpha then finishes it, also then shattering all of God Emperor Aldric's bones with a single strike from one of his tendrils of darkness while draining all of his power and even his life force until his corpse is crushed and disintegrates under the grip of a tendril of darkness before being absorbed into Darkest Emperor Alpha himself, "To stomping on all those beneath you or that you didn't like as if their lives didn't even matter." Darkest Emperor Alpha says before stomping God Emperor Aldric's skull into dust as an example.

"You dare speak of me like that, you abomination!" God Emperor Marcus screams, enraged. "You dare threaten the very fabric of reality itself with your insolence!"

Darkest Emperor Alpha then chuckles before then saying the most earthshattering thing ever that absolutely shatters God Emperor Marcus's perception of reality, "Said the pot, to the kettle."

"What?" God Emperor Marcus Aurelius asks, confused.

Darkest Emperor Alpha then continues with the perception of reality shattering, "Question, how many people have you killed? In the name of "The Light", of "Good"? Billions? Trillions? Nonillions? Decillions even? Or too many to even count? Or maybe you lost track? Or, maybe you never cared? Maybe you thought that their lives didn't matted enough for you to care about it?" Darkest Emperor Alpha asks, again shattering God Emperor Marcus Aurelius' perception of reality even more so.
"I..." God Emperor Marcus Aurelius whispers, unsure what to say.

Darkest Emperor Alpha then decides to ask God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, the last God Emperor left, one, final, perception of reality shattering, thing, "Give me one good reason I shouldn't kill you right now, permanently. Give me one good reason why I shouldn't execute you for all of the awful things you have done and all of the people you have stepped on and massacred in the name of "The Light" or "Good". Give me one good reason why I shouldn't end your reign now and forever. You can't think of one, can you? Good..." Darkest Emperor Alpha says.

God Emperor Marcus Aurelius can't think of a response, and thus, decides to rely on instinct, and try to fight for survival, and thus, he powers up to his Maximum Potential as a, no, THE God Emperor, and tries to fight Darkest Emperor Alpha, but he is just better than him in all ways, both in combat, and morally.

As he powers up, a blinding light surrounds the God Emperor, and the power within him grows to such an extent that it starts to warp the very fabric of reality around him, and as he unleashes a devastating attack on Darkest Emperor Alpha, he shouts, "YOU WILL NOT HAVE PEACE UNTIL YOU KNOW THE FULL EXTENT OF MY POWER!"

Darkest Lord Lothar takes note of his former son's power, it is immensely impressive, but not even quite enough to match him, let alone Darkest Emperor Alpha.

Legion can sense this, and recognizes that Shadow Goddess Zaria is probably most comparable to God Emperor Marcus Aurelius himself.

Darkest Emperor Alpha is utterly unaffected by it, "As I said earlier, I completely and utterly surpassed you long ago, and that was only a hint stronger than you were in your prime, and you needed to reset time to defeat me at my weakest while you were in your prime." Darkest Emperor Alpha says quite simply.

God Emperor Marcus Aurelius is shaken to his core by this revelation, as he realizes that Darkest Emperor Alpha is not just stronger, but vastly superior in every way. The God Emperor's power surges through him, and he unleashes his most powerful attack yet, hoping to end the confrontation and prove his worth.

Lothar notes how only now, with that final attack with all of his power, than his former son managed to only match him in power, and, of course, it is still nothing in the slightest compared to Darkest Emperor Alpha.

Legion realizes even Shadow Goddess Zaria would have to reach her basic Full Potential State to be stronger than God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, even then, she could grow beyond that.

Darkest Emperor Alpha simply catches the attack, letting the power slowly fizzle away, leaving God Emperor Marcus Aurelius completely and utterly broken, like he had made so many feel before.

Darkest Emperor Alpha then proceeds to hold God Emperor Marcus Aurelius up in the air, his limbs held stretched out so far by his tendrils of darkness that they are only a bit away from tearing off, and begins to brutally beat him down, releasing bits of dark energy into him with every punch and kick and tendril strike and stab, with those bits of dark energy building up inside of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius and slowly destroying him from within, bit by bit, ever so agonizingly.

"You... You think you can... judge me?" God Emperor Marcus Aurelius gasps, his voice weak and strained as he looks up at Darkest Emperor Alpha through bleary eyes. "You... You think you... are any... better than... me?"

Darkest Emperor Alpha then casually yet perfectly retorts, "And you think you are than me?" implying that God Emperor Marcus Aurelius is the fool for even thinking that he is morally comparable to Darkest Emperor Alpha, leaving him speechless.

As God Emperor Marcus Aurelius's strength continues to wane, Darkest Emperor Alpha effortlessly pummels him, his tendrils of darkness piercing through God Emperor Marcus Aurelius's body, draining him of his essence. Blood and darkness gush forth from his broken and bleeding form, staining the once-pristine Shadow Planet.

As his body loses power the tendrils of darkness holding his limbs just outside of tearing off are now within slowly tearing off range, as, now, God Emperor Marcus Aurelius' end is now occurring.

As finally, God Emperor Marcus Aurelius' limbs tear off and are absorbed by Darkest Emperor Alpha and his body now begins to crumble, Darkest Emperor Alpha then personally rips off the still living head of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius, and then, directly absorbs all of his remaining power and life force before then crushing his head in his hand, then giving Darkest Lord Lothar, Darkest King Mordecai, and Darkest Queen Lilith their fair share of God Emperor Marcus Aurelius' power, then Darkest King Mordecai resurrects and corrupts God Emperor Aldric, forcing him to thusly now serve them as a butler as there is nothing else he really can do now in his position.

Darkest Lord Lothar, his power now even greater than before, by a good bit, approaches Dark Butler God Emperor Aldric, and begins to casually talk to him, "You know, in my previous life I was your ancestor even further back than Marcus Aurelius, having been his father." Lothar lets him know before then walking off, leaving Dark Butler God Emperor Aldric just extremely shocked and at a loss for words.

Legion, The Demon Lord is just shocked at everything he has learned and witnessed, same with Isolde, The Vampire Queen and Dark Leviathan Donderdraak.

Chapter 74: True Goddess Winter

Then apparently Darkest King Mordecai then went down to earth and did some stuff with some apparent old summoned elemental beings to create what he calls, the "Elemental Dark Lord", then calls Isolde, Legion, and Geralt in to aid to claim some "Elemental Avatars" to fuse into the Elemental Dark Lord to complete it, and they accompany him in, and they find the location, under a volcano.

Then suddenly, a strange, concentrated blizzard appears within the structure, pushing out the strongest of them, leaving only the basic forces, whom are then decimated faster than the eye can follow, leading Geralt The Lich King, Legion The Demon Lord, and Isolde The Vampire Queen to go in there to investigate.

When they arrive, they find themselves face to face with Lady Winter, the former leader of the Armies of New Hampshire before that state became a part of the Empire of Frostreach, which she thusly apparently joined, and even was part of the God Emperor Ritual of God Emperor Aldric.

Lady Winter's eyes flashed with a fiery determination as she assessed the intruders. "You shall not pass," she declared, her voice echoing through the icy cavern. "This chamber holds the essence of the Avatars. Their power is not meant to be wielded by the likes of you."

Isolde The Vampire Queen, the only one capable of keeping up with her impressive speed, suddenly rushes at Lady Winter, supported by the undead army of Geralt The Lich King, and the fire and lightning of Legion The Demon Lord, as then Lady Winter quickly readies her icy blade.

Geralt The Lich King, unphased by the cold, summons forth a wave of undead soldiers, led by Isolde, while Legion The Demon Lord unleashes a torrent of fire and lightning against the frozen walls, trying to weaken them. Lady Winter, seeing this, quickly creates a shield of ice around herself, protecting her from the elemental assault.

Legion notes Lady Winter and wonders how on EARTH she got this powerful.

"You'll not break me so easily, fiends!" she cries out, her voice echoing through the chamber. "I have faced greater threats than you!" With a swift motion, she sends a gust of icy wind towards Isolde and her undead army, pushing them back momentarily.

She then, powers up, going from just Lady Winter, to Queen Winter, to Empress Winter, to even Goddess Winter, her icy powers and physical attributes increasing, in order to match her trio of opponents better.

Geralt The Lich King, still unphased by the cold, commands his undead army to attack once more, while Legion The Demon Lord summons forth a demonic wyvern, which breathes fire at Lady Winter, trying to weaken her defenses.

Legion doesn't question this new dragon summoning ability, probably came from being in the Sacred Relic way back when.

Even as Goddess Winter, Lady Winter still finds herself overwhelmed, she then, takes, a deep breath...

An intense blizzard begins to swirl throughout the chamber, pushing back her trio of opponents, as once of her signature wind shields forms around her, and then, the blizzard begins to concentrate around her and merge into her wind shield, which shrinks more and more around her, until eventually both merge into her, and she opens her eyes which are now glowing blue and below-zero levels of cold, and her voice is now echoey and just as cold as her eyes are, she has now ascended into her ultimate trump card, True Goddess Winter.

"I have seen the end of time, and time has seen me," she declares, her voice booming through the chamber. "Your petty struggles mean nothing against the inevitable. Surrender now, and I shall grant you a swift death. Fight, and you shall face oblivion."

True Goddess Winter then moves faster than her trio of opponents can possibly react and slices Isolde The Vampire Queen in half at the waist.

Geralt The Lich King then immediately communicates to his master, The Darkest King Mordecai, of their opponents sudden seemingly infinite increase in power and of Isolde's injury.

Legion and Geralt then begin to stand against and face Lady Winter together to stall until backup can arrive.

Legion goes truly all out, going into his newly Controlled Berserker State and creating a blade of Pure Golden Light Hellfire to attempt to match True Goddess Winter, meanwhile Geralt, The Lich King, conjures a Blade of the Undead that tracks True Goddess Winter's lifeforce to swing for him to at least counter her strikes for defense, to attempt to counter her infinitely greater speed.

Geralt even begins to use all of his various curses, which he doesn't use a majority of outside of emergencies, let alone ALL of them.

Legion even begins to work in subtly going into his True Form rapidly, to move so fast he just decides where he wants to be and then is there, while also being non-corporeal during so, and doing so both for dodging setting up attacks, and even for quickly moving to parry and block attacks.

And even with all of this, Legion and Geralt are only just barely keep up with True Goddess Winter, who is barely even trying, and she is not following her mind, but merely, her sword, she is a mindless force of combat at this point.

The duo's combined efforts manage to keep her at bay for a short while, their blades clashing and sparks flying. The heat of Legion's hellfire and the cold of True Goddess Winter's ice creating an explosive display of light and sound with every strike. Geralt's undead blade bites into the icy shield she's formed around herself, but it's clear that it's only a matter of time before it shatters under the relentless barrage of their attacks.

True Goddess Winter then begins trying slightly more and also conjures a second blade in her other hand, using it to quickly disarm Geralt and, after Geralt summons it back to himself, then using it to send Geralt flying backwards, and then does the same for Legion.

Darkest King Mordecai, Darkest Queen Lilith, and Darkest Lord Lothar then enter the chamber, Darkest Emperor Alpha deciding to stay behind until he's truly needed for now, leaving his three closest advisors to see what they can do here, Dark Matter Scientist Erebus/Dr. Erebus then enters the chamber after them, meanwhile the Elemental Dark Lord is waiting outside of the ruins themselves as it was instructed, awaiting the Avatars to be fused into it.

Geralt and Legion are taken aback by Lady Winter's newfound power. They look at each other, their eyes reflecting a mix of fear and determination. Despite the odds, they know they must press on. The Darkest King Mordecai steps forward, his eyes gleaming with "malicious" intent.

Darkest Lord Lothar then approaches True Goddess Winter, his own blade clutched tightly in his hand, and even he finds himself outsped by True Goddess Winter, but not enough to the point that he is completely and utterly unable to react, and thus, a duel between the two fastest and most skilled and powerful sword wielders in the universe begins.

Geralt The Lich King then uses his powers to attempt to slow down True Goddess Winter, focusing his magic on her sword, making it heavier and harder for her to move and swing, as he tries to give Darkest Lord Lothar an opening to strike.

Even then though, True Goddess Winter still holds the speed advantage, and their magic is powerful enough to cancel each other's out.

Darkest Lord Lothar finds himself on the defensive, his swordplay nothing but a blur as he desperately tries to keep her sword from connecting with his body. He knows that he cannot maintain this defense forever, and he must find a way to break through her defenses and land a killing blow.

He also knows that even he would be unable to endure too many subsequent blows from her in a row, but any amount that he could survive, he could swiftly regenerate from.

Meanwhile, on the ground nearby, Isolde The Vampire Queen's sliced in half body begins to regenerate itself back together unnoticed.

Darkest Lord Lothar sees this and takes advantage of the distraction, attempting to strike a blow on True Goddess Winter while her focus is elsewhere. His sword flashes through the air, aiming for her unprotected side, but she is too quick and agile, twisting her body out of the way just in time. The blade slices through the air mere inches from her flesh.

Legion also sees this, and begins to use lightning and Pure Golden Light Hellfire to support Darkest Lord Lothar by using it to at least weaken True Goddess Winter's defenses.

Darkest Lord Lothar then begins charging up the charged attack, leaving True Goddess Winter confused as to why he would use such a predictable attack that she could dodge so easily.

As he nears his destination, True Goddess Winter, sensing the intentions of the Darkest Lord Lothar, quickly turns her attention back to him, narrowing her eyes in concentration. The air around her begins to shimmer and crackle with frost, her powers building up for a counterattack.

Isolde, The Vampire Queen, having fully regenerated, then decided to double suicidedly rip out and eat her own heart to go Queen's Bloodfury and then jumped onto and held True Goddess Winter in place to make her unable to dodge Darkest Lord Lothar's Charged Attack.

As Darkest Lord Lothar then thrusted his Darkest Sword forwards as he unleashed his charged attack and blasted it at her.

Then Darkest Lord Lothar unleashes the charged attack, launching the ball of dark and lightning and electrical and storm energy and magic from his sword towards the restrained and off-guard True Goddess Winter.

As the ball of energy hurtles towards her, Isolde The Vampire Queen, still holding onto True Goddess Winter, closes her eyes and prepares for the impact, bracing herself against the force of the attack.

The explosion of the blast completely shatters True Goddess Winter's body and has reduced Isolde The Vampire Queen's body to mere pieces and chunks of flesh.

Lothar's attack has done its job; he watches with a mix of satisfaction and regret as the dust begins to settle. He knew that his attack would be fatal, but he couldn't help but feel a pang of guilt for killing his own mother and one of the few people who had shown him kindness in this dark world.

Legion could sense that from Lothar's mind, finding it interesting that Lothar's reincarnation was born the child of Lady Winter.

Then the decapitated head of Isolde The Vampire Queen begins talking, "Anyone wanna help bring my parts back closer together so I regenerate faster?" Isolde The Vampire Queen's head asks, then everyone else, apparently only just remembers, oh yeah, she's a vampire.

"Of course," Darkest King Mordecai responds, "Dr. Erebus, please gather the pieces of Lady Isolde and place them together as close to her body as possible." The mad scientist nods and begins to collect the scattered remains of the vampire queen.

Then once they are placed nearby each other Isolde The Vampire Queen then pieces her body parts back together and casually regenerates the insane damage each of her parts took from the blast.

"Well, that was... different," Darkest King Mordecai says with a mix of awe and amusement.

The Elemental Dark Lord then punches a hole in the top of the chamber and looks down, then Darkest King Mordecai fuses the shards of Lady Winter/True Goddess Winter into the Elemental Dark Lord before then fusing the Avatars as well as their crystal prisons into his creation as well.

Legion also observes as Isolde then looks over at Darkest Lord Lothar, who is looking melancholically up, and, Isolde, taking the chance, walks over and pats him on the back, seemingly recognizing from his expression that Lady Winter/True Goddess Winter must have been a parent of his.

Chapter 75: Father and Daughter

Then, Darkest Emperor Alpha has a God Emperor Ritual done on himself, which Legion, The Demon Lord was part of, increasing his power infinitely further, even finally returning Legion to his "Prime", and even then some, which is way less impressive by now, and Darkest Emperor Alpha became God Emperor of Darkness Alpha, Legion did also notice an oddly familiar power also partaking in the ritual, seemingly Zaria's, but he could just be confusing it for Alpha's own extremely similar power signature.

Then 10 Years passed of peace, where the USSB also was fused into the Empire of Shadowstein, along with their wealth, and there was peace and fun.

Legion also, extremely early on into that, went to the Planet of Saturn, the De-Facto Capital of the Empire of Shadowstein, and, much to his immense shock, he found Zaria there, as in, Shadow Goddess Zaria, and, from what he was sensing, Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential), and, yeah, her power was exactly as expected, probably even now second only to God Emperor of Darkness Alpha, and thusly, Legion approached her, shocked to see her there of all places.

"What are you doing here?" Legion asked, his voice echoing through the grand hall of the planet's castle.

"Some Dark guy with a sword found me in the ruins of Whispering Sands, when some unknown powerful enemy was pursuing me, and he so easily took it out, then gave me some portable space pod thing and left, and I got in it, and flew most of the way there, until, I suddenly reached my Full Potential explosively which destroyed the rest of it, and I began to fly the rest of the way here and, it was instantaneous, it was weird, so, anyways, nice to see you Legion, guess we are on the same side absolutely now, huh?" Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) explained casually.

Legion, still in a state of shock, tries to process the information. "But, why are you here, in the Empire of Shadowstein?" he asks, his mind racing with questions.

"Well, the dark Sword guy told me to come here, I didn't realize what it was, he just told me it was where I clearly belonged, and, who am I to disagree with someone who just saved me, just like you, Legion." Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) responded.

Legion nodded, understanding the gravity of the situation. "I suppose we do have a common cause now," he said, his eyes still wide with astonishment.

"Yeah, also, back like, 15 years ago, I noticed an odd thing about your perception of me. Do you see me like a daughter?" Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) asked.

Legion, still processing the recent events, took a moment before responding. "I... I suppose I have, over the years, grown to see you as a sort of... surrogate daughter, yes." He admitted, his expression softening slightly.

"Well then, guess I should just come out and call you dad, because, well, kinda to an extent ever since you saved me, but especially ever since the first time we saw each other since then, when I was 20, with our spar, I have seen you as a sort of, dad, to me." Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) responds honestly and warmly and happily.

"What threats? There are like two beings in all of Reality that are even threats from what I have heard during that ritual thing, and both of them are planned targets to be defeated and absorbed by Alpha apparently, and, that was always the plan, to get enough power to reset Reality to make that New Reality without war, segregation, suffering, or pain, so, there's nothing to worry about, dad." Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) casually responds, sounding positively giddy while saying that very last word.

Legion's smile falters slightly, his mind racing with the implications of what she's saying. He knew that Alpha had grand plans, but the thought of the entirety of existence being reshaped by his hand was a sobering one.

"What's wrong, Alpha is doing so to bring about that true peace, to create a Reality where all is good, dad?" Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) asks, still giddy while saying the word "dad" a word she hadn't gotten to say since she was 10, 185 years ago.

"Well, you yourself said it, me and Alpha are both Pure Good, and Pure of Heart, so to assume otherwise is to argue with yourself, dad." Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) responds, still giddy while saying the word "dad".

"And even the most evil of hearts can be turned to good, as shown by you dad, don't underestimate Alpha, he told me he has been planning this since his previous life, and has done stuff he definitely doesn't overall agree with but had to, all to achieve this goal of true peace and prosperity, just, trust in him, he knows what he's doing, and, well, even if he does go bad, there's nothing we could do, he holds the keys to our fate, and, well, we just remain confident he chooses the right door of fate to open, dad." Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) responds.

Legion nods, understanding her point of view. "You're right, we must trust him," he says, though his voice still holds a hint of doubt.

"That's it dad! Just trust in him! Also, it's so nice to get to say the word dad again!" Shadow Goddess Zaria (Full Potential) says giddily, even Awakening and becoming Dark Goddess Zaria, increasing her power yet infinitely, through pure joy and happiness, beginning to tap into her True Potential of her Shadow Goddess state, let alone that of her Dark Goddess state, let alone what is beyond that.

"Dad, calm down, I dealt with my problems with control literally a century ago, also, this barely matters, there's no way in *hell* that I'm catching up with Alpha." Dark Goddess Zaria responds, chuckling at her own hell pun relating to her new adoptive dad being The Demon Lord.

Legion explains.

Isolde nods slowly, stroking her child's hair as she thinks. "And what if someone tries to introduce these concepts again?" she asks, her eyes narrowing slightly.

Legion responds, and also on top of that advises Isolde.

Legion responds.
