Chapter 80: Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light

(385 YEARS LATER)

The Year is 666 AE, and from the depths of some other Reality, a being of true evil Light, Brilliant Erebus, Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light, the ultimate embodiment of the plague on Eternity (That which all of the Infinite Realities are within) that is "The Light", and the final and truest Ultimate Threat to the New Reality, and to all of Eternity itself.

He is a being that most would call holy, and he truly seems that way, and he possesses no darkness, for he is pure light, pure destruction, pure devastation, pure malice, pure evil.

True Light Lucifer Legion feels a disturbance in the force of light, a disturbance that is unlike anything he has ever felt before, and he knows that he must find the source of it, and destroy it, for it can only mean the end of everything that he holds dear.

Brilliant Erebus then appears within the New Reality, and suddenly everything, the entire Reality, bursts into Pure Golden Flames of Light, just from Brilliant Erebus's appearance, and his desire for pure devastation and destruction to bring about the ultimate victory of the Light and it consuming all of Eternity into itself.

The people of the New Reality, who had known peace for so long, now scream in terror as the buildings around them begin to crumble and the ground shakes beneath their feet. The animals flee in fear, their instincts screaming at them to escape the oncoming apocalypse. The very air seems to burn with the intensity of his power.

"YES! RUN! RUN LIKE SHEEP! RUN AND KNOW IT WILL MAKE NO DIFFERENCE! YOU WILL ALL BE TORTURED AND SLAUGHTERED ANYWAYS! FOR DARING TO EVEN TRY TO STAND AGAINST THE LIGHT, AGAINST ME!" Brilliant Erebus shrieks out, cackling at the same time.

The New Reality burns, the people, the animals, the buildings, everything is either already ash or will be. Omega Zaria and True Light Lucifer Legion are the only ones capable of facing this new threat, but even they are overwhelmed by the sheer power of Brilliant Erebus.

Omega Alpha then appears, and prepares to face off against Brilliant Erebus, Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light, being, himself, essentially the ultimate embodiment of The Dark and all it represents, pure peace, pure prosperity, pure freedom, pure good.

The two beings of immense power stand face to face, the very air around them crackling with the tension of their opposing forces. Omega Alpha's eyes, once filled with the warmth of a star, now burn with the cold, endless void of space. His very presence seems to cast a shadow over the blinding light that surrounds Brilliant Erebus.

Brilliant Erebus then in an instant appears in front of Omega Alpha, instantly attacking, and Omega Alpha instantly responds and blocks, both beyond all concepts, even speed itself.

The battle is one that defies description, their movements so fast that the fabric of reality is torn, creating a ripple effect that spreads across the New Reality. The sky turns a tumultuous mix of shadow and light, as if the very fabric of existence is being woven and then ripped apart by their clash.

They are overall even, yet, by the most minuscule, arguably non-existent, margin, Brilliant Erebus holds the upper hand.

The two beings dance in a deadly ballet, their blows and strikes leaving trails of light and shadow that scar the very fabric of the New Reality. The ground beneath them cracks and splits, the very air around them shimmering with the intensity of their power. Each clash is met with a sound that can only be described as a thunderclap that resonates through every being, shaking them to their core.

Omega Alpha, fueled by his determination to protect his reality, uses his shadowy tendrils to ensnare Brilliant Erebus, attempting to drain the pure light from his being. However, the Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light is not so easily contained, his power pulsing and pushing back against the shackles of darkness. The light from his form grows brighter, hotter, threatening to incinerate everything that touches it.

The air is filled with the scent of ozone as the two beings clash, the sky above them a tumultuous maelstrom of gold and black. The very fabric of the New Reality quivers with the strain of their power. The survivors of the Doomed Realities watch from afar, their hearts heavy with fear and hope. They know that the fate of all they have worked to rebuild rests on the outcome of this battle.

Brilliant Erebus, his form now a living sun, raises his sword of purest light high. "You may have conquered your darkness," he sneers, "but you shall never conquer the light!" With a swing of his blade, he sends a wave of blinding energy towards Omega Alpha.

Omega Alpha, unfazed by the display, pulls back his own shadowy blade, a weapon of pure anti-light. "Your light is a prison," he counters, "a lie to deceive and control. True darkness is freedom, and I shall show you the folly of your ways." As the waves of light and shadow collide, a deafening roar echoes across the landscape, sending shockwaves that threaten to rip the very fabric of the New Reality apart.

"And so be it." Brilliant Erebus casually responds.

The two titans of light and darkness clash in a display that could outshine the birth and death of stars. The very essence of their beings intertwines in a dance of cosmic proportions, each strike and parry threatening to tear apart the fabric of the New Reality. The ground cracks and splits, the sky roars with unseen forces, and the survivors watch in silent awe, knowing that the fate of their world is in the balance.

Omega Alpha and Brilliant Erebus then proceed to clash their blades infinitely, a challenge that will determine the winner by the power and durability of their weapon, their power.

Their swords of shadow and light meet in a cacophony of clanging steel and sizzling energy, sparks flying in every direction as the two beings push against each other with all their might. The ground beneath them turns into a sea of molten rock, the very air around them superheating into a plasma storm. The light from Brilliant Erebus' sword pierces the shadowy blade of Omega Alpha, while the darkness of Omega Alpha's weapon seems to eat away at the very essence of the light blade.

The two combatants grunt with effort, their eyes never leaving the other's. Each blow is met with a counter, each push met with an equally powerful pull. The sky above them turns into a swirling vortex of black and gold, the very fabric of reality distorting and stretching around them like a canvas being torn apart by two opposing forces.

The two then fire beams of their respective powers at each other, clashing their beams, in an attempt for one to overwhelm the other.

The New Reality becomes the battleground for a cosmic clash of wills. Omega Alpha unleashes a beam of pure shadow, a stream of absolute darkness that seeks to swallow the light whole. Brilliant Erebus counterattacks with a beam of blinding, white-hot light, the purest essence of the Wicked Light, aiming to purge the shadow from existence. The beams collide with the fury of a supernova, the impact sending a shockwave that knocks the remaining inhabitants of the city to their knees.

Then the beams are absorbed into the users blades, as they then charge towards each other, for a clash that, in one attack from each, will decide the victor of the battle, in of which combatant's blade, made of their own power, doesn't shatter, will win the battle thusly.

The anticipation is palpable as they charge, the air around them distorting with the sheer pressure of their approaching forms. Their swords, now imbued with the power of their clashed beams, blaze with a fury that could swallow worlds. The survivors hold their breath, their eyes fixed on the two figures that dominate the horizon.

The moment of impact is deafening. A blast of pure energy explodes outwards, a shockwave that flattens the city in an instant. The ground shakes as if the very core of the planet is trembling in fear. The clash of shadow and light is so intense that for a brief moment, the entire New Reality is bathed in an eerie, pulsing glow that alternates between the deepest black and the most brilliant gold.

The swords meet with a sound that seems to shatter the very concept of sound itself, a cacophony that echoes through the minds of every being present. And then, as quickly as it began, the light dims. The shadow blade, once a weapon of impenetrable darkness, cracks along its length, the pure light of Brilliant Erebus's weapon having found its weakness. The shadows retreat, withdrawing into Omega Alpha's form as he stumbles back, his eyes wide with shock and disbelief.

Brilliant Erebus then stabs Omega Alpha with his blade and throws him aside with it, leaving him badly injured.

Chapter 81: Savior of Eternity

Zaria, Shadow of Alpha then rushes in, away from her father, True Light Lucifer Legion, despite even her knowing that she is even weaker than Omega Alpha, but she at least has to try.

Her eyes glow with a fierce determination that belies her youth. She charges at Brilliant Erebus, her shadowy form moving with the grace of a panther, her blade a dark whisper of vengeance. The Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light turns to face her, a smirk playing on his lips. He sees her as a mere child playing with forces beyond her understanding.

Brilliant Erebus then stabs her, impaling her upon his blade of Pure Golden Hellfire Light, and just stares at her, watching as she swiftly begins to grow weaker, him slowly funneling Pure Golden Hellfire Light into her, to destroy her from within.

Omega Alpha, using every ounce of his power, forces himself to stand, and with a roar that could shake the heavens themselves, he charges at Brilliant Erebus, his sword of anti-light now a mere shadow of its former self, but his will undeterred.

Brilliant Erebus knocks him right back down with a casual backhand, as, Zaria, Shadow of Alpha, growing weaker, slowly reaches her hands down and around Brilliant Erebus's blade of Pure Golden Hellfire Light, seemingly as a show of defiance.

"I... won't... let... you... win... I.. won't... let... The... Light... win... f-for... my... parents..." Zaria, Shadow of Alpha says...

"Well, sounds like your parents are dead, aren't they? And how are you sure they want you to keep fighting, and not to just let me win?" Brilliant Erebus retorts.

"... You're right..." Zaria, Shadow of Alpha responds, much to Brilliant Erebus's immense confusion.

"I don't know what they would want me to do..." Zaria, Shadow of Alpha continues.

"...But I do know one thing... one thing they would want me to do... A-and thus... I will..." Zaria, Shadow of Alpha continues further.

"And what would that be, little brat?" Brilliant Erebus asked, maliciously chuckles to himself.

"... To do this... and to live... for myself!" Zaria, Shadow of Alpha screams out as then, suddenly, a massive sparking flash of darkness and shadow, with some Good Light mixed in there, as she then shatters and crushes Brilliant Erebus's blade of Pure Golden Hellfire Light with her hands, and regenerates the stab wound.

As, finally, under a shower of Dark and Good Light, and of her own self confidence and determination, Zaria TRULY Awakens, and reaches the True Potential of her Original Reality self, like with her New Reality Counterpart within her, and becomes...

TRUE ZARIA, SAVIOR OF ETERNITY

True Zaria then not only uses Dark and Shadow Magic that is in truth True Omnipotence, but also Good Light Magic, and being able to control the Light, including that which makes up Brilliant Erebus's very being.

True Zaria is truly the new Embodiment of The Dark, yet has the power to control The Light if needed.

The transformation is sudden and complete. The air around Zaria crackles with energy as she stands tall, her eyes now a swirling mix of shadow and starlight. The remnants of Brilliant Erebus's sword fall to the ground, shattered beyond repair. The Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light stares at her in disbelief, the smugness wiped from his face.

True Zaria then, in an instant appears in front of Brilliant Erebus, having already punched him in the gut, before then kicking up between his legs, sending him up and into the air before then flying up and chasing after him, and beginning to land a barrage of infinite kicks capable of destroying an entire Reality each on him.

Each kick she lands on Brilliant Erebus sends a shockwave through the air that could be felt across the entire New Reality, the power of her strikes resonating through every atom. The Supreme Satan of The Wicked Light is sent hurtling back, his form crackling and distorting under the onslaught of True Zaria's fury.

True Zaria then reaches out and grabs him by the face and instantly absorbs him into herself, killing him instantly.

The sudden absence of Brilliant Erebus's power is palpable, the light that had consumed the New Reality fading away as quickly as it had appeared. The survivors watch in awe as the young girl stands, now the undisputed ruler of the shadow and the light.

True Zaria then snaps her fingers, instantly repairing all of the damage that Brilliant Erebus had caused across all of Eternity and within the New Reality, before then flying back down next to her adoptive father, Legion, The True Light Lucifer.

"Are you okay, Zaria?" Legion asks, his eyes wide with shock and pride at the sheer power she had just displayed.

"Of course, dad, also, I just brought about true peace, considering that guy was the absolute embodiment of the old Light, the pure evil one, and I just took him out, that is now also gone, and now, only your light, the Good Light, exists." True Zaria, Savior of Eternity casually responds to her adoptive father before then hugging him.

The light that had once been a prison is now a gentle embrace, a warmth that fills the hearts of all who see it. The survivors of the Doomed Realities begin to gather, their eyes filled with hope and wonder. They had witnessed the birth of a new guardian, one who wields the power of darkness and light in perfect harmony.

(2,147,483,646,615 YEARS LATER)

And thusly, in 2,147,483,647,281 AE (Alpha Era), at the end of AE, a ceremony is held in the New Reality, attended by all, including Omega Alpha, Lord of Champions Lothar, Lady Isolde, The Vampire Empress, True Light Lucifer Legion, The God of Good Light, True Elemental Dragon God Donderdraak, Geralt, The True God of Magic, "God Emperor" Marcus Aurelius, Great Lady Winter, God Butler of Darkness Aldric, and all others, for the announcement of the next year counting term for the next era, ZE (Zaria Era), and it being made truly official.

The New Reality is ablaze with celebration, the buildings adorned with banners and lights that shimmer with the essence of both shadow and light. The streets are filled with cheers and laughter as the inhabitants revel in the newfound peace that has been brought upon them by True Zaria. The air is filled with the sweet scent of victory and hope, a stark contrast to the stench of war and destruction that had once plagued their lands.

Chapter 82: Goddess of Eternity

(INFINITE ETERNITIES LATER)

The Year is ??? ZEE (Zaria Eternal Epoch), and Zaria, Goddess of Eternity, exists out within Eternity, watching over all of the Infinite Realties, yet able to easily pick up and crush them with one hand if she so desired.

But she instead watches over all of them, and subtly helps them along, as a truly omnipotent deity, one of The Dark, and of The Good Light, as she is also writing a book, in the honor of her adoptive father, Legion, True God of The Good Light, and of his entire life, and how he impacted her.

A tale...

The Massive Demon Lord's Tale

THE END
